# REVISOP — MASTER BLUEPRINT

**Prepared:** December 2025  
**Last Updated:** 16/09/2026  
**Stack:** React 19 + Vite 7 · TailwindCSS + shadcn/ui · Supabase (PostgreSQL + Auth + RLS + Storage + Edge Functions) · Vercel  
**Live URL:** https://www.revisop.com (redirects from https://www.recallapp.co.in)  
**Repository:** https://github.com/ai1976/recall-app

**Sprint History:**
- **Sprint 0 (Dec 2025):** MVP — auth, flashcards, basic SRS (SuperMemo-2), notes upload, admin dashboard
- **Sprint 1 (Jan 2026):** Content visibility (private/friends/public), friendship system, upvotes, flashcard decks, 4-tier roles
- **Sprint 2 (Feb 2026):** Study groups, notifications (Realtime), achievement badges, push notifications, content flags
- **Sprint 3 (Mar 2026):** Study sessions, leaderboard (friends + following), daily goals, follow graph, batch groups, professor multi-course, card suspension/skip
- **Sprint 4 (Apr 2026):** Author profiles, content flagging UI, professor analytics, following page, admin analytics
- **Sprint 5 (May 2026):** Push notification infrastructure (Edge Functions, VAPID, cron)
- **Sprint 6 (Jun 2026):** 10 question types documented (MCQ, T/F, concept_card, etc.), Gemini import source
- **Sprint R7 (Jun 2026):** Full rebrand Recall → RevisOp — brand colors (#f59e0b amber + #1e1b4b navy), wordmark, PWA icons, domain migration, SMTP moved to hello@revisop.com
- **Phase 5 — Sprint 1 (Jun 2026):** Design-system foundation (presentational only). Additive brand HSL tokens (`brand.navy/amber/success` + `surface.*`) in `index.css` + `tailwind.config.js`; new `StudyItemCard` (deck/study-set card) and controlled `FlipCard` components; 3D flip utilities (reduced-motion aware); dev-only `/__design` QA route. **No DB changes; no existing shadcn token value changed.**
- **Phase 5 — Sprint 2 (Jul 2026):** ✅ *Deployed 2026-07-01* (`docs/database/phase5/03–08`). Adds `is_featured_on_landing` flag to `flashcard_decks`+`notes` (default false, `CHECK (featured => visibility='public')`), a BEFORE UPDATE auto-clear trigger that revokes the flag when content leaves public, partial indexes, and `get_featured_landing_content()` — a SECURITY DEFINER anon RPC returning curated public decks/notes with a **5-card hard cap** (via the 5-grouping-column join, never `deck_id`) and metadata-only notes. Also caps `get_public_deck_preview` teaser at 5 (was 10). **No frontend yet — hard prerequisite for S3/S4.**
- **Phase 5 — Sprint 3 (Jul 2026):** ✅ *Deployed 2026-07-01* (`docs/database/phase5/09–12`). Two-step curation gate layered on S2's flag: professors/admins nominate their own already-public decks/notes; admins/super_admins approve, reject, or unfeature. Adds 4 nullable audit columns (`featured_nominated_by/_at`, `featured_approved_by/_at`, FK → `profiles.id`) to `flashcard_decks`+`notes`; `CREATE OR REPLACE`s the existing S2 auto-clear trigger function (`fn_autoclear_featured_on_visibility_change`) to also null those 4 columns on unpublish — no new trigger. 6 new SECURITY DEFINER RPCs: `nominate_featured_content`, `approve_featured_nomination` (returns the resulting `is_featured_on_landing` boolean), `reject_featured_nomination`, `unfeature_content` (full removal — nulls all four fields so an unfeatured item leaves both the landing and the pending queue; functionally identical to reject, kept separate for UI clarity), `get_pending_featured_nominations`, `get_live_featured_content_admin`. Frontend (SQL deployed 2026-07-01, cleared to push): new `FeatureNominationButton` component (3 states: nominate / pending / featured) wired into `MyFlashcards.jsx` (deck group header, matched via the 5-grouping-column join) and `NoteDetail.jsx` (owner/admin header actions); `AdminDashboard.jsx` gets an always-visible "Landing Page Content" section (Pending Nominations + Currently Live tables) alongside the untouched Flagged Content card. S2 objects (column/CHECK/indexes/`get_featured_landing_content`/`get_public_deck_preview`) unmodified. **09→12 deployed & verified 2026-07-01 (test 12: all PASS); frontend cleared to push.**
- **Phase 5 — Sprint 4 (Jul 2026):** Landing rebuild — "show, don't tell." **SQL:** `docs/database/phase5/13` extends `get_platform_stats()` (`CREATE OR REPLACE`, same signature) to add `public_flashcards`/`public_notes` (visibility='public' counts); existing `student_count`/`educator_count`/`total_flashcards`/`total_notes` fields preserved as-is. **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (public_flashcards=693, public_notes=107).** **Frontend:** `Home.jsx` now has **zero direct `.from()` calls** — deleted the two `profiles` role-count reads and the two public-count reads on `flashcards`/`notes` (all were RLS-filtered/unreliable for anon visitors, per the §1.4 tech-debt note this closes). New `src/components/landing/HeroFlipDemo.jsx`: anonymous live card-flip demo driven by the first `get_featured_landing_content()` deck with teaser cards (falls back to 3 hardcoded generic cards if none — hero is never blank); controlled `FlipCard` + cosmetic Hard/Medium/Easy rating buttons styled after `StudyMode.jsx` (no SRS writes, no auth); ends with a "Sign up to save your progress" soft wall → `/signup`. New "Featured Study Sets" rail renders `get_featured_landing_content()` decks+notes as `StudyItemCard`s linking to `/deck/:id`/`/note/:id` with a "Featured" badge; omitted entirely (no empty state) when nothing is curated yet. Removed the 3 fake "— Student / Early Access" testimonial cards and their section wrapper. "For Institutes & Educators" section (mailto: CTA) untouched — real `/educators` route is Sprint 5. **`npm run build` passes; verified in-browser both with live featured content (real "Business Laws" deck, already curated via S3) and with featured content stubbed empty (client-side remount, no full reload) — rail correctly omits itself and hero correctly shows the 3-card fallback.** **13 deployed & verified 2026-07-01; frontend pushed to main.**
- **Phase 5 — Sprint 5 (Jul 2026):** ✅ *Deployed 2026-07-01* (`docs/database/phase5/14–16`). Findable B2B lead path — dedicated `/educators` route with a real lead form, replacing the `mailto:` CTAs. **SQL:** `14_SCHEMA` adds `request_type` (`text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'student_access'`, `CHECK IN ('student_access','institute_inquiry','educator_application')` — third value pre-added for Sprint 6, no re-migration) and `message` (nullable text) to `access_requests`. `15_FUNCTIONS` adds `submit_institute_inquiry()` (SECURITY DEFINER, `GRANT TO anon, authenticated`) mapping the institute lead form onto the existing `access_requests` columns (`name`/`whatsapp_number`/`course`/`email` reused directly; `content_name` reused for institute name since `content_id`/`content_type` stay NULL for institute rows; `message` is the one new column, combining city + optional note — no existing slot fit without contaminating `content_type`'s meaning in the admin UI). Admin notification reuses the existing `'access_request'` `notifications.type` value (its CHECK constraint doesn't include an institute-specific value; extending it was out of scope) — distinguished via `metadata->>'request_type'`. **16_TEST: all 6 PASS** (field mapping, course default, city-only message format, both required-field validations, admin notification fan-out). **Frontend (SQL deployed 2026-07-01, cleared to push):** new `src/pages/public/Educators.jsx` (`/educators`, anonymous, zero direct `.from()` — mirrors `ContentPreviewWall.jsx`'s form pattern) with institute lead form → `submit_institute_inquiry` → "We'll reach out within 1–2 business days" success state. `Home.jsx` gets a secondary nav link ("For Institutes") and the hero/section/footer `mailto:` CTAs are rewired to `/educators` (section content untouched). `AdminDashboard.jsx`'s Access Requests table gets a Type badge (Institute/Student) + filter (All/Student Access/Institute Inquiries); institute rows show institute name + city/message in a "Details" column and skip the signup-link/Grant-Access UI (leads only, no approval action this sprint). **`npm run build` passes; verified in-browser against the live deployed backend — form submit returned 204 and rendered the correct success copy, no console errors.** Educator-application-to-role-grant flow is Sprint 6, not started.
- **Sprint 6.0 — Correctness (Sep 2026):** ✅ *SQL deployed & verified (`02_TEST` 9/9 PASS incl. every `[CRITICAL]`); frontend pushed to main 02/09/2026.* Single source of truth for the "what's due" review queue + read-time course-awareness + a cluster of logged-in-experience bug fixes. **New RPC `get_study_queue(p_user_id uuid)`** (`docs/database/sprint6/01`, `02_TEST`) — SECURITY DEFINER, `STABLE`, `SET search_path TO public, extensions` (unquoted), `REVOKE FROM public/anon` + `GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated`, L5 read-IDOR guard (`p_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM auth.uid() AND NOT public.is_admin()` → RAISE — a NULL session also RAISEs). Consumed by Dashboard / ReviewSession / StudyMode, replacing three duplicated client-side date-math implementations (no `next_review_date`/`skip_until` comparison remains in any of the three). **"Due" =** review row exists AND `status='active'` AND `next_review_date <= today` AND (`skip_until IS NULL OR skip_until <= today`), *today* computed in `profiles.timezone`; never-reviewed cards are NOT part of the queue (StudyMode-standalone concern, still handled there). **Concept cards excluded.** **Read-time course filter (non-destructive, reversible):** a row is returned only when `profiles.course_level IS NULL OR flashcards.target_course IS NULL OR target_course = course_level`. The RPC **writes nothing** — switching course back restores the previous queue with schedules intact automatically (`02_TEST` block 9). **Null-course policy:** a student with no `course_level` set → no filter (sees everything); a card with no `target_course` → treated as matching (`flashcards.target_course` is `NOT NULL` in schema, so defensive only). Visibility guard re-asserts the L2 read predicates (own / public / accepted-friend) since SECURITY DEFINER bypasses RLS. **`vw_study_items` (or any SECURITY DEFINER view) is NOT reintroduced.** **"Keep one old-course card in the pipeline" was proposed and REJECTED by the auditor — Custom Course is the accepted escape hatch; there is no `reviews.pinned` column/flag/UI.** Also shipped: C1 stat-card source reconciliation (`get_admin_platform_overview` is the canonical internal admin source; `get_platform_stats` is landing-page-only), new `src/lib/qualityTier.js` shared colour-tier util, `NavDesktop` route-prefix active state (Sprint 6.2 dep), `NotePreview`/`DeckPreview` redirect logged-in users past the anon wall, neutral "Due Today: 0". See `now.md` + `docs/tracking/bugs.md` (C1–C6; C4 was a non-issue). **Follow-up (03/09/2026, pushed):** "Today's Reviews" → `/dashboard/review-session` added to the Study nav menu (`NavDesktop` + `NavMobile`) — the queue previously had no in-app link for any role (students used the dashboard CTA, professors only the push deep link). **`03_TEST` (friends/private visibility branches) 6/6 PASS.** C3/C5/C6 verified live. **⏳ Known gap:** `Progress.jsx` "Due Items Forecast" ("Due Today" etc.) is a 4th "due" surface still reading its own `forecast` source — NOT `get_study_queue` — so it is not course-filtered (observed 24 vs the queue's 4). Point it at `get_study_queue` in a later slice; relevant to the SRS Ladder Epic. **→ RESOLVED in the SRS Ladder Epic Phase 1 (below): `get_due_forecast` body rewritten to share the exact `get_study_queue` predicate.**
- **SRS Ladder Epic (Sep 2026):** ✅ *Phase 0 approved + Phase 1 (engine + schema) + Phase 2 (migration) DEPLOYED & VERIFIED 03/09/2026 (`03_TEST` 30/30 PASS; backfill `rung IS NULL`=0, distribution `856/3065/1871/781/1251`; platform due-count `4830 → 4830` unchanged). **Phase 3 (frontend) ✅ SHIPPED — `06_FUNCTIONS` deployed, frontend pushed to main (`8323ee3`). ⏳ live-verify on recallapp.co.in.*** Replaces the flat client-side per-rating interval (`StudyMode.jsx` `handleRating`: Easy +7 / Medium +3 / Hard +1, unconditional) with a deterministic server-side **expanding ladder** — see **D-2A**. **New:** `reviews.rung smallint`, `reviews.status` value `'mastered'`, config tables `srs_ladder_curves` + `srs_ladder_rules`, RPCs `submit_review` (write SSOT), `srs_preview`, `get_srs_ladder_config`, internal `srs_interval_for_rung`. **Changed:** `get_study_queue` gains `rung` in its return shape (MASTERED excluded for free); `get_due_forecast` body rewritten to the exact `get_study_queue` due predicate (closes the 6.0 forecast gap, signature unchanged). **Migration:** one `UPDATE` backfills `reviews.rung` from `repetition` capped by `easiness` (rung 4 / rung 2); `next_review_date` untouched; reversible. SQL in `docs/database/srs-ladder/01`–`05`. Phase 0 proposal: `docs/active/design-review/srs-ladder-proposal.md`. Deferred to Sprint 6.4: grade-button reskin + prominent interval-preview UI. Deferred to Phase 7+: exam-date anchor. Dropped: course-value normalization (Phase 0 proved no drift exists).
- **Sprint 6.3 — Dashboards Reskin + Forward Ledger live (Sep 2026):** ✅ *Code complete 05/09/2026; `npm run build` clean (17.4s); `npx eslint` clean on every changed file. **3 new SECURITY DEFINER RPCs ✅ deployed & verified 05/09/2026** (`02_TEST` 12/12 real assertions PASS incl. every `[CRITICAL]`; one fixture-only FAIL — summed all 8 lanes assuming a clean test student — rewritten delta-based, not an RPC defect). Per-role live verification on `revisop.com` pending an operator-supplied session.* Migrates **`src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` (all 4 role branches — student / educator / admin / super-admin)** onto the `--rv-*` layer + Plex, ships the **Forward Ledger macro** for the first time against real scheduled data, parks the gamification, and finishes the overlay-surface + nav-dropdown colour migration deferred from 6.2. `/admin` + `/super-admin` (`AdminDashboard.jsx` / `SuperAdminDashboard.jsx`) are the management consoles and are **out of scope** — the "admin/super-admin dashboard" with "Needs Review" is the `Dashboard.jsx` branch. **SQL (`docs/database/sprint6.3/`, ✅ deployed & verified 05/09/2026):** `get_due_forecast_buckets(p_user_id)` → 8 rows `(bucket_index, bucket_label, scheduled_count)` — the student forward-scheduled load keyed to `REVISOP_BUCKETS`/`BUCKET_DAYS` in `src/lib/revisop-tokens.js` (`Today · 1d · 3d · 6d · 2w · 1mo · 3mo · 6mo+` at centre-day `[0,1,3,6,14,30,90,180]`, nearest-centre; overdue folds into lane 0), same "scheduled review row" predicate as `get_due_forecast` (active · not `concept_card` · course-filtered · visibility-guarded · `skip_until` not future); `get_educator_accuracy_by_qtype(p_professor_id, p_course_level)` → one row per `question_type` with `total_graded`/`hits`/`accuracy_pct` using the SRS ladder's own hit/miss mapping (**hit = graded Medium or Easy `quality IN (3,5)`; miss = Hard `quality = 1`; `quality = 0` skip/suspend rows excluded from the denominator; `concept_card` excluded**), `HAVING` ≥ 1 graded review; `get_educator_cohort_forecast_buckets(p_professor_id, p_course_level)` → the cohort variant of the first RPC. All three: established L5 IDOR idiom (`p_id IS DISTINCT FROM auth.uid() AND NOT public.is_admin()` → RAISE — self-or-admin for the student RPC, professor-or-admin for the two educator RPCs), `STABLE`, `SET search_path TO public, extensions` (unquoted), `REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC/anon` + `GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated`, `NOTIFY pgrst`. `02_TEST` (BEGIN/ROLLBACK, impersonation): 8-lane shape, overdue→lane-0, +30d→lane-5, +400d→lane-7, concept-card exclusion `[CRITICAL]`, hit/miss mapping + skip-excluded-from-denominator, `concept_card` never returned `[CRITICAL]`, IDOR raises for a non-owned cohort `[CRITICAL]` (×2), cohort 8-lane shape. **Frontend — dashboards (`Dashboard.jsx`):** `<PageContainer className="font-plex">`; the 8 section eyebrows restyled to the 6.1 `Label` spec; all 6 stat-tile numerals → mono tabular figures (inline `Num`); bulk colour-only palette swap to `--rv-*` (`text-muted-foreground`→`text-rv-ink-400`, `hover:border-primary`→`hover:border-rv-navy-400`, `hover:bg-accent`→`hover:bg-rv-bg-2`, `text-amber-600`→`text-rv-navy`, amber icon chips → `bg-rv-navy-50 rounded-rec`, green chips → `bg-rv-green-50 rounded-rec`, grays → `text-rv-ink-*`/`border-rv-border`, `text-primary`/`#1e1b4b`→`text-rv-navy`) — **left on their own palette (semantic, per the 6.2 precedent):** the green "Start Review" CTA, the amber status surfaces ("All caught up" / "Get Started" / "Needs Attention"), the red "Needs Review" surfaces, the "Elevated" badge, `text-red-*` affordances, `text-green-500`/`text-orange-500` lucide accents. **Student:** new "Forward load" section = `<ForwardLedgerMacro data={forecastSeries} unit="items" />` fed by `get_due_forecast_buckets` folded to `number[8]`, self-gating on non-empty; **`<AnonymousStats>` removed** (+ its import, `classStats` state, `fetchClassStats`, `hasUserActivity`, and the `activeCourse` effect's call — component + `get_anonymous_class_stats` RPC left in the repo, **no DB migration, no trigger change**); **"Mastered" tile re-pointed** from "distinct cards ever reviewed" (`new Set(actualReviews.map(r=>r.flashcard_id)).size`) to the real mastered count (`get_mastered_cards(p_user_id).length`, `reviews.status='mastered'`), sublabel "Unique items" → "Items mastered". **Educator:** new "Accuracy by question type" widget (`get_educator_accuracy_by_qtype` → per-type navy bar on a `--rv-slate-50` track + `Num` numerals + footnote) and "Cohort forward load" ledger (`get_educator_cohort_forecast_buckets` → `<ForwardLedgerMacro … unit="reviews" />`), both before the Activity Feed, both self-gating; the `activeCourse` effect refetches them on course switch. New `formatQuestionType()` helper. **Gamification PARKED, not deleted — the badge/achievement DB tables + `trg_badge_*` triggers are untouched; `grep` shows zero migration and zero trigger change.** **Settled-card rule honoured — `git diff` shows no new conditional wrapping "Needs Attention" (educator) or "Needs Review" (admin/super-admin); both still render unconditionally, the pre-existing `length > 0 ? … : …` ternaries *inside* their classNames are unchanged.** **Frontend — overlay-surface family (`src/components/ui/`):** `select.jsx` / `dropdown-menu.jsx` / `popover.jsx` / `command.jsx` / `switch.jsx` onto `--rv-*` — **no shadcn token *value* edited, only class references swapped** (same method as 6.2): surface `bg-popover`→`bg-rv-bg-1`, `text-popover-foreground`/`text-foreground`→`text-rv-ink-900`, bare `border`/`border-input`→`border-rv-border`, `bg-muted`/`bg-border`→`bg-rv-border`, `text-muted-foreground`→`text-rv-ink-400`, active/selected `bg-accent`+`text-accent-foreground`→`bg-rv-navy-50`+`text-rv-navy`, `rounded-md`/`rounded-sm`→`rounded-rec`, `shadow-md`/`-lg`→`shadow-rv`, `font-plex` added; `select` trigger mirrors the 6.2 `Input` shell; `switch` on `bg-blue-600`→`bg-rv-navy`, off `bg-gray-200`→`bg-rv-border-strong`, `ring-blue-500`→`ring-rv-navy`. **Frontend — nav right-side dropdowns (`src/components/layout/`):** `ProfileDropdown` / `FriendsDropdown` / `ActivityDropdown` / `CourseSwitcher` trigger buttons + portaled bodies finish the colour migration (grays → `rv-ink`/`rv-bg`/`rv-border`, `bg-[#1e1b4b]` avatar → `bg-rv-navy`, `ActivityDropdown` unread tint amber → navy). **Left deliberately (semantic):** role badges (`getRoleBadgeClass`), sign-out red, Friends accept-green/decline-red, `ActivityDropdown` per-type lucide icon accents, and **`CourseSwitcher.COURSE_COLORS`** (a functional per-course "distinguish at a glance" identity array — only the structural grays around it were migrated). **`/__design`** gains a Sprint 6.3 Dashboard + Surfaces block (reskinned stat tiles, live-shaped `ForwardLedgerMacro`, open-state surface reproduction + live `Select`/`DropdownMenu`/`Popover`/`Switch`). **Verified — local `/__design`** (light + dark computed styles) **+ per-role live 05/09/2026** (dev server → live Supabase, inline browser, real student / professor / super_admin sessions): student — eyebrow `Plex 11px/500/uppercase/#6b7280`, numerals `Plex Mono 24px tabular #0a0e1a`, **Forward Ledger `[2,1,0…]` == `get_study_queue().length` 2**, `AnonymousStats` gone, Mastered tile = real `get_mastered_cards` count; professor — "Accuracy by question type" (`Flashcard 87% · 2240 graded`, `#1e1b4b` bar on `#f1f5f9`) + "Cohort forward load" `[1465,1,7,0,0,0,0,560]` live, "Needs Attention" unconditional; super_admin — "Needs Review" unconditional, `Manage` dropdown + `<Select>` open on `/dashboard/settings` = `--rv-bg-1`/`--rv-border`/`4px`/Plex, highlighted option `--rv-navy-50`/`--rv-navy`. Console: only a pre-existing 4× `401` (also on the untouched `/dashboard/notes` — not a 6.3 regression). `git diff --name-only` — zero study-loop files touched. **Deviations:** `get_educator_cohort_forecast_buckets` is a 3rd RPC beyond the one the operator approved — same pattern the sprint asks for, shipped as its own function so it can be dropped at deploy time; `CourseSwitcher.COURSE_COLORS` un-migrated; per-role live verification deferred to an operator-supplied session. **Deferred to 6.4:** study loop + Literata + `AnswerOption` green removal + the 2 remaining carried items (seeded test-card cleanup, Items-Reviewed `created_at`).
- **Sprint 6.4 — Study Loop UI Reskin (Sep 2026) — Phase 6 close-out:** ✅ *Code complete 05/09/2026; `npm run build` clean (7.6s); `npx eslint` clean on every changed file. **No core-reskin SQL.** Not yet pushed; live full-session verification pending an operator student session on `revisop.com`.* Migrates the front/back review loop onto the unified frame. **No SRS behaviour change** — same `submit_review`, same `get_study_queue`, same skip/suspend semantics; presentation + interval display only. **Study-loop shape:** `src/pages/dashboard/Study/ReviewSession.jsx` is a subject-picker that delegates the entire card UI to `<StudyMode>` (via the `flashcards` prop); `src/pages/dashboard/Study/StudyMode.jsx` (one file) renders the card, Show Answer, grade row, skip/suspend/reset menu, progress bar, and both session-complete states — no separate front/back component (inline JSX gated on `showAnswer`). **One reskin covers both entry paths.** **`StudyMode.jsx`:** card frame `bg-white rounded-xl shadow-xl` → `<Card elevated>` from `src/components/revisop` at `rounded-obj` (**r14 — the study-object radius, locked, not the dashboard's r12**) / `--rv-bg-1` / `--rv-border` / `font-plex`, with a leading `<VerifiedEdge on={showAnswer && currentCard.is_verified} />` and `key={currentIndex}` + `rv-forward-in`/`rv-forward-out` classes driven by a new `transitioning` state. Grade row: the three shadcn traffic-light `<Button>`s → one **`<GradeButtonRow>`** (equal-weight navy-outline, ≥48px @ 88px, `rounded-obj`, no red/amber/green; mono interval label differentiates; slate miss `#475569`, never red; `ForwardLedgerMicro` per button) fed by a new `gradeButtons` `useMemo` = `{label, rating, iv:'Nd', bucket: bucketForDays(days)}` derived from the **live SRS-Ladder Phase-3 `gradePreview` memo** — one-time `get_srs_ladder_config()` on mount, **zero per-card network**, labels run the exact `srs_preview` / `submit_review` transition maths (single `srs_ladder_rules` row read by client + both server fns → cannot drift). `handleRating` unchanged except the advance now waits out the animation (`prefersReducedMotion() ? 100 : 240` ms). Revealed answer neutralised: `ANSWER` chip `bg-green-100 text-green-700` → `bg-rv-navy-50 text-rv-navy`, `QUESTION` chips → `bg-rv-bg-2 text-rv-ink-600`; answer body adopts `font-literata font-normal leading-relaxed text-[1.35rem]` when `isReadingBody(back_text)` (see Literata below). Progress bar → `bg-rv-bg-2` track / `bg-rv-navy` fill; Easy/Medium/Hard session counters de-coloured (`CheckCircle`/`AlertCircle`/`XCircle` green/yellow/red → `Check`/`Minus`/`X` `text-rv-ink-400`, mono counts). Session-complete (normal + preview-wall), "No flashcards" empty, loading spinner → `<Card>` / `--rv-*` / Plex, neutral result tiles with mono `Num` (Hard tile a subtle slate tint, never red). Header + progress strip `bg-white border-gray-200` → `bg-rv-bg-1 border-rv-border`; page ground `bg-amber-50` → `bg-rv-bg-0`. Show Answer → full-width `min-h-[48px]`. Skip loses `hover:text-orange-600`; `text-red-600` Suspend/Reset dropdown items kept (semantic destructive affordance). **`ReviewSession.jsx`:** subject-list `Card`s, "All Caught Up! 🎉" (green circle → `bg-rv-navy-50`/`text-rv-navy`), "Reviewing: N cards due" banner (`bg-amber-100 border-amber-300` → `bg-rv-navy-50 border-rv-navy-100`), loading + grounds → `--rv-*` + Plex; counts → mono; shadcn `Card` radius left (6.3 precedent), border → `border-rv-border`. **`AnswerOption.jsx`** (primitive; only `/__design` consumes it): `correct` state **de-greened** — `border-rv-green bg-rv-green-50` → `border-rv-navy bg-rv-navy-50`, badge `bg-rv-green` → `bg-rv-navy`, "Correct answer" label `text-rv-green` → `text-rv-navy`; correctness now reads via the check/cross glyph + label alone; `missed` (slate) unchanged. **Post-forward animation** (`src/index.css`, additive): `@keyframes rv-forward-out` (answered card `translateY(-10px) scale(.97)` + fade, 240ms `cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)`) + `rv-forward-in` (next card rises from `+8px` + fade, 180ms, 120ms delay); `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` collapses both to a 100ms linear opacity fade. **Literata activation:** `src/lib/revisop-tokens.js` — `REVISOP_LITERATA_ENABLED` flipped `false → true`; new `LITERATA_MIN_CHARS = 320` + `isReadingBody(text)` (guarded by the flag). Literata is applied **only** to a revealed flashcard answer with `back_text.trim().length >= 320` — the first such answer to mount triggers the lazy same-origin `/fonts/literata.woff2` fetch (~85.7 KB); never on session start, never on chrome. No note/case-study reading bodies exist in the front/back loop (Phase 7). **`reviews.last_reviewed_at` — no schema/function change:** the column already exists (`timestamptz`, `DEFAULT now()`) and has always been maintained (pre-ladder `handleRating` set it on every UPDATE/INSERT; `submit_review` sets it too). The "Items Reviewed (last 7 days)" bug was purely that the stat *queries* filtered `reviews.created_at` (the card's FIRST review — never moves, since `submit_review` UPDATEs the one `UNIQUE(user_id, flashcard_id)` row). **Fix (frontend only):** `Dashboard.jsx` (`fetchPersonalStats` — the 7d "Reviews" tile count + the GoalProgressWidget today-count) + `Progress.jsx` (7d/30d window "Items Reviewed") now filter recency on `last_reviewed_at ?? created_at`. **Streak + weekly-accuracy left on `created_at`** for a Phase 7 analytics pass (operator decision). `docs/database/sprint6.4/01_DIAGNOSTIC_items_reviewed_recency.sql` (read-only) + `02_DATA_backfill_last_reviewed_at.sql` (idempotent NULL backfill, expected 0 rows, not a deploy-order blocker) provided but **not required**. **`/__design`:** new "Study loop" block in `ReskinGallery` (light + dark). **Deferred to Phase 7:** new question types (MCQ multi / FITB / match / case-study) + the unified question frame beyond front/back; exam-date anchor; nav-shell rebuild; the 4× `401` console noise; streak/weekly-accuracy recency re-point; `IntervalChip` `tone="green"`. **With 6.4, Phase 6 reskin is CODE COMPLETE (6.0 → 6.4); it closes once 6.3 + 6.4 are pushed and live-verified.**

the mobile `SheetContent` portals out of the `<nav>` subtree so it carries its own `font-plex bg-rv-bg-1 text-rv-ink-900`). `NavDesktop.jsx` + `NavMobile.jsx`: inline `#f59e0b`/`#1e1b4b` wordmark → shared **`<Wordmark />`** component (now live in nav); active-item treatment **amber → navy tint** (`bg-amber-50 text-amber-700` → `bg-rv-navy-50 text-rv-navy`, matches the Pass 2 `SideRail` reference + the neutral direction that made the grade row navy-outline), inactive `text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50` → `text-rv-ink-600 hover:bg-rv-bg-2`, nav-pill radius `rounded-md` → `rounded-rec`. **Sprint 6.0's route-prefix active-state logic (`underAny`/`isStudyActive`/`isCreateActive`) is untouched — only its className output is restyled.** `NavMobile` sheet interior migrated colour-only (grays → `rv-ink`/`rv-bg`, active course-switcher tint amber → navy); role badges (`getRoleBadgeClass`) and the sign-out `text-red-600` affordance deliberately left (semantic, not chrome palette). **`Button` variant map:** `default` → `bg-rv-navy text-rv-bg-1` (navy in light, accent lavender in dark — `--rv-navy` flips, `--rv-bg-1` flips with it so the label stays contrasting); `secondary` → navy-50 tint; `outline` → `border-rv-navy-400` neutral ink; `ghost` → `text-rv-ink-600 hover:bg-rv-bg-2` (matches nav inactive); `link` → `text-rv-navy`; `destructive` → `bg-rv-danger` (`#b91c1c` light / `#ef4444` dark) `text-white`, delete-confirmations only. Base radius `rounded-md` → `rounded-rec` (all sizes); `font-plex`; focus ring `ring-rv-navy` (navy light / accent dark, one token). **`GradeButtonRow` is NOT affected** — it uses raw `<button>`, not this atom. **`Input`/`Textarea`:** `rounded-rec`, `border-rv-border` (=the old `--input` value in light, so the border colour is visually unchanged — the deltas are radius 6→4px, Plex, navy focus ring, `placeholder:text-rv-ink-400`), `disabled:bg-rv-bg-2`, `aria-[invalid=true]:border-rv-danger`. `bg-transparent` kept (lower regression risk on tinted containers). **`Label`** (shadcn, form-field — distinct from `revisop/Label.jsx` the section eyebrow): `font-plex text-rv-ink-900`. **`/__design`** gains a "Chrome" section — representative reskinned nav strip + all six `Button` variants + all `Input`/`Textarea`/`Label` states, inheriting the page's light/dark toggle + both-themes view. **`context.md`** stale "⚠️ STALE (do not use)" blue→purple gradient tombstone replaced with a positive two-tone typographic branding description (per §1041/§1209). **Deferred to 6.3:** dashboard content / cards / stat tiles / charts / badges; the dropdown-surface atom family. **Deferred to 6.4:** study-loop primitives (`GradeButtonRow`/`AnswerOption`), note/case-study reading bodies + Literata activation, `AnswerOption` correct-state green removal, the three carried items (Items-Mastered stat re-point, seeded test card, Items-Reviewed `created_at`).
- **Sprint 6.1 — Design Foundation (Sep 2026):** ✅ *Code complete 04/09/2026; `npm run build` clean; committed + on `main` (`f73e444`). No SQL, no page migrated, no behaviour change — additive only.* Lays the reskin foundation as reusable tokens + isolated primitives. **`--rv-*` token layer** (`src/index.css`, additive): full LIGHT + DARK objects from `docs/active/design-review/revisop-pass2-reference.jsx` (the committed Pass 2 reference) → precise HSL triplets. Scales: `--rv-bg-0/1/2`, `--rv-border(-strong)`, `--rv-ink-900/600/400`, `--rv-navy(/-400/-100/-50)`, `--rv-amber(/-ink/-50/-edge)`, `--rv-green(/-50)`, `--rv-slate(/-50)`, `--rv-danger`; two-tier radius `--rv-radius-rec` 4px (records) / `--rv-radius-obj` 14px (study objects); `--rv-shadow` / `--rv-shadow-bar`; `--rv-font-sans` (IBM Plex Sans) / `--rv-font-mono` (IBM Plex Mono) / `--rv-font-read` (Literata). Dark values under the existing `.dark` selector. **No shadcn or Phase-5 `--brand-*`/`--surface-*` value changed** (`git diff src/index.css` = pure additions). `tailwind.config.js` gains new keys only: `colors.rv.*`, `borderRadius.rec/.obj`, `fontFamily.plex/plex-mono/literata`, `boxShadow.rv/rv-bar`. **Self-hosted type system** (`public/fonts/*.woff2` + `@font-face` in `index.css`, `font-display: swap`, Latin subset, served same-origin — **no font-CDN request**): `plex-sans.woff2` (variable, wght 100–700, ~45.7 KB), `plex-mono-400.woff2` / `plex-mono-500.woff2` (~14.7/14.9 KB), `literata.woff2` (variable, ~85.7 KB, **gated off** via `REVISOP_LITERATA_ENABLED` in `src/lib/revisop-tokens.js` — applied to nothing). Faces are applied ONLY by the new primitives (no global `body`/base rule). **Primitives** — `src/components/revisop/` (presentational, isolated, NOT imported by any prod page): `Wordmark` (two-tone amber `Revis` + navy `Op`, token-driven, **no gradient** — the reference's blue→purple gradient wordmark is explicitly rejected; light-mode output byte-identical to the current `NavDesktop.jsx:83` inline mark), `IntervalChip` (mono micro forward-ledger marker, r4), `Card` (r14), `Row` (r4, optional `VerifiedEdge`), `VerifiedEdge` (3px navy rail), `AnswerOption` (glyph + label, slate miss — never red), `ForwardLedgerMicro` (single-item bucket rail, lives in a grade button), `ForwardLedgerMacro` (Today / cohort scheduled-load bar; consumes `get_study_queue` / `get_due_forecast` shapes via `ledgerFromForecast()`; sample data on the QA route), `GradeButtonRow` (the climax — ≥48px targets @ 88px, equal-weight navy-outline, mono interval text, slate miss state, no traffic-light colour), plus `Label` / `Num` type atoms and an `index.js` barrel. **QA route:** the existing dev-only `/__design` (`src/pages/dev/DesignShowcase.jsx`) extended with every `--rv-*` swatch + every primitive, light **and** dark via a theme toggle (`.dark`-class wrapper) + a both-themes-side-by-side view. The `/__design` route **and** its lazy import are now gated on `import.meta.env.DEV` — a production build registers no route and emits no chunk for it. **Literata verdict:** keep the file + `@font-face` (an unmatched `@font-face` = 0 bytes over the wire); measured cost when 6.4 wires it to reading bodies ≈ **85.7 KB** Latin-subset woff2 (larger than all three UI faces combined ~75 KB) → 6.4 should lazy-trigger the download when a reading body mounts, not put it in the critical path. **`context.md` branding line:** verified already corrected 30/08/2026 — no stale blue→purple gradient instruction remains; step 7 already satisfied. **Deferred:** wordmark→Nav swap (6.2); ledger + dashboards live-wiring (6.3); `GradeButtonRow`→`get_srs_ladder_config` + Literata on + study loop onto tokens (6.4).
- **Sprint 7.0 — Global Perf & Correctness (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 1:** ✅ *SHIPPED 08/09/2026 (`6c78f73`, pushed to `main` → Vercel auto-deploy). `npm run build` clean (7.2s); `npx eslint` clean on every changed file. **NO SQL — frontend only, §1.4 unchanged.** Invisible plumbing before the question-type epic — no study-loop behaviour change, no data-model change. **Verified per-role on the dev server → live Supabase (student + professor + super-admin):** `/dashboard/notes` `profiles` 17→3 all roles, four nav RPCs 6→1 each, nav sweeps 0 4xx / 0 console errors, role gating identical, 7.0-E inversion live (professor "Due Today: 7" → amber, was neutral). Operator follow-ups: disable Speed Insights in Vercel (7.0-C); optional cold-load spot-check on `revisop.com` (7.0-B, production-only path).* Clears the isolated perf/correctness debt from the Phase 6 live-verification report (Findings 3, 5, 6). **7.0-A — nav / notification over-fetch (Finding 5).** Root cause: `AuthContext` called `setUser(session?.user ?? null)` on every supabase-js auth event (`INITIAL_SESSION`, `SIGNED_IN` — re-emitted on each tab focus — `TOKEN_REFRESHED`), each with a **fresh object reference**, so every `[user]`-keyed effect app-wide (`useRole`, `useNotifications`, `useFriendRequestCount`, `CourseContext.fetchTeachingCourses`, `updateUserTimezone`, `useActivityFeed`) re-fired ~12× per load. Fix: **(1)** `applySession()` sets `user` via a functional updater that returns the previous reference when `prev?.id === next?.id` → React bails, `user` identity is stable across token refreshes / repeat SIGNED_IN. **(2)** new **`src/contexts/NavDataContext.jsx`** — `<NavDataProvider>` mounted once (in `App.jsx`, above `<BrowserRouter>`, inside `CourseContextProvider`) owns `useRole()` / `useNotifications(5)` / `useFriendRequestCount()` as **one instance**; `Navigation.jsx` and nine former `useRole()` consumers (`AdminAnalytics`, `AdminDashboard`, `BulkUploadTopics`, `SuperAdminAnalytics`, `SuperAdminDashboard`, `BulkUploadFlashcards`, `ProfessorAnalytics`, `MyFlashcards`, `NoteDetail`) import the drop-in `useRole` shim from the context instead of `@/hooks/useRole` (identical return shape; `useRole.js` stays as the implementation the provider calls). `Help.jsx` left on `@/hooks/useRole` — the import swap surfaced a pre-existing `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` error at `Help.jsx:192` (unrelated to this sprint); `professor/ProfessorTools.jsx` is dead/unrouted, left as-is. **(3)** `updateUserTimezone` gated by a module-scoped `tzSyncedThisSession` flag → at most one `profiles` read per page load (was one per getSession + per SIGNED_IN). **(4)** `ProfileDropdown` reads the name from `user.user_metadata.full_name` first (same source `NavMobile` uses), falling back to a `profiles` read only for accounts without it. **(5)** `CourseContext` now exposes its already-fetched `role` + `courseLevel`; `BrowseNotes.jsx` consumes them instead of its own duplicate `profiles.select('role, course_level')`. **Before → after on one `/dashboard/notes` load (super-admin, dev server):** `profiles` 17 → **3** (`timezone` + `useRole` role + `CourseContext` role/course — each once); `get_recent_notifications` / `get_unread_notification_count` / `friendships` / `role_permissions` 6 → **1 each**. (Production Phase-6 baseline was `profiles` ×46, those four ×12.) Targets (`profiles ≤ 3`, each nav RPC ≤ 2) met. Realtime notification + friend-request subscriptions unaffected (one channel each). Role gating (`isAdmin`/`isProfessor`/`isSuperAdmin`), the Manage dropdown, notification/friend badges and the mobile sheet render identically (super-admin confirmed; student + professor pending operator). **7.0-B — 2× `400` on every authed page (Finding 6).** Root cause named: `get_recent_notifications` / `get_unread_notification_count` (and the `role_permissions` / `profiles` / `friendships` reads) fired during the window where the Supabase access token is stale / not-yet-attached, so `auth.uid()` is NULL server-side and the RPC's `RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authenticated'` surfaces as **HTTP 400 (`P0001`)** — **not** a 401, and **not** an RPC defect (direct calls with a valid session all return 200; no grant / overload / signature issue). **Fix layer = frontend, no SQL:** the 7.0-A `user`-identity stabilisation removes the ×12 churn that kept the race landing; the only remaining fire is the single post-`getSession` one (token already valid). Post-fix: **zero 4xx** across a 9-route soft-nav sweep (super-admin); cold-load 3-role sweep pending operator. **7.0-C — web-vitals `startTime` TypeError (Finding 3).** Not in the repo — no `web-vitals` / `@vercel/speed-insights` / `@vercel/analytics` in `package.json`, no import in `src/`, nothing in `index.html` / `vercel.json`. It is injected by **Vercel Speed Insights** at the edge (`/_vercel/speed-insights/script.js`, bundles `web-vitals`), toggled on in the Vercel *project dashboard* — not version-pinnable by us. Known upstream web-vitals issue on soft-nav / bfcache; benign (RUM reporter only), 0/6 reproductions in the Phase-6 sweep. **Resolution:** operator to disable Speed Insights in the Vercel dashboard (RUM unused) → removes the script at source; documented benign-upstream otherwise. **7.0-D — `<Num>` / `rv-*` class override.** The `text-rv-*` half of the `reference_rv_num_atom_tailwind_ordering` memo is **stale**: `tailwind-merge@3.4.0` already resolves `text-rv-*` / `bg-rv-*` / `border-rv-*` last-wins via its permissive colour-position fallback (verified live: `cn('text-rv-ink-900','text-rv-danger')` → `'text-rv-danger'` → `rgb(185,28,28)`). What *was* genuinely un-merged: `rounded-rec` / `rounded-obj` (twMerge kept `rounded-lg rounded-rec` both) and `shadow-rv` / `shadow-rv-bar` — latent (no live collision, but a real footgun). **Fix — approach (a):** `src/lib/utils.js` `cn()` now uses `extendTailwindMerge` registering `rounded → rec|obj`, `shadow → rv|rv-bar`, and the `text/bg/border-rv-*` colour scale (the last as hardening against a future tw-merge that tightens the fallback). Regression-checked on `/__design` light + dark — computed radii (4px / 14px), shadows and colours identical before/after; toned `<Num>` now provably takes its override (`text-rv-danger` → `#b91c1c` light / `#ef4444` dark). The Sprint 6.5 `NUM_TYPE` workaround reverted in `Progress.jsx` `ForecastCard` → `<Num className={t.text}>`; the `NUM_TYPE` const kept only for the "inherit a muted parent colour" case (QuestionTypeRow). Memo `reference_rv_num_atom_tailwind_ordering` updated. **7.0-E — Due-Today vs forecast tone hierarchy.** Anand's call (30-year educator, product decision): **invert** — "Due Today" is the loudest surface because it is the only call to action. `Progress.jsx` `ForecastCard` `FORECAST_TONES` now: `calm` (0 → muted `--rv-green`) / `amber` (1..`DUE_TODAY_ALARM_THRESHOLD` → `--rv-amber-50`/`--rv-amber-edge`, "do these now") / `danger` (> threshold → `--rv-danger`, genuine backlog); **"Next 7 / 30 Days" → new `quiet` tone** (`--rv-bg-1` / `--rv-border` / `text-rv-ink-600`) — was `amber`, now the softest. `DUE_TODAY_ALARM_THRESHOLD` unchanged (24). Aligned surfaces: `Dashboard.jsx` student **"N items ready" CTA** (legacy `from-green-50` gradient → `bg-rv-amber-50 border-rv-amber-edge`, icon on amber, text on `--rv-ink-*`, button loses the `bg-green-600` override → default navy) and **"All caught up" card** (legacy `bg-amber-50 border-amber-200` → `bg-rv-green-50 border-rv-border` calm, matching Progress "Due Today: 0"). `ForwardLedgerMacro` already a navy magnitude ramp (not an alarm) — unchanged. Retrospective "Your Week" stat-tile lucide accents left (not a due/forecast surface). Verified super-admin: Progress "Due Today: 0" → calm green, "Next 7/30 Days" → quiet ink; amber + danger tone classes computed-checked. **Sentry:** 7.0 is a reasonable home for an init (error visibility before the risky epic sprints) but **not added** — needs Anand's go-ahead + a DSN. **Files:** `src/contexts/{AuthContext,NavDataContext,CourseContext}.jsx`, `src/App.jsx`, `src/components/layout/{Navigation,ProfileDropdown}.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Content/BrowseNotes.jsx`, `src/lib/utils.js`, `src/pages/dashboard/Study/Progress.jsx`, `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx`, + import-path swap on the 9 `useRole` consumers above.
- **Sprint 7.1 — Mobile Bottom Navigation (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 2:** ✅ *SHIPPED 08/09/2026. `npm run build` clean (7.0s); `npx eslint` clean on every file this sprint authored or structurally changed (pre-existing baseline errors in `FlashcardCreate.jsx` / `NoteUpload.jsx` / `ReviewFlashcards.jsx` are unrelated — className-only edits there). **NO SQL.** No new data fetching. Desktop (`md:` and up) visually unchanged. `/__design` light + dark computed-style verified; navActive parity proven (20 routes × 5 predicates, byte-identical to the old NavDesktop inline logic). **Live-verified across all three roles** (student / professor / super-admin) on the dev server → live Supabase — two issues found & fixed (review-picker lost the bar; pre-existing 390px card clip), see item 6 below.* Ships the mobile bottom-tab bar (kickoff item G) on top of the 7.0 nav refactor. **Layout A — decided with Anand** ("reconcile the hamburger"; thumb-reach + no duplicate nav for a non-power-user cohort were decisive; Groups going one tap deeper into the Menu drawer is acceptable since Review, not Groups, is the daily driver). **New component — `src/components/layout/NavBottomTabs.jsx`:** `md:hidden`, `position: fixed; inset-inline: 0; bottom: 0; z-50`, full-width — rendered by `Navigation.jsx` as a **sibling** of the top `<nav>` (a fragment now), so it is NOT inside the `max-w-7xl` container. Surface `bg-rv-bg-1 border-t border-rv-border shadow-rv-bar font-plex`; row `h-14` (56px); every tab target `min-h-[48px]`. **Data-driven `TABS` array** — `route` slots (Dashboard / Review / Progress) render a `<Link>`; `create` and `menu` are structural. A later top-level destination (exam anchor, a new question-type route) is a one-line addition to `TABS`. **Consumes the `navProps` bundle from `Navigation.jsx` only** — no `useRole` / `useNotifications` / `useFriendRequestCount` call (would re-introduce the 7.0 over-fetch); zero fetch of its own. **Slots (exactly 5):** Dashboard · Review · **＋** · Progress · Menu. **＋** = a small bottom action-sheet (`<Sheet side="bottom">`, `rounded-t-obj`) — Upload Note / Create Flashcard / Bulk Upload; **Bulk Upload hidden unless `isProfessor || isAdmin || isSuperAdmin`**. **Menu** = the EXISTING NavMobile drawer, moved **verbatim** into new **`src/components/layout/NavMenuSheet.jsx`** (the Radix `<Sheet side="right">` + all its content — Study/Create/Groups/Following/Progress/Contributions/Achievements/Help/Settings + Course-context switcher + professor/admin/super-admin sections + Sign Out); Friends + Bell (and their unread/pending badges) stay in the top bar (no counted entity maps to a bottom tab without a new fetch — a deliberate departure from the kickoff's "unread/pending badge on the bar" line). **Shared active-route module — `src/lib/navActive.js`:** the Sprint 6.0/6.2 route predicates (`isExact` / `underAny` / `isCreateActive` / `isStudyActive` / `isManageActive` / `isGroupsActive`) factored out as pure `(pathname) => boolean` fns; `/dashboard/notes/:id` → Study via prefix). New in the module: **`isReviewTabActive(pathname)`** (bottom-bar "Review" tab — a tighter slice than `isStudyActive`: the study-session cluster only — `review-flashcards` / `review-session` / `review-by-subject` / `study`; the Browse-Notes and My-Flashcards **list** routes and note detail read as *no active tab*, since the bar has its own Dashboard + Progress tabs and a library-browsing student is not in review). *(An `isFullScreenStudyRoute` helper existed in the first cut but was dropped — see the item-6 refinement above; the bar now hides via `StudySessionContext`, not a route match.)*. **Study-loop collision (item 6) — resolved via (a), refined after live verification:** the bar hides while the full-screen card loop is mounted. A route match (`/dashboard/study` + `/dashboard/review-session`) was the first cut but `revisop.com` testing showed `/dashboard/review-session` is a **subject-picker list** until the user taps Start — a route check stranded them there with no nav. Final mechanism: new **`src/contexts/StudySessionContext.jsx`** (`<StudySessionProvider>` in `App.jsx`, above the router; `useStudySession()` → `{ inStudySession, setInStudySession }`, safe outside the provider). **`StudyMode.jsx`** — the one component that renders the card loop in BOTH entry paths (the `/dashboard/study` route AND embedded in `ReviewSession`'s active view) — sets the flag `true` on mount / `false` on unmount; `NavBottomTabs` returns `null` while it is `true`. Result: the review-session picker keeps the bar, tapping Start (which mounts `StudyMode`) removes it, "Back to Selection" restores it. **Pre-existing bug fixed as a ride-along (surfaced by the same live pass):** the `StudyMode` card clipped on the right at 390px (`overflow-hidden` card + a no-wrap `Skip 24hr · Show Answer · ⋮` row + `p-8`) — presentation-only fix: inner padding `p-8` → `p-5 sm:p-8 md:p-12` + `min-w-0` on the flex child; both action rows → `flex-wrap`; `Show Answer` `px-8` → `px-6 sm:px-8`. Pages NOT wrapped in `PageContainer` (10 `dashboard/Content` + `Study` list pages on `min-h-screen bg-gray-50`, 3 admin dashboards, `MyContributions`, `NoteEdit`) got the same clearance utility appended to their root — additive class only, no reskin, `bg-gray-50` left as-is. **`PageContainer` ride-along:** `bg-gray-50` → `bg-rv-bg-0` (one-token swap; `#f9fafb` → `#f9f9fb`, no visible regression). **`/__design`:** new "Mobile bottom nav" block in `ReskinGallery` (the bar with active/inactive tabs + the ＋ action-sheet, light **and** dark) — computed-style checked: light bar `#fff` / border `--rv-border` / active `#1e1b4b` @ 2px top-border / inactive `#6b7280`; dark bar `#171a35` / active accent `#a5b4fc` / inactive `#9aa1b8`; `shadow-rv-bar` present. **Files:** new — `src/components/layout/NavBottomTabs.jsx`, `src/components/layout/NavMenuSheet.jsx`, `src/lib/navActive.js`, `src/contexts/StudySessionContext.jsx`; - **Phase 5 — Sprint 6 (Jul 2026):** ✅ *Deployed & verified 2026-07-02* (`docs/database/phase5/17–21`). Final Phase 5 sprint — hybrid educator on-ramp: self-serve application form → admin approval → `professor` role grant (two-step, admin-gated; no self-upgrade). Ground-truth introspection (before any `CREATE OR REPLACE`, per the S4 42P13 lesson) found `access_requests` has no approver/approved-at column and `link_access_request(p_ref_token uuid) RETURNS void` is far simpler than assumed — it only tags `profiles.access_request_ref`, it never touches `access_requests`. **SQL:** `17_SCHEMA` extends `access_requests_status_check` (was `('pending','contacted','enrolled')` — notably missing `'dismissed'` too, a pre-existing gap since closed by `22_SCHEMA` ✅ deployed 2026-07-02) to add `'approved'`/`'rejected'`; no new column added. `18_FUNCTIONS` adds `submit_educator_application()` (SECURITY DEFINER, `GRANT TO anon, authenticated`) — required fields full name/WhatsApp/credential-or-LinkedIn URL (the vetting proof), optional email/institute/course/why; maps onto `access_requests` exactly like `submit_institute_inquiry` (`content_name` reused for institute, `message` combines credential+why); **returns the row's `ref_token` (not void)** so an anonymous applicant's browser can store it into `localStorage['revisop_access_ref']` — the same slot `Signup.jsx`'s `?ref=` param already populates — so a same-browser signup later auto-links. `19_FUNCTIONS` adds `approve_educator_application(p_request_id)` (admin/super_admin only, mirrors the S3 nomination RPCs' role-guard idiom; returns `'role_granted'` if the applicant already has an account — flips `profiles.role` + notifies immediately — or `'approved_pending_signup'` if not, deferring the grant) and `reject_educator_application(p_request_id)`. `20_FUNCTIONS` extends `link_access_request` (`CREATE OR REPLACE`, exact introspected signature/return type preserved, no other request_types affected) so that on first login, if the carried `ref_token` matches an already-approved `educator_application`, it grants `professor` right then — closing the apply-anonymously-then-sign-up path (hardened post-review to a one-time, atomically-claimed grant guarded on `requester_user_id IS NULL`, so a token mints at most one professor and never demotes an admin). **21_TEST: 11 blocks** covering anon submit + field mapping, required-field/course-default validation, admin notification, the admin-only gate, both approve outcomes (linked immediate grant vs. deferred), reject, the link-on-signup flip, and a regression guard that a still-pending application never grants the role, plus a security check that an approved token is one-time (replay by a second account is rejected). **Frontend (deployed & pushed 2026-07-02):** `Educators.jsx` gets a second, clearly-labeled "Apply to Teach on RevisOp" section (distinct from the S5 institute form) — anonymous-safe, zero direct `.from()`, "We'll review and reach out within 1–2 business days" success copy. `AdminDashboard.jsx`'s Access Requests queue gets an "Educator Applications" filter + purple "Educator" badge, a read-only status badge (bypassing the generic pending/contacted/enrolled/dismissed dropdown, which must not be used for this request_type since it would bypass the approval RPCs' role-grant side effects), and Approve/Reject buttons that show the resulting state (role granted / awaiting signup with a copyable signup link / rejected). **`npm run build` passes; verified in-browser against the live (undeployed) backend — the new form renders, submits with exactly the RPC's parameter names, and the resulting PGRST202 "function not found" confirms the wiring is correct pending deployment.** **Phase 5 is COMPLETE — all six sprints deployed & shipped 2026-07-02.** See `now.md`.

Live-verified per-role (student TestOutlook + professor CA Anand More), dev server → live Supabase, desktop + 390px mobile.* **7.2-D — student dashboard restructure:** the Streak/Accuracy/Mastered/Reviews stat tiles (`Dashboard.jsx` "Your Week" section) moved ABOVE the review CTA (was below); the professor's own `dueToday` (read from the same `NavDataContext` singleton 7.2-F wires up — no third `get_due_forecast` call) now sits beside it for contrast. **7.2-E — Create Group:** `NavBottomTabs.jsx`'s ＋ action-sheet gains a "Create Group" entry (`Network` icon → `/dashboard/groups/new`), same pattern as the other three entries, no professor-gating (Groups is student-visible). **7.2-B — unified notification center:** new **`src/components/layout/NotificationCenter.jsx`** merges the former `FriendsDropdown` + `ActivityDropdown` into one bell icon/dropdown — imported by both `NavDesktop.jsx` and `NavMobile.jsx` (single shared component, no duplicated merge logic); `FriendsDropdown.jsx` / `ActivityDropdown.jsx` deleted (no other importers). One feed: pending friend requests (inline Accept/Decline preserved verbatim, fetched on open exactly as before) render at the top when present, content notifications below; the Find People/My Friends/Following/View All Requests quick links preserved as a footer (dropping them would have orphaned `/dashboard/find-friends` + `/dashboard/my-friends`, unreachable elsewhere in the nav). Single badge = `unreadCount + pendingCount` (summed, capped `"9+"` — the two-part-indicator alternative wasn't worth the extra visual complexity for one bell). Mobile top bar is now Wordmark (left) · Bell (right); desktop nav's right-side icon row lost one icon the same way. `useFriendRequestCount`/`useNotifications` still flow through `NavDataContext` unchanged — UI merge only, zero new fetch. **7.2-C — professor dashboard restructure:** the "Quick Actions" grid (Upload Note/Create Flashcard/Bulk Upload/Analytics-link) removed entirely — redundant since 7.1 put Create actions in the desktop Create dropdown + mobile ＋ sheet. New "Analytics Snapshot" section embedded directly on `Dashboard.jsx`'s professor branch: the `overview` row (Cards Published/Students Reached/Total Reviews/Avg Quality) + "Challenging Cards" (top 5 `get_professor_weak_cards`, min-3-reviews) — the two most actionable `ProfessorAnalytics.jsx` sections, styled as a light duplicate (own `--rv-*` markup, not a shared component — `ProfessorAnalytics.jsx` is still on pre-reskin gray tokens, so extracting a shared component would have forced an unplanned reskin of that page) rather than importing `ProfessorAnalytics.jsx`'s gray-toned internals; Subjects/Top-Cards/Weekly-Reach stay behind a new "View full analytics →" link. Fetch: `fetchEducatorWidgets()` (`Dashboard.jsx`) now fires FOUR RPCs in one `Promise.all` (`get_educator_accuracy_by_qtype` + `get_educator_cohort_forecast_buckets` from 6.3, plus new `get_professor_overview` + `get_professor_weak_cards`) — once per mount, once per course switch, matching `ProfessorAnalytics.jsx`'s own pattern; network-traced to confirm no Sprint-7.0-over-fetch regression. "Needs Attention" moved to the top of the page (still fully unconditional — no new render gate, per the settled-design rule); "Your Content" (My Notes/My Flashcards) pushed to just above the Accuracy-by-question-type section (was first). **Files:** *new* — `NotificationCenter.jsx`, `useDueForecast.js`. *deleted* — `FriendsDropdown.jsx`, `ActivityDropdown.jsx`. *changed* — `Dashboard.jsx`, `NavDataContext.jsx`, `Navigation.jsx`, `NavDesktop.jsx`, `NavMobile.jsx`, `NavBottomTabs.jsx`.
- **Sprint 7.3 — Dashboard Reporting Surface & Study-Time Split (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 4:** ✅ *SHIPPED 12/09/2026 (`77cfe0c`, pushed to `main` → Vercel auto-deploy). `npm run build` clean; `npx eslint` clean on every changed file. Inserted ahead of the `review_events`/`apply_review` engine sprint (now **7.4**) — final round of dashboard/nav polish before 100+ new students land.* **SQL deployed + confirmed live** (`docs/database/sprint7.3/`): `01_SCHEMA` added `profiles.has_dismissed_goal_prompt boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`; `02_FUNCTIONS` DROP+CREATEd `get_study_time_stats` to add 4 additive in-app/offline split columns (existing 4 columns/IDOR guard/search_path unchanged). Final order: Goal Progress → Leaderboard → Forward Load → Study Time (report only) → Recent Activity; Get Started card, push banner, and the caught-up browse-links strip unchanged/unmoved. **7.3-B — Daily Goal split into action + report:** `OnboardingModal.jsx` gains a 4th step ("Set your daily goal" → `/dashboard/settings`, same shape/skip semantics as the other 3 — no render-logic change needed). `ProfileSettings.jsx` gains a "Daily Goal" Card (students only, `!isContentCreator`) — type toggle (Reviews/day vs Study minutes/day) + Save/Clear, same `update_daily_goal` RPC as the widget, no new RPC. `GoalProgressWidget.jsx` renamed "Goal Progress", two states: goal set → progress bar + de-emphasized "Revise target" link below the numbers (was a prominent top-right "Edit"); no goal set → no card/header, just a dismissible line ("Set a daily goal →" / "Not now") — dismissing calls a new `handleDismissGoalPrompt` (Dashboard.jsx, mirrors the existing `handleDismissOnboarding` pattern) that persists `has_dismissed_goal_prompt` + shows a one-time toast. Goal-vs-actual comparison still uses the combined (in-app + offline) total. **7.3-C — Study Time split + localStorage collision fix + app-wide timer:** new **`src/contexts/StudyTimerContext.jsx`** (mounted in `App.jsx` beside `StudySessionProvider`) is now the sole owner of the manual timer's state and its 3-tier stale-session policy (<4h auto-resume / 4-16h prompt / >16h discard) — classified via lazy `useState` initializers on provider mount (app-wide, not gated behind loading `StudyTimerWidget`'s old host page), with a `window.addEventListener('storage', …)` handler for cross-tab sync and a coarse (~30s) `elapsedMs` refresh (per-second precision stays local to the dedicated page's own DOM-ref clock). **New localStorage key `revisop_manual_timer_started_at`** — no longer shares `revisop_session_started_at`/`revisop_session_source` with `StudyMode.jsx`/`ReviewSession.jsx` (those two keep their keys/values exactly as-is), fixing a real collision where starting a manual timer and an in-app review session concurrently clobbered whichever wrote last. Context exposes `{ isRunning, startedAt, elapsedMs, recoveryPrompt, start(), stop(), stopAndLog(durationSeconds), discard() }` — `stop()` applies the tiered policy once, shared by both the nav chip and the dedicated page's Stop button. `StudyTimerWidget.jsx` rewritten as a thin consumer (no more owned state) and moved off the Dashboard onto a new dedicated route **`/dashboard/study-time`** (`StudyTimePage.jsx`), reachable from both the desktop Create dropdown and the mobile ＋ sheet. New **`StudyTimerChip.jsx`** nav indicator (beside the bell, both `NavDesktop`/`NavMobile`) — hidden unless running, tap-to-stop (<4h logs + toasts, 4-16h navigates to the dedicated route where the recovery prompt is already showing via context state, >16h defensively discards + toasts). Dashboard's Study Time report now shows Today/This Week each as combined + in-app/offline breakdown, no interactive control. **7.3-D — Bulk Upload removed from the mobile ＋ sheet for ALL roles** (`canBulkUpload` gate deleted, not narrowed — CSV import isn't a phone workflow); desktop Create dropdown keeps it unconditionally for all roles, plus gains the same "Log Study Time" entry (with a divider) that the mobile sheet has. **Files:** *new* — `src/contexts/StudyTimerContext.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Study/StudyTimePage.jsx`, `src/components/layout/StudyTimerChip.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Profile/MyReports.jsx`, `docs/database/sprint7.3/*.sql`. *changed* — `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx`, `src/components/dashboard/{GoalProgressWidget,StudyTimerWidget,OnboardingModal}.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Profile/ProfileSettings.jsx`, `src/components/layout/{NavDesktop,NavMobile,NavBottomTabs,ProfileDropdown,NavMenuSheet}.jsx`, `src/App.jsx`. **Follow-up (same session, post-ship):** confirmed with Anand that Quick Actions and My Contributions were meant to come off the student dashboard too, so Recent Activity is the last card — both removed (`My Contributions` page is still reachable via the `ProfileDropdown`/`NavMenuSheet` link, so nothing was lost). The conditional "My Reports" card (a moderation-flag status tracker — content the student themselves flagged, unrelated to My Progress) moved off the dashboard entirely onto a new dedicated page **`src/pages/dashboard/Profile/MyReports.jsx`** at **`/dashboard/my-reports`**, labeled **"Report History"** in the nav (name chosen to read as a status list, not be confused with academic progress reports) — linked from `ProfileDropdown.jsx` and `NavMenuSheet.jsx` alongside My Progress/My Contributions/My Achievements.
