Found and fixed a stale copy bug surfaced by that pass: `NavBottomTabs.jsx`'s sr-only `SheetDescription` still said "…or a bulk upload" after 7.3-D removed it. Stale-session test: faked `revisop_manual_timer_started_at` to 6h old, reloaded `/dashboard` (not the study-time page) — `StudyTimerContext` classified it into `recoveryPrompt` at the app root (no chip shown, since `isRunning` stays false during recovery); client-side nav (no reload) to `/dashboard/study-time` showed the "Your timer ran for 6h 0m…" prompt already up, proving classification happens at Dashboard-mount, not re-derived per page. Faked it to 20h old — silently discarded on mount, confirmed via `localStorage.getItem` returning null, no chip, clean idle state on the study-time page. **Not yet tested:** two-tab cross-sync via the `storage` event listener. **Dashboard header cleanup (Anand's ask):** removed the subtitle line entirely (`"You have N items ready for review"` / `"All caught up! 🎉"` for returning users, `"Let's get you started…"` for new users) — it duplicated the Review-tab due badge (7.2-F) and took up vertical space right below the "Welcome back" line; the header is now just the `<h1>`, no `<p>` subtitle. `reviewsDue` state stays (still gates the caught-up browse-links strip below).
- **Sprint 7.4 — review_events, apply_review, two-measure analytics semantics (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 5 (the de-risk sprint):** ✅ *SQL deployed & verified 13/09/2026 (all 4 files, `docs/database/sprint7.4/`); frontend live-verified against production via a real student account (both new-card and review-session grading paths, 200 OK, correct rung/interval each time); `npm run build` clean; `npx eslint` clean on every changed file.* Architectural de-risk gate for the whole question-type epic — no question-type rendering here, StudyMode stays pure front/back; this is data/engine layer only (see D-11). **Phase 0 introspection (`00_DIAGNOSTIC`) surfaced two deviations from the sprint's own draft SQL, both fixed before deploying:** (1) `reviews.user_id` actually references `auth.users(id)`, not `profiles(id)` — `review_events.user_id` matches that, not the draft's assumption; `flashcard_id`'s `ON DELETE CASCADE` matched as assumed. (2) `get_question_type_performance`/`get_educator_accuracy_by_qtype` needed DROP+CREATE (not `CREATE OR REPLACE`) since the `RETURNS TABLE` shape changes (rename + 2 new columns) — same call this project made for `get_study_queue` in the SRS Ladder epic, for the same reason. **`01_SCHEMA`:** new `public.review_events` (bigint identity PK, one row per grade ever, RLS enabled with zero policies/grants — every access via SECURITY DEFINER RPC only). **`02_FUNCTIONS`:** new `apply_review(p_user_id, p_flashcard_id, p_rating, p_is_correct DEFAULT NULL, p_source DEFAULT NULL)` absorbs `submit_review`'s live body verbatim (confirmed byte-identical via introspection before extending) and adds one `review_events` INSERT in the same transaction as the `reviews` write — atomic by construction, proven with a real forced-failure trigger test, not just asserted. `submit_review` becomes a thin `LANGUAGE sql` wrapper delegating to `apply_review(..., NULL, NULL)` for backward compat. **`03_FUNCTIONS`:** both analytics RPCs renamed `accuracy_pct`→`recall_success_pct` (computation unchanged) and gained `graded_count`/`answer_accuracy_pct` (NULL until graded) — pre-aggregated `review_events` into a one-row-per-key CTE first in both, specifically to avoid fanning out the existing accuracy arithmetic (review_events is append-only/many-rows-per-card, unlike the at-most-one-row-per-card `reviews` joins already in those queries). **`04_TEST`** (BEGIN/ROLLBACK): re-ran the full srs-ladder Phase 1 transition-maths suite against `apply_review` instead of `submit_review` — 29/29 real assertions PASS (one authoring slip in the test itself, a hardcoded wrong expected interval, caught and fixed — not a function defect) — plus new checks: `rung_before` NULL on a new card, exactly one `review_events` row per grade, the `submit_review` shim logging `is_correct=NULL`, both IDOR paths, concept-card rejection, and a genuine atomicity test (a `pg_temp` trigger forces a failure inside `apply_review` after the `reviews` write; confirmed the `reviews` write rolled back with it via PL/pgSQL's implicit EXCEPTION-block savepoint — not simulated). **Frontend:** `StudyMode.jsx` `handleRating` calls `apply_review` (not `submit_review`) with `p_is_correct: null` (no verdict exists yet) and `p_source` = `'new_card'` | `'review_session'` | `null` (preview mode), reusing the existing `rung === undefined` new-card convention from `fetchFlashcards`. `Progress.jsx`'s `QuestionTypeRow` and `Dashboard.jsx`'s professor "Accuracy by question type" widget both now show two labeled rows per question type — "Recall success" (always populated) and "Answer accuracy" ("No graded answers yet" until `graded_count>0`) — with an updated footnote naming both measures; verified live that "Recall success" is the same number as before the rename (rename-only proof) and "Answer accuracy" correctly shows the empty state for every type (no graded question types exist pre-7.5). **7.4-E storage projection (real measurements, not estimates):** ~142 bytes/row (`pg_column_size` on real inserted rows); current DB 53MB against the Free-plan 500MB limit; even a generous 10×-of-observed daily rate projects to low-single-digit MB/year — no near-term concern, so no archival/rollup/partitioning was built, per the sprint's own "measure, don't build machinery" instruction. **Files:** *new* — `docs/database/sprint7.4/*.sql` (00_DIAGNOSTIC, 01_SCHEMA, 02_FUNCTIONS, 03_FUNCTIONS, 04_TEST, 05_DIAGNOSTIC). *changed* — `src/pages/dashboard/Study/StudyMode.jsx` (handleRating), `src/pages/dashboard/Study/Progress.jsx` (QuestionTypeRow + footnote), `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` (educator accuracy widget + footnote). **Not live-verified this sprint:** the `Dashboard.jsx` professor widget (no professor test account available) — code-reviewed against the same live-confirmed RPC shape as the student widget, not clicked through live; low risk (same render pattern, same RPC family). **Ready for Sprint 7.5** (MCQ-single vertical slice — the architectural proof, and where D-10's professor-only role-gate lands).
- **Sprint 7.6 — Browse/My Study Sets rename + question-type filter (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 7:** ✅ *SQL deployed & verified live (13/09/2026); frontend built, `npm run build`/`npx eslint` clean, live-verified against production via a real student account.* Small, focused sprint fixing the naming confusion raised at the end of 7.5 — `ReviewFlashcards.jsx` now browses ALL question types (flashcard + mcq, more coming), so the page needed a name that matches what it does. **Copy-only rename (routes/files/components unchanged):** `ReviewFlashcards.jsx`'s `<h1>` "Review Flashcards" → **"Browse Study Sets"** (subtitle drops flashcard-specific wording); `MyFlashcards.jsx`'s `<h1>` "My Flashcards" → **"My Study Sets"**; both `NavDesktop.jsx`'s Study dropdown and the mobile Menu drawer's (`NavMenuSheet.jsx`) matching item relabeled the same way. Also swept the same label into everywhere else it pointed at these two pages, so the rename doesn't reintroduce the inconsistency it's fixing: `Dashboard.jsx`'s student quick-link card, `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`'s post-upload "View My Flashcards" button, `helpContent.js`'s "Review by Subject" step, and `guideContent.js`'s 4 `linkLabel` occurrences (their `linkTo` values were left untouched — 3 of those steps' prose actually describes the due-today queue, not the browse page, a pre-existing `/dashboard/review-flashcards` vs. `/dashboard/review-session` mismatch unrelated to this rename; flagged separately rather than fixed here since correcting it is a functional change, not copy). **Question Type filter, added to `ReviewFlashcards.jsx`'s existing filter row (Course/Subject/Topic/Role/Author + new Question Type, same single-select `filterX`/`setFilterX` + `applyFilters()`/`clearAllFilters()` idiom):** options are "All" + `flashcard`/`mcq` only (the two types with a real authoring path this sprint — built from a `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` array in the new shared `src/lib/questionTypes.js`, not hardcoded JSX, so 7.7+ types are a one-line addition), labeled via `formatQuestionType` (moved from a `Dashboard.jsx`-local const into the same shared file so both pages use identical display strings). Semantics: narrows which DECKS show (a deck qualifies if it has ≥1 card of that type visible to the viewer) — does not change `card_count` (still whole-deck) or what a study session serves once a student clicks in (still the whole deck, mixed types). **SQL: `get_browsable_decks` v5** (`docs/database/sprint7.6/01_FUNCTIONS_get_browsable_decks_v5_question_type_filter.sql`) adds `p_question_type text DEFAULT NULL` — deployed live. **Real gotcha hit and fixed:** the first deploy attempt used plain `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_browsable_decks(p_question_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL)`, which did NOT replace the old zero-arg function — Postgres treats a changed parameter list (even one added with a DEFAULT) as a distinct overload, leaving both the old zero-arg and new one-arg versions live simultaneously and every unparameterized call ambiguous (`function get_browsable_decks() is not unique`, caught live via `02_TEST`). Fixed with an explicit `DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS get_browsable_decks();` before the `CREATE OR REPLACE` — worth remembering for any future RPC that adds (not just changes) a parameter. Frontend calls `get_browsable_decks({ p_question_type })`, refetching (not client-filtering, unlike the other five filters) since the deck-level type membership isn't in the payload otherwise; the existing dependent-filter effects (Subject/Topic re-validating against the narrower `allDecksFlat`) needed no new code, since they already react to `allDecksFlat` changing. **Live-verified (13/09/2026, dev server → `revisop.com`'s live Supabase, real student account, TestOutlook):** nav shows "Browse Study Sets" in both the desktop dropdown and mobile Menu drawer; heading/subtitle render correctly; Question Type dropdown shows exactly "All Types"/"Flashcard"/"MCQ" (via `formatQuestionType`); selecting MCQ narrowed 464→3 cards, showing only the one deck (Income Tax → Deductions from Gross Total Income) known from SQL testing to mix flashcard+mcq cards; clicking a filtered deck's "Study All" produced the un-parameterized `/dashboard/study?deck=...` URL (filter did not leak into the study session); `clearAllFilters()` correctly reset Question Type to "All" alongside the other five; console clean (one pre-existing, self-recovering `refresh_token_not_found` warning on hard reload, unrelated — reproduces without this sprint's changes). **Files:** *new* — `src/lib/questionTypes.js`, `docs/database/sprint7.6/{01_FUNCTIONS,02_TEST}*.sql`. *changed* — `src/pages/dashboard/Study/ReviewFlashcards.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Content/MyFlashcards.jsx`, `src/components/layout/{NavDesktop,NavMenuSheet}.jsx`, `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` (formatQuestionType extraction + quick-link label), `src/pages/dashboard/BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`, `src/data/{guideContent,helpContent}.js`, `docs/active/blueprint.md`, `docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`, `docs/active/now.md`, `docs/tracking/changelog.md`. **✅ Committed** (`c629ed3`, on `main`) — confirmed via this session's git log while working Sprint 7.7; this line's earlier "Not yet committed" note had gone stale (the commit landed after that note was written, same pattern as the 7.5 stale-note correction).
- **Sprint 7.7 — True/False, Correct/Incorrect + free-recall labels + Create-page naming sweep (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 8:** ✅ *SQL deployed & verified live (13/09/2026, all 3 files — `00_DIAGNOSTIC`/`01_SCHEMA`/`02_TEST`, 6/6 test rows PASS against real profiles). Frontend built and live-verified end to end on both professor + student accounts (real credentials, account owner typing them directly — Claude never saw or entered a password). `npm run build`/`npx eslint` clean on every changed file (pre-existing baseline errors in `FlashcardCreate.jsx`/`Home.jsx`/`ReviewFlashcards.jsx` confirmed via `git stash` to predate this sprint). **✅ Committed and pushed** (`1693e1a`, on `main`).* Second and third proof points that the question-type architecture generalizes cheaply: `true_false`/`correct_incorrect` are near-duplicates of the mcq machinery 7.5 already built, and `theory`/`test_your_understanding` need zero new StudyMode rendering at all. **Pre-flight (`docs/database/sprint7.7/00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql`, drafted):** confirms exactly one live `get_browsable_decks` signature (the 7.6 v5 one-arg version — the exact overload landmine class documented at §1.11 #3, hit for real during 7.6) and captures the live `chk_flashcards_question_type`/D-10-policy definitions to build the schema change from, not from memory of the docs. **Real finding, not assumed:** re-checking `project_content_types.md`/blueprint.md D-10 against the live CHECK constraint (Sprint 7.5's own diagnostic) showed `true_false`/`correct_incorrect` were ALREADY live values AND already in the D-10 RESTRICTIVE gate's IN-list — so 7.7-A needed zero RLS/schema changes, only a real authoring UI for two already-gated types. `test_your_understanding` was the opposite case: explicitly NOT live (confirmed twice now, first by 7.5's diagnostic, again here) — a genuine hard SQL prerequisite, not a docs correction, since `theory` and `test_your_understanding` were kicked off as a matched pair of free-recall types but only one of them could actually be inserted. **7.7-A (true_false/correct_incorrect):** `options` auto-populated from `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS` (`src/lib/questionTypes.js`) — `["True","False"]` / `["Correct","Incorrect"]`, never professor-typed — `correct_answer` the 0/1 index, `back_text` derived, same as mcq; `FlashcardCreate.jsx` swaps mcq's free-text options editor for a 2-way toggle button pair. **StudyMode.jsx needed a one-line generalization, not a new branch:** the mcq-only condition (`question_type === 'mcq'`) became `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES.includes(question_type)` (new export, `['mcq','true_false','correct_incorrect']`) — the existing `AnswerOption`-list render, hybrid-grading (wrong→auto-hard+Continue, correct→reveal+GradeButtonRow), and `apply_review` call shape are used completely unmodified, since a 2-option array renders through the identical map/state-machine as a 4-option one. **Answers this sprint's own report-back question: true_false and correct_incorrect turned out to be 100% identical in rendering** — the only difference anywhere in the codebase is the `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS` pair and the front-side "Statement" label text; zero divergent code paths were needed. **7.7-B (theory/test_your_understanding):** confirmed by code-reading `StudyMode.jsx`'s render ternary — it's a 3-way chain (`GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` branch → `!showAnswer` → `showAnswer`), and neither new type matches the graded branch, so both fall through to the exact same front/back/GradeButtonRow path `flashcard` already uses. Zero StudyMode changes needed for these two, confirming the kickoff's prediction. `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s question-type selector is now shown to ALL users (previously hidden entirely unless `canAuthorGradedTypes`) — the two free-recall additions are ungated per D-10, so only the 3 graded options within the same dropdown stay conditionally rendered. **`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`:** CSV template gains example rows for all 4 new types + updated column docs (`correct_option` semantics differ by type: 1-4 for mcq, 1-2 for true_false/correct_incorrect); parser's `question_type` recognition list extended from mcq-only to all 4; 42501 RLS-rejection error message generalized to name all 3 gated types instead of just mcq. **SQL — ✅ deployed & verified live (13/09/2026):** `00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql` run first — confirmed exactly one live `get_browsable_decks` signature and that the live `chk_flashcards_question_type`/D-10-policy definitions matched this sprint's drafted `01_SCHEMA` exactly, no adjustment needed before deploying (the introspect-before-DDL practice paying off again, though this time confirming rather than catching drift). `01_SCHEMA_add_test_your_understanding_type.sql` widened `chk_flashcards_question_type` to add `test_your_understanding` (purely additive — every existing row's type was already in the old list, so no existing row could violate the new one; no RLS change, since the type is free-recall and was never going to be in the D-10 gate's IN-list) — **deployed to production**. `02_TEST_verify_new_question_types.sql` (BEGIN/ROLLBACK, mirrors the 7.5 `02_TEST` impersonation technique) — **run against production, all 6 rows PASS**: test_your_understanding now insertable, theory unaffected, and — as a regression check, not new coverage — true_false/correct_incorrect confirmed still rejected for a real student profile and still accepted for a real professor profile (this sprint touched zero RLS, and the test proves it). **✅ Live frontend verification done this session (13/09/2026, dev server → live Supabase, real professor + student accounts):** authored one true_false/correct_incorrect/theory/test_your_understanding card manually as professor (question-type selector correctly shows all 6 types, only the 3 graded ones role-gated; card header + front label switch per type; 2-way toggle renders/saves correctly); bulk-uploaded a 6-row CSV mixing all 6 types (`back_text` derived correctly for every graded row); graded a true_false wrong (auto-hard + single Continue, ledger incremented pre-Continue) and a correct_incorrect right (reveal + full `GradeButtonRow`, no default) — the payoff, confirmed live: professor Dashboard's "Accuracy by question type" widget shows real two-measure data for all 6 types, correctly distinguishing verdict-bearing (both measures populate) from free-recall (Answer accuracy stays "No graded answers yet"); student account's question-type selector correctly shows only the 3 ungated types; Browse Study Sets filter's 6-type dropdown narrows correctly for both new graded types. Console clean throughout (one non-critical, pre-existing `admin_audit_log` 403 on bulk upload — already try/caught in code predating this sprint, unrelated to question types, not chased).

**Mid-session naming sweep (Anand's catch):** `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s page title ("Create Flashcards") and every "Create Flashcard" nav/button label sitewide still used the old singular-type name even though Sprint 7.6 already renamed the browse/list pages to "Study Sets" — inconsistent given this sprint just added the most non-flashcard types yet. Copy-only rename to **"Create Study Item"** across: page title/subtitle, `NavDesktop.jsx`/`NavMenuSheet.jsx`/`NavBottomTabs.jsx` (+ its doc-comment) nav triggers, every empty-state/quick-link button pointing at `/dashboard/flashcards/new` (`MyFlashcards.jsx`, `MyContributions.jsx` ×2, `ReviewFlashcards.jsx`, `NoteDetail.jsx`), `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`'s title/subtitle/toasts/buttons, `guideContent.js`/`helpContent.js`'s nav-instruction lines, `Home.jsx`'s public landing-page step 3, `DesignShowcase.jsx`'s /__design mockup, and `PageContainer.jsx`'s doc-comment — plus `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s own toast/validation copy ("N study item(s) created successfully", "Item N: ...", "Add Another Item", "Create N Items"). **Deliberately left alone:** `helpContent.js`'s "Creating Flashcards" section title/prose (needs a real content rewrite describing all 6 types, not a label swap) and `ProfessorTools.jsx` (dead/unrouted, per backlog). Routes, file/table/column names, and internal identifiers (`flashcards` state, `.from('flashcards')`, etc.) untouched — copy-only, same discipline as 7.6's rename. Live-verified: desktop nav, mobile ＋ sheet, and mobile Menu drawer all show "Create Study Item"; toast confirmed reading "1 study item(s) created successfully".

**Files:** *new* — `docs/database/sprint7.7/{00_DIAGNOSTIC,01_SCHEMA,02_TEST}*.sql`. *changed* — `src/lib/questionTypes.js` (`GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`, `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS`, extended `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES`), `src/pages/dashboard/Study/StudyMode.jsx` (2-line generalization), `src/pages/dashboard/Content/FlashcardCreate.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`, `src/components/layout/{NavDesktop,NavMenuSheet,NavBottomTabs,PageContainer}.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Content/{MyFlashcards,MyContributions,NoteDetail}.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Study/ReviewFlashcards.jsx`, `src/data/{guideContent,helpContent}.js`, `src/pages/Home.jsx`, `src/pages/dev/DesignShowcase.jsx`, `docs/active/blueprint.md`, `docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`, `docs/reference/FILE_STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/active/now.md`, `docs/tracking/changelog.md`.

- **Sprint 7.12 — Concept Card: structured authoring + browse-only viewer (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 13, closes the Phase 7 question-type roster:** ✅ *Code complete, zero SQL needed for the schema itself (`concept_card` was already a live CHECK-constraint value, 0 pre-existing rows) — one additive RPC migration (`get_browsable_decks` v6, DROP+CREATE for the new `has_concept_card` return column, ✅ deployed + 5/5 test PASS). LIVE-VERIFIED end to end 14/09/2026 (dev server → live Supabase, professor session, operator signed in directly — Claude never saw or entered a password). `npm run build` clean; `npx eslint` clean (same 2 pre-existing `FlashcardCreate.jsx` baseline errors reconfirmed).* The last type in the roster, and the only one that's never graded: `front_text`=heading, `back_text`=summary (typed directly, not derived), `options`=jsonb array of `{term, definition}` pairs (keyTerms, min 1) — third reuse of that column after mcq/fitb. `correct_answer`/`explanation`/`scenario`/`subtype` all stay NULL. Ungated (open to all users, like flashcard/theory) since nothing is ever graded — no authoring-judgment risk to gate against. **Real bug found and fixed, not just a new feature:** `get_study_queue` had excluded `concept_card` from the due-set since Sprint 6.0 (D-06), but `StudyMode.jsx`'s separate "never-reviewed → treat as new" fallback filter never checked `question_type`, so a never-reviewed concept card (no `reviews` row by definition) could still pass that fallback and reach a student, gradeable through the plain flashcard flip UI — the exact violation D-06 exists to prevent. Pre-flight confirmed this had never happened to real content (0 `reviews`/`review_events` rows for `concept_card` ever existed) — a live gap, not a live incident. Fixed with one added clause in the same filter; live-verified by replaying the exact fetch pipeline (deck fetch + `get_study_queue` + `reviews` + the filter) against the live DB — the test concept card (never-due, never-reviewed) was present in the deck's raw 29-card set but absent from the final 24-card filtered study list, and the study list's `question_type`s were all `flashcard`. **Browse Study Sets (`ReviewFlashcards.jsx`):** a deck tile with ≥1 visible concept_card gets a "Read Concepts" button (from `get_browsable_decks` v6's new `has_concept_card` flag) opening `ConceptCardViewer.jsx` (new) — a read-only accordion modal (heading always visible, click expands to summary + keyTerms), zero grade buttons, zero `apply_review` calls, fetched directly via the 5-column deck-membership join (D-04) scoped to `question_type='concept_card'`. **Bulk upload — explicitly deferred at the time**, same call as `match_the_following` (7.8-C): keyTerms is a variable-length list of `{term, definition}` **pairs**, not a flat list like fitb's acceptable-answers — a second-level CSV-cell delimiter would silently mis-split real CA content (confirmed live: the verification card's own definition text contained a colon). **[Superseded — built in Sprint 8.6b via a grouped-row CSV format, see that entry above.]** **`MyFlashcards.jsx` needed no change** — its generic `FlashcardCard` tile already renders `front_text`/`back_text` for any question type with no type-specific logic (true for every other rich type too), so a concept-card batch already displays correctly there for content management; Browse Study Sets is the sole student-facing read surface, a deliberate choice not a default. Live verification: authored a real 2-keyTerm concept card (Auditing & Ethics → Audit Evidence, same topic as 28 pre-existing gradeable cards) — "Study" served only the 24 due/new gradeable cards, "Read Concepts" opened correctly with both keyTerms rendered, zero `reviews` rows ever created for the concept card (confirmed via direct REST read, before and after all testing), QA row deleted after verification (operator confirmed).
- **Sprint 7.11 — Fill in the Blank (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 12:** ✅ *Code complete, zero SQL needed (fitb was already a live CHECK-constraint value and already in the D-10 RESTRICTIVE gate's IN-list since Sprint 7.5), LIVE-VERIFIED end to end 14/09/2026 (dev server → live Supabase, professor session already authenticated in the driven browser — Claude never saw or entered a password). `npm run build` clean; `npx eslint` clean on every changed file (same 2 pre-existing `FlashcardCreate.jsx` baseline errors + 1 warning reconfirmed).* The last of the 5 D-10-gated types to get a real authoring path. Its whole point (D-13) is a confidence-gated three-way verdict, not MCQ's binary one: the author supplies 1+ acceptable-answer strings (repurposing `options`, generalized per D-13's impact line); a student's typed answer, normalized identically on both sides (trim/lowercase/strip punctuation/collapse whitespace, `src/lib/fitb.js` `normalizeFitbAnswer`) via one shared function used at both authoring-compaction and grading time, is checked against every accepted variant. **Match** → confident-correct, reveal + `GradeButtonRow`, `apply_review(p_is_correct=true)`. **No match** → never auto-marked wrong — falls back to the exact free-recall UX (reveal canonical answer + all accepted variants, full self-grade), `apply_review(p_is_correct=null)`. There is no third "confident-wrong" state; no code path for fitb ever passes `is_correct=false` to `apply_review`. `correct_answer` stays NULL (same pattern as `match_the_following`). **Representation (`src/lib/fitb.js`, new):** `FITB_BLANK_PATTERN`/`FITB_BLANK_PLACEHOLDER` (`______`, 3+ underscores) validate the authored sentence contains a visible blank; `normalizeFitbAnswer`/`isFitbMatch`/`deriveFitbBackText` (first accepted answer)/`validateFitbBlank`/`validateFitbOptions`/`compactFitbOptions`/`splitFitbSentence` (renders the sentence around an inline `<input>` in StudyMode). **StudyMode.jsx** gets its own render branch (checked before `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`, same reasoning as `match_the_following` in 7.8 — the verdict isn't the clean binary that array assumes): sentence with an inline text input where the blank goes, a Submit action running the match logic, then either grading path converging on the same unmodified `GradeButtonRow`. **`FlashcardCreate.jsx`:** new "Fill in the Blank" type (professor/admin/super_admin, D-10) — sentence-with-blank textarea (blank-marker validated at submit time) + a repeatable 1-6-row acceptable-answers list editor + Why textarea. **`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`:** new `fitb_answers` CSV column, semicolon-delimited within one cell (no case_study_mcq-style multi-row fan-out needed, so no reason to defer this like 7.8-C did for match_the_following). **Task 0 (7.10 gap-check, done first):** uploaded a real 3-row/1-`case_group` `case_study_mcq` CSV through `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` as a professor — confirmed live: all 3 rows shared one `batch_id`, one `scenario` (byte-identical), correct per-row `correct_answer`/`back_text`. Found and fixed one unrelated pre-existing template bug along the way: the `descriptive_case_study` example row's `back` field had an unquoted comma, corrupting that row's CSV column count — quoted it. **Live verification:** authored a real fitb card (Income Tax → Overview and Basic Concepts, "The basic exemption limit for individuals below 60 years is ______." / accepted: "₹2.5 lakhs", "2.5 lakhs") and studied it twice. **Match path:** typed "2.5 LAKHS!!" → revealed navy/matched, graded Easy → `reviews.rung` advanced normally (0→2, 7-day interval) and `get_question_type_performance` showed `fitb: graded_count=1, answer_accuracy_pct=100`. **No-match path** (after `reset_card`): typed "Rs 3 lakhs" → revealed the "ACCEPTED ANSWERS" box (not navy) + full self-grade, graded Medium → `reviews.rung` still advanced normally (0→1, 3-day interval, not forced to rung 0) and the analytics aggregate's math (`graded_count=1` even after 2 total reviews, both counting the FIRST review's is_correct=true — a null-`is_correct` event is invisible to `graded_agg`, exactly as `answer_accuracy_pct` is documented to undercount fitb, D-13) is only consistent with the second event's `is_correct` being NULL, not `false` — confirming no confident-wrong path exists. All 4 QA rows (3 case_study_mcq + 1 fitb) deleted after verification (operator confirmed via AskUserQuestion).

- **Sprint 7.8 — Match the Following (Sep 2026) — Phase 7, sprint 9:** ✅ *Code complete + SQL confirmed + LIVE-VERIFIED end to end 14/09/2026 (dev server → live Supabase, real professor + student accounts, account owner typing credentials directly into the driven browser — Claude never saw or entered a password). `npm run build` clean; `npx eslint` clean on every changed file (the pre-existing `FlashcardCreate.jsx` `showCustomSubject`/`showCustomTopic`/eslint-disable baseline issues reconfirmed via `git stash` to predate this sprint). **SQL pre-flight ✅ run by the operator, zero SQL needed** (`docs/database/sprint7.8/00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql`) — this session only has the anon key (`.env.local`), no service-role/DB connection string, so the operator ran it in the Supabase SQL Editor and shared the result back. **Ready to commit.*** The first genuinely new interaction mechanic since mcq — left-item/right-item pairing, not a collapse into the shared mcq/true_false/correct_incorrect machinery like 7.7's types were. Verdict is still deterministic (fully correct or not), so the hybrid-grading contract is unchanged; `apply_review`/`review_events`/both analytics RPCs needed zero changes — confirmed both by re-reading their bodies AND live (professor's "Accuracy by question type" and student's "Performance by Question Type" widgets both show real `match_the_following` data with the exact expected math: 1 wrong + 1 correct grade → 50%/50%).
  - **7.8-A representation (`options` jsonb, locked):** deliberately different shape from mcq's flat array — `{ left: string[], right: {k,v}[], correct: {[leftIndex]: k} }`, correct mapping colocated inside `options` rather than double-encoded into `correct_answer` (which stays NULL for this type — there's no single scalar "the answer"). `back_text` auto-derived as a flattened `"left — key"` summary. Left/right lists locked to the same length this sprint (no distractor right-options). New `src/lib/matchTheFollowing.js`: `keyForRightIndex()` (auto-generates A/B/C... by right-list position, never professor-typed), `buildMatchOptions()`, `deriveMatchBackText()`, `validateMatchPairs()` — same shared-helper pattern as `src/lib/mcq.js`.
  - **7.8-B authoring (`FlashcardCreate.jsx`, professor/admin/super_admin only):** new "Match the following" entry in the question-type selector (role-gated identically to mcq/true_false/correct_incorrect). Left-item and right-item lists are edited as two independent add/remove editors (not paired rows) — removing a right item clears or reindexes any correct-mapping entries that pointed at it or past it. A third "Correct Mapping" section renders one `<Select>` per left item, listing the current right items by their live-computed letter. `validateMatchPairs()` enforces equal-length lists + a complete mapping before save; front-side label reads "Instructions" for this type (vs. "Question"/"Statement" for mcq/two-way-verdict). Draft autosave/restore extended to carry `matchLeft`/`matchRight`/`matchCorrect`; the draft-restore role-downgrade check (previously keyed only on `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`) now also catches `match_the_following` via a local `isD10GatedType()` helper, since this type is D-10-gated but deliberately not in that array (see below).
  - **7.8-C bulk upload — explicitly deferred, not built this sprint:** the variable-length left/right/mapping shape doesn't fit the flat `option_1..4`/`correct_option` CSV convention mcq/true_false/correct_incorrect share, and a matching-question set is lower-volume, more bespoke authoring than a big MCQ CSV. `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` untouched.
  - **7.8-D StudyMode rendering + hybrid grading — genuinely new render branch, not a `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` extension:** new `src/components/revisop/MatchZone.jsx` (the one revisop primitive actually wired into a production page, unlike its `/__design`-only siblings) implements the pick-a-left-row/assign-a-right-badge interaction — tapping an already-assigned left row un-assigns it for reassignment, and assigning a right badge already used elsewhere un-assigns it from its previous row. Because the answer builds up across multiple taps (unlike MCQ/true_false's single tap), there's no implicit completion moment — a "Check Answers" button, enabled only once every left row has an assignment, is the explicit commit action. `StudyMode.jsx` gets a new top-level branch (`currentCard.question_type === 'match_the_following'`, checked *before* the existing `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES.includes(...)` branch) with its own `matchPairs`/`matchRevealed`/`matchIsCorrect` state (reset every card change, same pattern as `mcqSelectedIndex`/`mcqIsCorrect`). On submit: every row reveals its own correct/incorrect state via `MatchZone`'s `correct` prop — navy check / slate cross + a "Correct answer: K — text" hint on incorrect rows, following `AnswerOption`'s established never-red/green convention — **regardless of the overall verdict**, since per-row feedback has pedagogical value independent of what happens next. Overall verdict is **all-or-nothing** (every row must match; 3 of 4 correct still grades as wrong) — a deliberate simplification, not an oversight; no partial-credit threshold or scheduling nuance this sprint. Hybrid grading is the unchanged contract: wrong → immediate `apply_review('hard', false)` on the Check-Answers tap + a single Continue button (mirrors MCQ's reveal-time auto-submit); fully correct → reveal + the existing `GradeButtonRow`, no pre-selected default. `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` (`src/lib/questionTypes.js`) deliberately does NOT gain `match_the_following` — that array specifically drives the shared single-tap `AnswerOption`-list branch, which this type does not use.
  - **`BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` extended** to include `match_the_following` (one-line addition, `src/lib/questionTypes.js`) — Browse Study Sets' Question Type filter now offers it. `formatQuestionType()` already had the `'Match the following'` label from Sprint 6's original 10-type documentation pass, so no change needed there.
  - **D-10 (§3.1) — ✅ re-verified live 14/09/2026, zero SQL needed.** `docs/database/sprint7.8/00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql` run by the operator in the Supabase SQL Editor (this session has no direct DB credential to run it itself): live `pg_get_constraintdef(chk_flashcards_question_type)` includes `'match_the_following'::text` exactly, and both RESTRICTIVE policies' `pg_get_expr(polwithcheck, ...)` list it exactly in their `<> ALL (ARRAY[...])` clause — the exact same introspect-before-DDL discipline that caught the `fitb`-vs-`fill_in_the_blanks` drift in Sprint 7.5, this time confirming rather than catching drift. Zero pre-existing `match_the_following` rows. **Real finding: this sprint needed zero SQL**, matching true_false/correct_incorrect's outcome in 7.7.
  - **✅ Live verification DONE 14/09/2026** (dev server → live Supabase, real professor + student accounts, joint session — operator typed credentials, Claude drove the rest): authored a 4-pair card + a 3-pair card as professor (`back_text` derivation confirmed exact, letter auto-generation confirmed, independent left/right add/remove editors confirmed including mid-authoring row removal); graded the wrong path (2-of-4 rows deliberately swapped — `apply_review('hard', false)` confirmed firing on Check Answers via the session ledger incrementing before Continue, per-row reveal confirmed showing correct/incorrect simultaneously regardless of overall verdict); graded the correct path (all rows matched — `GradeButtonRow` confirmed with real interval previews, no pre-selected default, tap-to-grade-and-advance confirmed); confirmed the due-filter correctly excluded the already-graded card on re-entry (zero special-casing needed in `get_study_queue`); confirmed the student Create page shows no leak of any D-10-gated type; confirmed Browse Study Sets' Question Type filter narrows correctly; confirmed both analytics widgets show real two-measure data. Console clean.
  - **Naming follow-up (Anand's catch, same session):** the Create Study Item page's per-block heading read just `"Flashcard 1"` (or `"MCQ 1"`, etc.) — confirmed this doesn't rename the `formatQuestionType()` type labels themselves (Question Type dropdown / Progress / Dashboard / Browse-filter all keep "Flashcard" as the correct name for that one item type), only made the per-block heading pattern read more explicitly as an item count: `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s `<CardTitle>` → **`Item {index + 1} — {formatQuestionType(...)}`** (e.g. "Item 1 — Flashcard", "Item 1 — Match the following"). Live-verified both states.
  - **Real bug found and fixed along the way (not originally in scope, low-risk one-line fix, same pattern as Sprint 7.5's Dashboard.jsx bonus fix):** `Progress.jsx` had its own stale, independently-maintained question-type label map (`QT_LABELS`, keys `match`/`fill_blank`/`short_answer`/`case_study` — none ever real CHECK-constraint values) instead of the shared `formatQuestionType()` `src/lib/questionTypes.js` already exports for exactly this purpose — live-observed rendering the new `match_the_following` row as its raw db key. Fixed by deleting the local map and delegating to the shared helper; re-verified live, `npx eslint` clean.
  - **Files:** *new* — `src/lib/matchTheFollowing.js`, `src/components/revisop/MatchZone.jsx`, `docs/database/sprint7.8/00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql`. *changed* — `src/lib/questionTypes.js`, `src/components/revisop/index.js`, `src/pages/dashboard/Study/StudyMode.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Content/FlashcardCreate.jsx`, `src/pages/dashboard/Study/Progress.jsx` (bonus fix), `docs/reference/{DATABASE_SCHEMA,FILE_STRUCTURE}.md`, `docs/active/blueprint.md`. **Not yet done:** git commit + push (operator action, per this sprint's own MANDATORY checklist).

## Part 1 — Revised Blueprint (Current Truth)

### 1.1 Database Schema

**Total tables: 29 (was 30 — `comments` dropped 02/07/2026, L1) | Views: 0 (`vw_study_items` dropped 02/07/2026, L1) | RLS: enabled on all 29 tables | Triggers: 18 in public schema + 2 on `auth.users` | Functions: 90+ (7 dead trigger-fns dropped 02/07/2026, L1)**

> **Verified against live DB (Jun 29, 2026)** via `information_schema` / `pg_catalog` introspection. Counts and column lists below reflect actual database state, not intent. Known DB-level problems (duplicate triggers, dead functions, load-bearing legacy columns) are catalogued in **§1.11 DB Cleanup Backlog & Landmines** — read it before any pivot migration.

---

#### profiles
User accounts with 4-tier role system. Created by `handle_new_user()` trigger on `auth.users` — never by client INSERT (email confirmation = no session = RLS blocks client insert).

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | Matches auth.users.id |
| full_name | text | Display name |
| email | text UNIQUE | From Supabase auth |
| role | text NOT NULL | student / professor / admin / super_admin |
| course_level | text | User's primary enrollment (CA Foundation/Inter/Final) |
| institution | text | Coaching institute name |
| account_type | text NOT NULL | self_registered (B2C) or enrolled (B2B) |
| status | text NOT NULL | active / suspended |
| has_seen_onboarding | boolean | Controls first-login modal |
| timezone | text | IANA timezone, auto-detected on login |
| daily_review_goal | integer | CHECK >0 AND <=200; NULL = no goal |
| daily_study_goal_minutes | integer | CHECK >0 AND <=480; NULL = no goal |
| has_dismissed_goal_prompt | boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false | Sprint 7.3-B — one-time dismissal of the dashboard "no goal set" prompt line |
| access_request_ref | uuid | B2B lead linkage; set by `link_access_request` RPC (was undocumented) |
| created_at | timestamptz | Account creation |
| updated_at | timestamptz | Last profile update (was undocumented) |

**Key distinction:** `role` = permission level; `account_type` = business relationship (enrolled users see professor public content, self_registered see only their own).

---

#### notes
User-uploaded study notes with optional image and OCR text.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| user_id | uuid FK→profiles | Uploader |
| contributed_by | uuid FK→profiles | Professor attribution (separate from uploader) |
| target_course | text | Who this content is FOR (nullable in DB) |
| discipline_id | uuid FK→disciplines | |
| subject_id | uuid FK→subjects | |
| topic_id | uuid FK→topics | |
| custom_subject | text | Free-text for non-taxonomy courses |
| custom_topic | text | Free-text for non-taxonomy courses |
| title | text NOT NULL | |
| description | text | Free-text description (was undocumented) |
| content_type | text | Text / Table / Math / Diagram |
| image_url | text | Supabase Storage URL |
| extracted_text | text | OCR output |
| tags | text[] | #important, #revision |
| ~~is_public~~ | ~~boolean~~ | ✅ **DROPPED (03/07/2026, L2)** — public-read RLS re-keyed onto `visibility`. See §1.11 #2. |
| visibility | text | private / friends / public (default 'private'). **Sole basis of all read RLS** (public + friends tiers) as of 03/07/2026. |
| view_count | integer | Engagement metric |
| upvote_count | integer | Auto-maintained by `trigger_update_upvote_counts` |
| course / subject / topic | text | ⚠️ Legacy free-text columns, superseded by FK + custom_* (still present in DB). See §1.11. |
| created_at | timestamptz | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | |

> **⚠️ Correction:** the blueprint previously listed a `notes.is_verified` column — **it does not exist in the database.** Removed.

---

#### flashcards
Spaced repetition cards. `deck_id` column exists but is NEVER populated — do NOT filter by it. Use the 5-column join to associate cards with decks.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| user_id | uuid FK→profiles | Owner |
| contributed_by | uuid FK→profiles | Professor attribution |
| creator_id | uuid FK→profiles | Who uploaded (operational) |
| content_creator_id | uuid FK→content_creators | Who gets revenue (financial) |
| target_course | text NOT NULL | Who this content is FOR |
| note_id | uuid FK→notes | Optional source note |
| front_text | text NOT NULL | Question side |
| back_text | text NOT NULL | Answer side |
| front_image_url | text | Optional image |
| back_image_url | text | Optional image |
| discipline_id | uuid FK→disciplines | |
| subject_id | uuid FK→subjects | |
| topic_id | uuid FK→topics | |
| custom_subject | text | Free-text; mutually exclusive with subject_id |
| custom_topic | text | Free-text; mutually exclusive with topic_id |
| batch_id | uuid NOT NULL | Groups cards from same upload (CRITICAL for display) |
| batch_description | text | Optional batch label |
| is_verified | boolean | |
| difficulty | text | easy / medium / hard |
| visibility | text NOT NULL | private / friends / public |
| question_type | text NOT NULL | **As of Sprint 7.9 (see D-10/D-12/D-14 §3.1):** flashcard / mcq / correct_incorrect / theory / case_study_mcq / match_the_following / fitb / concept_card (8 values). Live-introspected 13/09/2026 (Sprint 7.5 `00_DIAGNOSTIC`) as 10 values, `test_your_understanding` added 13/09/2026 (Sprint 7.7), then **Sprint 7.9 (14/09/2026) dropped `test_your_understanding` (collapsed into `theory`+`subtype`, D-10 correction), `integrated_case` (never had an authoring path, D-12), and `true_false` (merged into `correct_incorrect`, D-14 — CA Revision Portal's own schema documents them as functionally identical) from the live `chk_flashcards_question_type` CHECK constraint** — `docs/database/sprint7.9/{01_SCHEMA_sprint7.9_hygiene,04_SCHEMA_true_false_removal}.sql`. None of the three is insertable any more. `fill_in_the_blanks` is not and was never a live value — the live value is `fitb`. |
| options | jsonb | Answer options for MCQ-type questions |
| correct_answer | text | For question types that need it |
| hints | jsonb | Optional hints array |
| points_to_remember | jsonb | Key takeaways shown post-answer, **free-recall types only as of Sprint 7.9** (`flashcard`/`theory`/`concept_card`) — see `explanation` below for the split-off graded-type sibling. |
| explanation | jsonb | **New, Sprint 7.9 (14/09/2026).** Grading-rationale text ("Why") for graded types — `mcq`/`correct_incorrect`/`match_the_following` write here instead of `points_to_remember` now (same shape: jsonb array of strings, one per non-blank line). Split out because the two concepts (free-recall "key takeaways" vs graded "why this is the answer") were sharing one column since Sprint 7.5/7.8. Nullable, additive — no backfill needed, the only rows that had graded-type `points_to_remember` data pre-migration were QA/testing artifacts, deleted in the same migration. `StudyMode.jsx`'s post-reveal WHY block reads this column for `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` and `match_the_following`. |
| scenario | text | Case study / scenario text |
| subtype | text | Sub-classification within question_type. **Activated Sprint 7.9 (14/09/2026)** — previously declared but genuinely unused (0 non-null rows, confirmed via pre-flight). Now required for `question_type='theory'`: `pure_theory` \| `descriptive_case_study`, mirroring the CA Revision Portal's own D14. Classification metadata only — does not change `StudyMode.jsx` rendering. |
| source | text NOT NULL | manual / bulk_upload / gemini_import |
| next_review | timestamptz | ⚠️ VESTIGIAL — written at create, never read (see below) |
| interval | integer | ⚠️ VESTIGIAL — written as `1` at create, never read |
| ease_factor | numeric | ⚠️ VESTIGIAL — written as `2.5` at create, never read |
| repetitions | integer | ⚠️ VESTIGIAL — written as `0` at create, never read |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

**Critical rules:**
- Group by `batch_id`, NEVER by timestamp
- `concept_card` items excluded from all review metrics (Items Reviewed, streak, accuracy)
- To fetch cards for a deck: join on 5 columns (user_id, subject_id, topic_id, custom_subject, custom_topic) — NOT by deck_id

**MCQ representation (Sprint 7.5, 13/09/2026 — SQL pending, see D-10 §3.1):** `question_type='mcq'` uses only existing columns, no schema change. `options` = jsonb array of option strings (2-6, default 4 in the create form). `correct_answer` = text, the **0-based INDEX** of the correct option as a string (`"0".."5"`) — not the option text, not a letter; index-based because two options can have identical text. `back_text` (NOT NULL) is never typed directly for mcq — it's auto-derived as `options[correct_answer]` at save time, keeping it a sane fallback for any generic code that reads `back_text` without knowing about `question_type`. `explanation` = jsonb array (one entry per non-blank line of the "Why" textarea) — shown post-reveal (**was `points_to_remember` until Sprint 7.9's column split, see §1.1 flashcards `explanation` row**). `hints`/`scenario`/`subtype` stay NULL for this type. Confirmed zero pre-existing `question_type='mcq'` rows before building (no authoring path had ever existed).

**case_study_mcq representation (Sprint 7.10, 14/09/2026 — ✅ SHIPPED, zero SQL needed):** a shared scenario narrative followed by one or more independently-gradeable mcq questions. Each question is its **own `flashcards` row** — own SRS card, own rung, reviewed on its own schedule — sharing one `batch_id` (D-01 pattern) and one `subject_id`/`topic_id` (single anchor topic per case, D-12). Per-question fields are identical to mcq's representation above (`options`/`correct_answer`/`back_text`/`explanation`). `scenario` (activated this sprint — 0 non-null rows before it, confirmed via `00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql`) is duplicated verbatim across every row in the case, not normalized into a separate table, so it renders on any review day for any question without a JOIN — the acceptable tradeoff being N-way text duplication for a short/medium narrative. Browse Study Sets groups a case's rows into one deck card for free via the existing 5-column join (no new grouping code). StudyMode renders through the **same shared `AnswerOption`/hybrid-grading branch as mcq** (`case_study_mcq` added to `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`, not a new render branch) — the only addition is a collapsible "CASE SCENARIO" block above the question, open by default. Authoring (`FlashcardCreate.jsx`): one shared Scenario textarea + 2-8 repeatable mcq-shaped question blocks (`src/lib/caseStudyMcq.js`), fanning out to N INSERTs sharing the case's `batch_id` on save. Bulk upload (`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`) built, not deferred — a `scenario` + `case_group` column pair lets a flat CSV group rows into a case, unlike match_the_following's variable-length shape (7.8-C) which didn't fit the flat convention (match_the_following's own bulk upload later shipped in Sprint 8.6b, via a grouped-row rather than flat-row CSV shape — see that entry). See `docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`'s case_study_mcq Representation section for full detail.

**fitb representation (Sprint 7.11, 14/09/2026 — ✅ SHIPPED, zero SQL needed):** confidence-gated verdict, not a binary one (D-13) — the only question type whose `is_correct` can be `true` or `NULL` but never `false`. `options` = jsonb array of acceptable-answer strings (1-6), reusing the same column mcq/case_study_mcq use with a generalized meaning. `front_text` holds the blank-containing sentence (validated at authoring time to contain a blank marker, `______`/3+ underscores). `correct_answer` stays NULL, same pattern as `match_the_following` — there's no single scalar "the answer," the verdict is computed at grading time by normalizing the student's typed answer (`src/lib/fitb.js` `normalizeFitbAnswer` — trim/lowercase/strip punctuation/collapse whitespace) and checking it against every normalized `options` entry, the exact same function called on both sides of the comparison. `back_text` (NOT NULL) auto-derived as the first accepted answer. `explanation` = jsonb array, same shape/convention as every other graded type since Sprint 7.9. StudyMode.jsx renders through its own branch (not `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` — the verdict isn't MCQ's clean binary), converging either path (match or no-match) on the same unmodified `GradeButtonRow`.

**concept_card representation (Sprint 7.12, 14/09/2026 — ✅ SHIPPED, last type in the Phase 7 roster):** browse-only reference material — no grade, no rung, no `reviews` row, ever (D-06). `front_text` = heading (concept name). `back_text` (NOT NULL) = summary, typed directly like a plain flashcard's back — not derived. `options` = jsonb array of `{term, definition}` objects (keyTerms, min 1) — third reuse of that column after mcq (7.5) and fitb (7.11), this time as object pairs rather than scalar strings. `correct_answer`/`hints`/`scenario`/`subtype`/`explanation` all stay NULL. Zero pre-existing rows confirmed via pre-flight before this sprint (never properly built before). Open to all users (like `flashcard`/`theory`), not D-10-gated — authoring judgment isn't the risk here since nothing is ever graded. `src/lib/conceptCard.js` holds `buildConceptOptions`/`validateConceptTerms`. Bulk upload (`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`) explicitly deferred at the time, same call as `match_the_following` (7.8-C) — keyTerms is a variable-length list of `{term, definition}` **pairs**, not a flat list like fitb's acceptable-answers; a second-level CSV-cell delimiter would silently mis-split real content (CA terminology routinely contains colons/commas of its own — confirmed live: the verification card's own definition read "Measure of quality: relevance + reliability..."). **[Superseded — built in Sprint 8.6b via a grouped-row CSV format (`concept_group`/`concept_term`/`concept_definition` columns), see `docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`'s concept_card Representation section.]** **The StudyMode leak this sprint closed:** `get_study_queue` already excluded `concept_card` from the due-set since Sprint 6.0, but `StudyMode.jsx`'s "never-reviewed → treat as new" fallback filter did not check `question_type`, so a never-reviewed concept card (which by definition has no `reviews` row) passed that fallback and could reach a student in a live study session, gradeable via the plain flashcard flip UI. Fixed by adding an explicit `question_type !== 'concept_card'` exclusion to that fallback filter — live-verified via a full pipeline replay against the live DB (deck fetch + `get_study_queue` + `reviews` + the fixed filter): the test concept card (never-due, never-reviewed — exactly the leak condition) was confirmed present in the deck's raw card set but absent from the final filtered study list. `ReviewFlashcards.jsx` (Browse Study Sets) gained a "Read Concepts" action on any deck tile with at least one visible concept_card (`get_browsable_decks` v6's new `has_concept_card` flag, computed in the same visibility-filtered lateral as `visible_card_count`) — opens `src/components/flashcards/ConceptCardViewer.jsx`, a read-only accordion (heading always visible, click expands to summary + keyTerms) that queries `flashcards` directly (5-column join, D-04) scoped to `question_type='concept_card'`. No `apply_review` call anywhere in that path. `MyFlashcards.jsx` needed no change — its generic `FlashcardCard` tile already shows `front_text`/`back_text` for every question type without type-specific rendering (true for mcq/fitb/etc. too), so a concept-card batch already displays correctly there for content management; Browse Study Sets is the sole student-facing read surface.

**✅ RESOLVED — vestigial SRS columns.** `next_review`/`interval`/`ease_factor`/`repetitions` were dropped from `flashcards` 02/07/2026 (Landmine L1, `05_SCHEMA`); `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s hardcoded write block was stripped in commit `921280b`. **This paragraph previously claimed FlashcardCreate.jsx still wrote them at ~line 468 — stale; re-read during Sprint 7.5 (13/09/2026) confirms the current INSERT has no such fields**, matching `DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`'s flashcards section, which already had this correctly marked resolved. All live SRS scheduling reads from `reviews` (`reviews.next_review_date`/`easiness`/`repetition`), unchanged.

---

#### reviews
Single source of truth for all spaced repetition state (SuperMemo-2 algorithm).

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| user_id | uuid FK→profiles | |
| flashcard_id | uuid FK→flashcards | |
| quality | integer | 1=Hard, 3=Medium, 5=Easy |
| easiness | double precision | SuperMemo-2 EF value (default 2.5) — NOT `easiness_factor`, and the type is `double precision` (float8), NOT numeric |
| interval | integer | Days until next review |
| repetition | integer | Consecutive correct reviews — NOT repetitions |
| next_review_date | date | ⚠️ DATE (not timestamptz) — stored as `YYYY-MM-DD` local-date string. `StudyMode.jsx` builds it from local Y/M/D (~line 289) and compares as a string. Treating it as a timestamp will cause "wrong day" bugs. |
| last_reviewed_at | timestamptz | Timestamp of most recent rating |
| status | text | active / suspended |
| skip_until | date | Card hidden until this date (24h skip) |
| created_at | timestamptz | Used for streak calc — NOT reviewed_at |

**UNIQUE(user_id, flashcard_id)** — one review record per user per card.

---

#### review_events (Sprint 7.4, 13/09/2026)
Append-only per-review history, alongside `reviews` (which stays the current-state SSOT — unchanged shape/semantics, still the table `get_study_queue`/streak/heatmap read). One row per grade, ever, written atomically with the `reviews` write inside `apply_review`. No synthetic backfill — history starts at deploy (13/09/2026). RLS enabled, zero policies, zero grants (`REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated`) — every access is through a SECURITY DEFINER RPC.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | bigint PK (GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY) | |
| user_id | uuid FK→auth.users ON DELETE CASCADE | **Not** `profiles(id)` — matches what `reviews.user_id` itself actually references (confirmed live, not assumed) |
| flashcard_id | uuid FK→flashcards ON DELETE CASCADE | |
| reviewed_at | timestamptz | Default now() |
| rating | text | hard / medium / easy |
| is_correct | boolean, nullable | NULL = no deterministic verdict (self-graded flashcard/theory/etc.) — populated only once Sprint 7.5's graded question types exist |
| question_type | text | Snapshot at review time |
| topic_id | uuid, nullable | Snapshot; NULL for custom_topic cards |
| rung_before | smallint, nullable | NULL = card was new (no prior ladder position) |
| rung_after | smallint | |
| status_after | text | active / suspended / mastered |
| interval_days | integer | |
| next_review_date | date | |
| source | text, nullable | NULL \| 'review_session' \| 'new_card' \| 'exam_final_pass' |
| study_session_id | uuid, nullable | Reserved — stays NULL, session-logging rework is a later sprint |

**Indexes:** `(user_id, reviewed_at DESC)`, `(flashcard_id, reviewed_at DESC)`.
**Measured cost (13/09/2026, live):** ~142 bytes/row (`pg_column_size`); no near-term storage concern against the Free-plan limit at current/projected volume — see changelog Sprint 7.4 entry for the full projection. Re-measure after real organic (non-test) volume accumulates.

#### disciplines / subjects / topics
Course taxonomy. Pre-loaded with CA Intermediate data (147 topics across 8 subjects). Course-agnostic by design; CA/CMA/CS is current beta content only.

All three tables use `order_num` (NOT `sort_order`). disciplines and subjects both have a secondary `order` column.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| name | text NOT NULL | |
| code | text NOT NULL | disciplines only; no default — must be provided |
| discipline_id / subject_id | uuid FK | Parent reference |
| order_num | integer | Display order |
| is_active | boolean | Enable/disable |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

#### study_groups
Group metadata. Any group can be joined via `/join/:invite_token`.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| name | text NOT NULL | |
| description | text | |
| created_by | uuid FK→profiles ON DELETE CASCADE | |
| is_batch_group | boolean | True = official B2B group; hides Leave/Delete for members |
| batch_course | text | For batch isolation |
| batch_institution | text | For batch isolation |

| created_at | timestamptz | |
| updated_at | timestamptz | |

| archived_by | uuid FK→profiles ON DELETE SET NULL | Sprint 8.1 (D-16). Admin who archived; cleared on restore. |

#### study_group_members

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| group_id | uuid FK→study_groups ON DELETE CASCADE | |
| user_id | uuid FK→profiles ON DELETE CASCADE | |
| role | text | admin / member |
| status | text | invited / active / requested / closed. **`requested`** added Sprint 8.0 (D-15) — a student's own self-request via a batch invite link, awaiting admin approval; opposite direction from `invited` (staff-initiated, awaiting the student's accept). **`closed`** added Sprint 8.1 (D-16) — an outstanding `requested`/`invited` row closed out by `archive_batch_group`, not deleted; `join_group_by_token` reactivates a `closed` row back to `requested` on an explicit re-request after restore. Content-access RPCs treat anything but `active` as no access. |
| invited_by | uuid FK→profiles ON DELETE SET NULL | |
| joined_at | timestamptz | Updated to NOW() on accept |
| closed_at | timestamptz | Sprint 8.1 (D-16). Set when status → `closed`. |
| closed_reason | text | Sprint 8.1 (D-16). e.g. `'batch_archived'`. |

**UNIQUE(group_id, user_id)**

#### batch_group_archives
Sprint 8.1 (D-16). One row per archive event — a restore-then-re-archive cycle adds a new row rather than overwriting, so earlier snapshots remain as historical records. No client-facing RLS policies; reads only through `get_batch_group_archive()`.

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| id | uuid PK | |
| group_id | uuid FK→study_groups ON DELETE CASCADE | |
| archived_at | timestamptz NOT NULL | Matches `study_groups.archived_at` at the moment of this archive event |
| archived_by | uuid FK→profiles ON DELETE SET NULL | |
| report | jsonb NOT NULL | `{ group: {name, description, batch_course, batch_institution}, member_count, members: [...get_batch_group_member_stats rows] }` |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

**UNIQUE(group_id, archived_at)**

#### badge_definitions / user_badges / user_activity_log / user_stats

**badge_definitions:** Catalogue of achievement badges (key, name, icon_key, category, threshold, order_num, is_active).

**user_badges:** Awarded badges per user (user_id, badge_id, earned_at, notified, is_public). **UNIQUE(user_id, badge_id)**. Per-badge privacy toggle.

**user_activity_log:** Daily activity log for streak and badge calculations (user_id, activity_type, activity_date, activity_hour). **UNIQUE(user_id, activity_type, activity_date)**.

**user_stats:** Counter table for O(1) badge eligibility checks — maintained by `trg_aaa_*` triggers (fires before `trg_badge_*` alphabetically).

### 1.2 Migration History

No formal migration files exist (direct Supabase SQL editor). Milestones by sprint:

| Sprint | Date | Changes |
|--------|------|---------|
| 0 — MVP | Dec 2025 | Core tables: profiles, notes, flashcards, reviews, disciplines/subjects/topics, comments, upvotes, role_permissions. Basic CRUD RLS. |
| 0 — Batch | Dec 26, 2025 | batch_id + batch_description on flashcards. Back-filled 52 existing cards. |
| 0 — Super Admin RLS | Jan 2, 2026 | super_admin SELECT policies on profiles, notes, flashcards, reviews. Fixed empty admin dashboard. |
| 1 — Attribution | Jan 9, 2026 | creator_id on flashcards. content_creators table. friendships table. |
| 1 — Visibility | Jan 11, 2026 | visibility column replaces is_public on notes (16 rows migrated) and flashcards (567 rows migrated). |
| 1 — Decks & Upvotes | Jan 24, 2026 | flashcard_decks table. Polymorphic upvotes (content_type + target_id). update_deck_card_count trigger. |
| 1 — Badges | Jan 26, 2026 | badge_definitions, user_badges, user_activity_log, user_stats. trg_badge_* + trg_aaa_counter_* triggers. |
| 2 — Groups | Feb 6, 2026 | study_groups, study_group_members, content_group_shares. 14 group RPCs. |
| 2 — Notifications | Feb 6, 2026 | notifications table (pre-existing columns + metadata JSONB + title + type expansion). |
| 2 — Card Suspension | Feb 6, 2026 | status + skip_until on reviews. Partial composite index. |
| 2 — Flags | Mar 2026 | content_flags table. submit_content_flag, get_my_content_flags, get_admin_flags, resolve_content_flag RPCs. |
| 3 — Sessions | Mar 2026 | study_sessions table. get_study_time_stats RPC. |
| 3 — Groups v2 | Mar 19, 2026 | invite_token, group_type, linked_course on study_groups. handle_new_user trigger for profile creation. |
| 3 — Goals | Mar 22, 2026 | daily_review_goal + daily_study_goal_minutes on profiles. update_daily_goal RPC. |
| 3 — Follows | Sprint 3.4 | follows table. follow_user, unfollow_user, get_follow_status, get_following_with_stats RPCs. |
| 3 — Leaderboard | Sprint 3.5 | get_friends_leaderboard, get_following_leaderboard RPCs. |
| 6 — Question Types | Mar 2026 | 10 content-type columns on flashcards now documented: question_type, options, correct_answer, hints, points_to_remember, scenario, subtype, source, custom_subject, custom_topic. |

### 1.3 Storage Buckets

| Bucket | Access | Notes |
|--------|--------|-------|
| `notes` | Public read | Note images. Compressed at upload: max 500 KB / 1920px. **16/09/2026:** note deletion (`MyNotes.jsx`, `AdminDashboard.jsx`) now removes the Storage object via `deleteNoteStorageImage()` — previously the DB row delete left the image orphaned forever (no trigger covered it either). Found via a manual storage-vs-`image_url` audit; 8 pre-existing orphans (~47MB) required a manual Dashboard cleanup since the fix is not retroactive. |
| `flashcard-images` | Public read | Flashcard images. Compressed at upload: max 200 KB / 1200px. Migrated from base64 in Feb 2026 (167 images, 110 MB). |

### 1.4 Backend Functions & RLS

#### SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (70+ functions)

> **Audit note (Jun 2026):** the original blueprint listed ~16 RPCs. A code sweep (`grep -o "\.rpc('...')"`) found **70+ distinct RPCs actually called**. The table below is the original curated set; the **"Additional RPCs found in code"** table that follows it captures everything previously undocumented. Together these are the full inventory as of the current code.

| Function | Purpose | Caller |
|----------|---------|--------|
| `get_user_activity_stats()` | Admin dashboard stats (total users, active today/week, new signups) | SuperAdminDashboard, AdminDashboard |
| `get_filtered_authors_for_notes(p_course, p_subject_id, p_role)` | Authors with public notes matching filters | BrowseNotes.jsx |
| `get_filtered_authors_for_flashcards(p_course, p_subject_id, p_role)` | Authors with public decks matching filters | ReviewFlashcards.jsx |
| `get_anonymous_class_stats()` | "You vs Class" comparison widget (min 5 users) | AnonymousStats.jsx |
| `get_user_streak(p_user_id)` | Consecutive study day streak | Multiple callers |
| `get_discoverable_users()` | Same-course users caller can send friend requests to (masked email) | FindFriends.jsx |
| `get_my_friends_with_stats()` | Accepted friends with weekly stats | MyFriends.jsx |
| `get_batch_group_member_stats(p_group_id)` | Professor view of batch member activity (live, relative to `CURRENT_DATE`). **Sprint 8.1 (D-16):** unchanged — reused as-is inside `archive_batch_group`'s snapshot capture. | ProfessorAnalytics.jsx, GroupDetail.jsx |
| `get_study_time_stats(p_user_id, p_local_date)` | Today/week study time — combined + in-app/offline split (Sprint 7.3-C, additive columns) | Dashboard.jsx |
| `follow_user(p_followee_id)` | Idempotent follow + fires notification | AuthorProfile.jsx |
| `unfollow_user(p_followee_id)` | DELETE from follows | AuthorProfile.jsx, Following.jsx |
| `get_follow_status(p_target_id)` | Is caller following target? | AuthorProfile.jsx |
| `get_following_with_stats()` | All followees with weekly stats | Following.jsx |
| `get_friends_leaderboard()` | Caller + mutual friends ranked by weekly reviews | LeaderboardWidget.jsx |
| `get_following_leaderboard()` | Top 20 followees (students) + caller by weekly reviews, `DENSE_RANK` over the full cohort. Directional follow-join: `follows WHERE follower_id = auth.uid()` → `followee_id` (no `friendships`, no reciprocal clause). **Sprint 6.5 `[FIX]` (`02` deployed 07/09/2026; `03_TEST` 9/9): ambiguous `rank` (`42702`) 400'd the "Following" tab — fixed in place (no signature change) via `#variable_conflict use_column` + window alias `rnk`. Task 6.5-D audit (`04_AUDIT_*`, 07/09/2026): live body captured (`pg_get_functiondef`) = byte-identical to `02_FUNCTIONS`; original body unrecoverable from git; reconstruction diffed vs the Sprint 3.5 contract @ `071395d` — follow-join / filter / window all MATCH; live membership set-equality 5/5 PASS → faithful, no `[FIX]`, Finding 2 fully closed. `docs/database/sprint6.5/`.** | LeaderboardWidget.jsx |
| `update_daily_goal(p_review_goal, p_study_goal_minutes)` | Set or clear daily goals on profile | GoalProgressWidget.jsx |
| `get_public_note_preview(p_note_id)` | Public note metadata (no auth required) | NotePreview.jsx |
| `get_group_preview(p_token)` | Group metadata for join page (no auth required). **Sprint 8.0 (D-15):** returns jsonb, additive fields `group_type`/`batch_course`/`batch_institution`/`viewer_status` (caller's own membership status, if any). **Sprint 8.1 (D-16):** additive `is_batch_group`/`archived_at`; `stats` returns `null` (not the live aggregate) when the batch is archived — public previews never expose activity data for an ended batch. | GroupJoin.jsx |
| `join_group_by_token(p_token)` | Join group; redirects unauthenticated to login. **Sprint 8.0 (D-15):** return type changed `uuid`→`jsonb` (`{group_id, status}`). For a `group_type='batch'` group joined by a `role='student'` caller, inserts `status='requested'` (idempotent) instead of activating — a pre-existing `'invited'` row flips straight to `'active'` (staff-initiated invite is a stronger signal than a self-request). Never writes `profiles.course_level`/`institution`. Byte-identical (`status='active'` direct) for non-batch groups and non-student callers. **Sprint 8.1 (D-16):** locks the resolved group row (`FOR UPDATE`) first and raises `'This batch has ended'` if `archived_at` is set, before any status change; the `'requested'` insert's `ON CONFLICT` now reactivates a `'closed'` row (post-restore explicit re-request) instead of only ever doing nothing. | GroupJoin.jsx |
| `approve_batch_join_request(p_membership_id)` / `reject_batch_join_request(p_membership_id)` | Admin approves/rejects one pending batch-join request (`status='requested'` → `'active'`, or row deleted). **Sprint 8.0 (D-15).** `is_admin()` guard; raises if the row isn't currently `'requested'`. **Sprint 8.1 (D-16):** resolves the request's group, locks that `study_groups` row, refuses `'This batch has been archived'` — mostly moot in practice since archiving already closes outstanding requests, but keeps the same lock convention as every other enrollment path. | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `get_admin_pending_batch_requests()` | Admin's pending-batch-request queue (student, batch, requested_at) across all batch groups. **Sprint 8.0 (D-15).** `is_admin()` guard. | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `archive_batch_group(p_group_id)` / `restore_batch_group(p_group_id)` | Sprint 8.1 (D-16). Admin-only, `SECURITY DEFINER`, lock the batch row (`FOR UPDATE`) before acting. Archive: captures a snapshot into `batch_group_archives` (reuses `get_batch_group_member_stats`), sets `archived_at`, closes outstanding `requested`/`invited` rows to `'closed'` — all one transaction, idempotent (repeat call returns existing state, no snapshot replacement). Restore: clears `archived_at`, leaves active memberships and closed rows untouched, idempotent. | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `get_batch_group_archive(p_group_id)` | Sprint 8.1 (D-16). Reads the frozen report/roster snapshot for the batch's *current* archive event (matched by exact `archived_at`, so restore→re-archive always resolves the latest). Gated identically to `get_batch_group_member_stats` (professor/admin/super_admin). | GroupDetail.jsx |
| `submit_content_flag(...)` | Flag content; dedup check; priority escalation | ContentFlagModal |
| `get_my_content_flags()` | Professor queue: pending content_error flags on own content | Dashboard.jsx |
| `get_admin_flags(p_status)` | Admin queue: all flags by status | AdminAnalytics |
| `resolve_content_flag(...)` | Resolve/reject/remove flag | Admin UI |
| Notification RPCs (6) | get/mark/delete notifications | useNotifications.js |
| Group RPCs (14) | create/invite/accept/decline/leave/share groups | MyGroups, GroupDetail |
| `cleanup_old_notifications()` | Delete notifications > 60 days (pg_cron) | cron job |

#### Additional RPCs found in code (previously undocumented)

These were called by the frontend but missing from the blueprint. Grouped by domain; file references are the call sites.

| Function | Purpose | Caller |
|----------|---------|--------|
| **Landing / public (anon)** | | |
| `get_public_educators()` | Public educator list for landing page | Home.jsx |
| `get_platform_stats()` | Public headline stats: `student_count`/`educator_count`/`total_flashcards`/`total_notes`, plus `public_flashcards`/`public_notes` (visibility='public' counts). **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01** (`docs/database/phase5/13`, S4) | Home.jsx, AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `get_public_deck_preview(p_deck_id)` | Public deck metadata + first **5** cards (front_text only) for share page (no auth) | DeckPreview.jsx |
| `get_featured_landing_content()` | Curated featured public decks/notes for landing hero+teaser (anon). 5-card hard cap per deck (front/back/type) via 5-grouping-column join; notes metadata-only. **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/06`)**. Drives `HeroFlipDemo.jsx` + the "Featured Study Sets" rail (S4). | Home.jsx (S4) |
| **Landing / curation — nomination & approval (Phase 5 S3, authenticated)** | | |
| `nominate_featured_content(p_content_type, p_content_id)` | Professor/admin nominates own already-public deck/note for featuring; public-only guard; idempotent. **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | `FeatureNominationButton.jsx` (MyFlashcards.jsx, NoteDetail.jsx) |
| `approve_featured_nomination(p_content_type, p_content_id)` | Admin/super_admin approves a pending nomination → sets `is_featured_on_landing=true`; returns the resulting boolean so the caller can detect a non-public race. **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `reject_featured_nomination(p_content_type, p_content_id)` | Admin/super_admin clears a pending nomination (all 4 audit fields nulled). **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `unfeature_content(p_content_type, p_content_id)` | Admin/super_admin removes live content from the landing; full removal — nulls all four nomination/approval fields (leaves landing + pending queue). **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `get_pending_featured_nominations()` | Admin/super_admin queue of nominated-but-not-yet-live decks+notes (UNION ALL). **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| `get_live_featured_content_admin()` | Admin/super_admin view of currently-live featured decks+notes, for unfeaturing. **✅ Deployed 2026-07-01 (`docs/database/phase5/11`)** | AdminDashboard.jsx |
| **Dashboard / activity** | | |
| `get_study_queue(p_user_id uuid)` | **✅ Sprint 6.0 (deployed 02/09/2026, `docs/database/sprint6/01`).** The single source of truth for the "what's due" review queue — returns due, non-concept, course-matched flashcard rows (review row exists AND `status='active'` AND `next_review_date <= today` AND `skip_until` null/past; *today* in `profiles.timezone`). Read-time course filter (`course_level`/`target_course` match, null-safe both ways), L2 visibility guard, L5 read-IDOR guard, `GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated` only. Replaced client-side due-math in Dashboard / ReviewSession / StudyMode. Reachable from the Study nav menu ("Today's Reviews", added 03/09/2026). **SRS Ladder Epic Phase 1 (`docs/database/srs-ladder/02`, ✅ deployed 03/09/2026):** DROP+CREATE to append `rung smallint` to the return shape (body otherwise byte-identical); `status='mastered'` rows are excluded automatically (filter is already `status='active'`). **Forecast SSOT resolved:** `get_due_forecast` now shares this exact due predicate (no longer a separate off-SSOT surface). | Dashboard.jsx, ReviewSession.jsx, StudyMode.jsx |

Signature unchanged → no frontend change. Closes the 6.0 forecast follow-up. | Progress.jsx |

IDOR guard, CTEs, concept_card exclusion all preserved exactly; `review_events` pre-aggregated into a one-row-per-card CTE before joining, so it can't fan out the pre-existing `recall_success_pct` arithmetic. | Progress.jsx |

Here the `review_events` aggregate is pre-grouped by `question_type` (not by card) since the base query already fans out one row per student-reviewer per card; broadcast-joined back via `MAX()`. | Dashboard.jsx (professor) |

| `apply_review(p_user_id, p_flashcard_id, p_rating, p_is_correct DEFAULT NULL, p_source DEFAULT NULL)` | **Sprint 7.4 (13/09/2026, `docs/database/sprint7.4/02`) — the new write SSOT for review scheduling**, superseding `submit_review` (D-02/D-2A). Absorbs `submit_review`'s body verbatim (confirmed byte-identical against live before extending — same IDOR guard, same rung transition maths, same new-card-vs-existing-card branches) and, in the same transaction as the `reviews` write, INSERTs one `review_events` row (durable per-grade history — see §1.1 `review_events`). `rung_before` is `NULL` for a brand-new card, else the pre-transition rung. `p_is_correct` stays `NULL` until Sprint 7.5's graded question types exist; `p_source` is `'new_card'` \| `'review_session'` \| `NULL` (preview mode). Same signature return shape `(new_rung, next_review_date, new_status, interval_days)`. `GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated` only. | StudyMode.jsx `handleRating` |
| `submit_review(p_user_id uuid, p_flashcard_id uuid, p_rating text)` | **Sprint 7.4: now a thin `LANGUAGE sql` compat shim** — `SELECT * FROM apply_review(p_user_id, p_flashcard_id, p_rating, NULL, NULL)`. Kept so a stale cached client bundle calling the old 3-arg signature during the deploy window keeps working; its events log with `is_correct = NULL`, identical to today's self-graded flow. Grants unchanged (`GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated`). No longer called by current frontend code (`StudyMode.jsx` calls `apply_review` directly). | — (compat only) |
