#### Role-check helper functions

| Function | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `is_admin()` | Pre-existing role-check helper (admin/super_admin) used throughout RLS policies and SECURITY DEFINER guards. |
| `is_professor_or_admin()` | **New, Sprint 7.5 (13/09/2026), SQL drafted not yet deployed.** Mirrors `is_admin()`'s pattern — `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE id = auth.uid() AND role IN ('professor','admin','super_admin'))`. Backs the D-10 (§3.1) RESTRICTIVE RLS policies gating verdict-bearing question-type authorship on `flashcards`. `SECURITY DEFINER`, `GRANT EXECUTE TO authenticated` only. |

#### Key Triggers

| Trigger | Table | Purpose |
|---------|-------|---------|
| `trg_create_profile_on_signup` → `fn_create_profile_on_signup()` | auth.users AFTER INSERT | Creates the profiles row on signup. SECURITY DEFINER, `ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`. Required because email confirmation = no session = client INSERT blocked by RLS. **Sole signup trigger as of Jun 30, 2026** — the former duplicate `on_auth_user_created → handle_new_user()` was dropped (see §1.11 #1, ✅ resolved). |
| `trigger_update_deck_card_count` → `update_deck_card_count()` | flashcards AFTER INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE | Maintains card_count on flashcard_decks (SECURITY INVOKER). Do NOT add a second trigger — causes double-counting. **Sprint 7.5 (13/09/2026), two fixes:** (1) INSERT branch's UPDATE now also widens `flashcard_decks.visibility` (private < friends < public, never narrows) when a new card is more permissive than the deck's stored visibility — a deck's visibility was previously frozen at whatever its first-ever card had, silently hiding later public cards from Browse/Recent Activity. (2) the auto-create-deck branch (fires when no matching deck row exists yet) now also sets `target_course` from the triggering flashcard — it never did before, live impact confirmed as 2 decks / 34 public cards invisible to every student for 5 months (since professors/admins bypass `get_browsable_decks`' course gate, nobody noticed). Both backfilled for existing bad rows — see `bugs.md` for full detail on each. |
| `trg_aaa_counter_*` → `fn_update_*_counter()` | flashcards, notes, reviews, upvotes, friendships | Maintains user_stats counters. Named trg_aaa_ to fire before trg_badge_. |
| `trg_badge_*` → `fn_badge_check_*()` | flashcards, notes, reviews, upvotes, friendships | Awards badges on milestone events. (Note: a parallel `trg_check_badge_*` function set formerly existed unwired — dead code, dropped 02/07/2026 in L1; see §1.11 #4.) |
| `trg_badge_new_profile` → `fn_badge_check_new_profile()` | profiles AFTER INSERT | Initializes user_stats row + awards pioneer badge. |
| ~~`trg_auto_enroll_batch_group` → `fn_auto_enroll_batch_group()`~~ | ~~profiles AFTER INSERT/UPDATE~~ | **✅ RETIRED (Sprint 8.0 follow-up, D-15).** Dropped entirely — both its "add by course match" and "remove by course match" logic carried the same wrong-batch ambiguity once two batches can share a course+institution. Batch membership now only ever changes via `join_group_by_token`, `enroll_user_in_batch_group`, `approve_batch_join_request`/`reject_batch_join_request`, or the pre-existing explicit `remove_group_member`/`leave_group`. |
| `trg_auto_resolve_flashcard_flags` / `trg_auto_resolve_note_flags` → `auto_resolve_content_error_flags()` | flashcards, notes AFTER UPDATE | ⚠️ **Corrected:** auto-**CLEARS** open content_error flags when the author edits the flagged card/note. NOT priority escalation, and NOT on the `content_flags` table (the blueprint previously stated both incorrectly). |
| `trg_notifications_updated_at` → `fn_notifications_set_updated_at()` | notifications BEFORE UPDATE | Maintains updated_at (SECURITY INVOKER). |
| `trg_notify_badge_earned` → `notify_badge_earned()` | user_badges AFTER INSERT | Creates a badge_earned notification. |
| `trigger_update_upvote_counts` → `update_upvote_counts()` | upvotes AFTER INSERT/DELETE | Maintains upvote_count on notes / flashcard_decks (separate from the aaa counter trigger). |

#### Edge Functions (Supabase)

| Function | Trigger | Purpose |
|----------|---------|---------|
| `push-subscribe` | Client call | Saves push subscription to push_subscriptions |
| `push-unsubscribe` | Client call | Soft-deletes subscription |
| `notify-friend-event` | DB trigger | Instant push for friend request/accept |
| `notify-content-created` | pg_cron (4-hour aggregated) | Notify followers of new content |
| `cron-review-reminders` | pg_cron 02:30 UTC daily | Daily review reminder push |
| `cron-daily-study-summary` | pg_cron every 15 min | Nightly 22:00 local-time study summary |

**CRON_SECRET** is shared by all pg_cron-triggered Edge Functions. JWT Verification disabled on all Edge Functions — auth handled via `x-cron-secret` header inside each function.

#### RLS Summary

All tables have RLS enabled. Key patterns:
- **Students:** CRUD on own rows only (`user_id = auth.uid()`)
- **Public content:** `visibility = 'public'` for SELECT
- **Friends content:** `visibility = 'friends'` + friendship check
- **Professor/Admin:** content management + analytics
- **Super Admin:** SELECT all rows on profiles, notes, flashcards, reviews via role-check subquery
- **Unauthenticated pages (NotePreview, DeckPreview, GroupJoin):** MUST use SECURITY DEFINER RPCs — direct table queries return 0 rows for anon users

> **✅ RESOLVED (Phase 5 S4, 2026-07-01).** `Home.jsx` no longer issues any direct anon table reads — the `profiles` role-count reads and the `flashcards`/`notes` public-count reads were deleted. `get_platform_stats()` now returns `public_flashcards`/`public_notes` alongside the existing totals (SQL: `docs/database/phase5/13`, **✅ deployed 2026-07-01**). `Home.jsx` has zero direct `.from()` calls as of this sprint.

**MyFlashcards.jsx** (`/dashboard/flashcards`, page heading **"My Study Sets"** since Sprint 7.6 — route/file name unchanged)
- **Reads:** flashcards (own), flashcard_decks (grouped by batch_id display; also fetched separately with `is_featured_on_landing`/`featured_nominated_at` for the Phase 5 S3 curation control, matched to each batch group via the 5-grouping-column join)
- **Writes:** flashcards (delete), flashcard_decks (visibility toggle cascades), `nominate_featured_content` RPC (professor/admin, public decks only) **✅ deployed 2026-07-01**
- **UI:** Cards grouped by batch_id; inline edit; visibility toggle; suspend/skip per card; group header shows `FeatureNominationButton` for public decks when professor/admin

---

**FlashcardCreate.jsx** (`/dashboard/flashcards/new`, page heading **"Create Study Item"** since Sprint 7.7 — route/file name unchanged, matching Sprint 7.6's "Browse/My Study Sets" rename pattern)
- **Sprint 7.5:** gained a question-type selector (Flashcard / Multiple Choice), rendered only for professor/admin/super_admin via `useRole` from `@/contexts/NavDataContext`. MCQ path adds an options editor + optional "Why" explanation; see §1.1 flashcards MCQ representation note and D-10 (§3.1) for the server-side gate.
- **Sprint 7.7:** selector now shown to ALL users (not just professor/admin/super_admin) — the two new free-recall types (Theory / Test your understanding) are ungated and available to everyone; only the three graded options within the same dropdown (Multiple Choice / True-False / Correct-Incorrect) are still conditionally rendered for `canAuthorGradedTypes`. True/False and Correct/Incorrect reuse the mcq options/correct_answer/back_text columns but replace the free-text options editor with a 2-way toggle (options are auto-populated from `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS`, never typed) — see §1.1 true_false/correct_incorrect representation note.
- **Sprint 7.9:** "Test your understanding" removed from the selector (collapsed into Theory+subtype, D-10 correction). Theory now shows a required "Subtype" `<Select>` (`pure_theory`/`descriptive_case_study` from `THEORY_SUBTYPE_LABELS`) right below the Back textarea — validated in the same submit-time loop as MCQ options/match pairs. Graded types' "Why" textarea now writes to the new `explanation` column instead of `points_to_remember` (mechanical swap, same `toPointsToRemember` helper — see `src/lib/mcq.js` entry above). **Same sprint, extended scope (D-14):** "True / False" also removed from the selector (merged into Correct/Incorrect). `isTwoWayVerdictType()` retired — with only one member left, it collapsed back to a direct `card.questionType === 'correct_incorrect'` check at each of its 6 call sites rather than being kept as a now-pointless 2-member abstraction.
- **Sprint 7.12:** "Concept Card" added to the selector, ungated (open to all users, alongside Flashcard/Theory) — closes the Phase 7 question-type roster. Front label becomes "Concept Name", Back label becomes "Summary", plus a repeatable Key Terms (term + definition pair) list editor below the Back textarea (min 1 pair, `src/lib/conceptCard.js`). No "Why" field — concept cards are never graded, `explanation` stays NULL.
- **Reads:** disciplines, subjects, topics (dropdowns)
- **Writes:** flashcards (INSERT with batch_id, source='manual'; question_type defaults to 'flashcard' — no selector in UI yet)
- **UI:** Front/back text fields, subject/topic dropdowns, image upload. No question_type selector — pending Phase 6 UI work.

**BrowseNotes.jsx** (`/dashboard/notes`) **/ ReviewFlashcards.jsx** (`/dashboard/review-flashcards`, page heading **"Browse Study Sets"** since Sprint 7.6 — route/file name unchanged)
- **Reads:** `get_browsable_notes()` or `get_browsable_decks(p_question_type DEFAULT NULL)` (own + public + friends + group-shared; **Sprint 7.6:** `p_question_type` narrows decks by whether they contain a visible card of that type; **Sprint 7.12 (v6):** additive `has_concept_card` boolean return column, computed in the same visibility-filtered lateral as `visible_card_count` — drives the "Read Concepts" button below), `get_filtered_authors_for_notes/flashcards()` for author dropdown
- **Writes:** upvotes (toggle), content_group_shares (share to group)
- **UI:** Filter bar (course, subject, topic, author, role, **+ question type, Sprint 7.6** — options built from `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` in `src/lib/questionTypes.js`; flashcard/mcq as of 7.6, +true_false/correct_incorrect/theory/test_your_understanding, Sprint 7.7, **+match_the_following, Sprint 7.8, test_your_understanding removed Sprint 7.9 (collapsed into theory+subtype, D-10 correction), true_false removed Sprint 7.9 (merged into correct_incorrect, D-14), +concept_card, Sprint 7.12**), content cards with upvote, share, view buttons. **Sprint 7.12:** a deck tile with `has_concept_card=true` gets a secondary "Read Concepts" button alongside its normal click-to-study area — opens `ConceptCardViewer` (read-only accordion modal, `src/components/flashcards/ConceptCardViewer.jsx`), no grading, no `apply_review` call.

**ProfessorAnalytics.jsx** (`/dashboard/professor-analytics`)

**StudyMode.jsx** (`/dashboard/study` — also used as an embedded component by ReviewSession)
- **Dual role:** Standalone page at `/dashboard/study` (receives cards via searchParams) AND a reusable component imported by ReviewSession (receives cards via props). Same file serves both.
- **Reads:** flashcards (via props or searchParams deck query), reviews (existing SRS state per card)
- **Writes:** `submit_review` RPC (the write SSOT — SELECT-or-INSERT + deterministic rung transition; SRS Ladder Phase 3), `study_sessions` (INSERT on session complete with local session_date). No direct client `reviews` write path remains.
- **UI (Sprint 6.4 reskin):** the card under review on the `<Card>` primitive at `rounded-obj` (r14) / `--rv-*` / Plex, leading `<VerifiedEdge>` once answered; TTS (SpeakButton + SpeechSettings); the grade row is `<GradeButtonRow>` — three equal navy-outline ≥48px targets, mono interval label from the session-cached `get_srs_ladder_config` (zero per-card network), slate miss, **no traffic-light colour**; revealed answer neutralised (navy `ANSWER` chip, Literata body when `back_text` ≥ 320 chars); post-forward animation on grade submit (`prefers-reduced-motion` aware); FlagButton; ContentPreviewWall for preview-mode limit; session-complete + "All Caught Up" on `--rv-*`; `previewMode` param (limits to 10 cards for non-enrolled users).
- **Sprint 7.12 fix:** `fetchFlashcards`'s SRS-aware filter (`dueIds.has(c.id) || !reviewedIds.has(c.id)`, the "due OR never-reviewed" fallback) didn't check `question_type`, so a never-reviewed `concept_card` — which by definition has no `reviews` row — passed the "new card" fallback and could reach a student in a live session (D-06 violation: `get_study_queue` already excluded it from the *due* set, but this separate never-reviewed fallback re-admitted it). Fixed with an explicit `c.question_type !== 'concept_card'` clause in the same filter. Concept cards are now never fetched into a study session at all, at any point in the pipeline.

**ProfileSettings.jsx** (`/dashboard/settings`)
- **Reads:** profiles (own)
- **Writes:** profiles (full_name, institution, daily goals via update_daily_goal RPC)
- **UI:** Name, institution fields; daily review goal and study time goal inputs

**StudentGuide.jsx** (`/guide`)
- **Reads:** None (static content)
- **UI:** Onboarding guide for new students — explains SRS, how to create flashcards, etc.

**Educators.jsx** (`/educators`) — Phase 5 Sprint 5, ✅ deployed 2026-07-01; Sprint 6 addition ✅ deployed 2026-07-02
- **Reads:** None (static value-proposition content)
- **Writes:** `submit_institute_inquiry()` RPC (SECURITY DEFINER, anon-callable); Sprint 6 adds `submit_educator_application()` RPC (same anon-callable pattern) — zero direct `.from()` on either form, per the unauthenticated-page rule
- **UI:** Institute benefits (mirrors the "For Institutes & Educators" copy on `Home.jsx`) + lead form (institute name, contact name, email, WhatsApp, city, course, message). Success state: "We'll reach out within 1–2 business days." Reuses `ContentPreviewWall.jsx`'s form/validation pattern (WhatsApp country-code normalization, trim-and-validate). **Sprint 6:** second, clearly-labeled "Apply to Teach on RevisOp" section below it — full name, WhatsApp, email, institute (optional), credential-or-LinkedIn URL, course(s) taught, why (optional). Same success copy. On submit, an anonymous applicant's browser stores the RPC's returned `ref_token` into `localStorage['revisop_access_ref']` so a same-browser signup later auto-links via the extended `link_access_request`.

**AdminDashboard.jsx** (`/admin`) **/ SuperAdminDashboard.jsx** (`/super-admin`)
- **Reads:** `get_user_activity_stats()`, profiles (all), flashcards (all), notes (all via super_admin RLS), reviews (all), content_flags, admin_audit_log, `get_pending_featured_nominations()` + `get_live_featured_content_admin()` (Phase 5 S3) **✅ deployed 2026-07-01**, `access_requests` (all — `select('*')` now includes `request_type`/`message`, Phase 5 S5) **✅ deployed 2026-07-01**
- **Writes:** profiles (role change, suspend), admin_audit_log (INSERT on every action), `approve_featured_nomination` / `reject_featured_nomination` / `unfeature_content` RPCs (Phase 5 S3) **✅ deployed 2026-07-01**; Sprint 6 adds `approve_educator_application` / `reject_educator_application` RPCs **✅ deployed 2026-07-02**
- **UI:** "Needs Review" content flag card (always visible), "Landing Page Content" section (Pending Nominations + Currently Live tables, both always-visible with zero-state — Phase 5 S3), user management table, stats cards. Access Requests tab (Phase 5 S5): Type badge (Institute/Student) + filter (All/Student Access/Institute Inquiries); institute rows show institute name + city/message in a "Details" column and a "Lead — follow up" label instead of the signup-link/Grant-Access UI (no approval action for institute leads). **Sprint 6 (✅ deployed 2026-07-02):** adds an "Educator Applications" filter + purple "Educator" badge; educator rows show institute/credential-or-why/course in the Details column, a read-only status badge (bypasses the generic pending/contacted/enrolled/dismissed dropdown, which must never touch educator_application rows since it would skip the role-grant RPCs), and Approve/Reject buttons whose outcome renders as "Educator role granted ✓", "Awaiting signup" (+ copyable signup link, reusing the existing `/signup?ref=` mechanism), or "Rejected".

| NavBottomTabs.jsx | **Sprint 7.1**, badge added **7.2-F**, Bulk Upload removed / Log Study Time added **7.3-C/D** — mobile bottom-tab bar (`md:hidden`, fixed, safe-area padded). Data-driven `TABS`: Dashboard · Review (due-count pill) · ＋ (create action-sheet — Upload Note / Create Flashcard / Create Group, divider, Log Study Time — **no Bulk Upload, any role**) · Progress · Menu. Consumes `navProps` only, no own fetch. Returns `null` while `useStudySession().inStudySession` (set by `StudyMode` on mount) |

Pending requests (inline accept/decline) render above content notifications; badge = `unreadCount + pendingCount`; Find People/My Friends/Following/View All Requests kept as a footer |

- `Wordmark` (two-tone amber `Revis` + navy `Op`, token-driven, no gradient), `IntervalChip` (mono, r4), `Card` (r14), `Row` (r4 + optional `VerifiedEdge`), `VerifiedEdge` (3px navy rail), `AnswerOption` (glyph + label, slate miss — never red), `ForwardLedgerMicro` / `ForwardLedgerMacro` (the signature device; macro consumes `get_study_queue`/`get_due_forecast` shapes), `GradeButtonRow` (≥48px navy-outline, mono interval, slate miss), `Label` / `Num` atoms, `index.js` barrel.
- Shared JS: `src/lib/revisop-tokens.js` — `REVISOP_LITERATA_ENABLED` (off), `REVISOP_BUCKETS`, `bucketForDays()`, `ledgerFromForecast()`.

### 1.8 Utility Libraries & Hooks

#### Libraries
| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `src/lib/supabase.js` | Supabase client (singleton). Always import as `import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'`. |
| `src/lib/noteStorage.js` | **New, 16/09/2026** — `extractNoteStoragePath(imageUrl)` (public-URL → bucket path) and `deleteNoteStorageImage(imageUrl)` (best-effort `storage.remove()`, warns not throws). Shared by `MyNotes.jsx` and `AdminDashboard.jsx`'s note-delete handlers so deleting a note also removes its image — `NoteEdit.jsx`'s own inline extractor (image *replace* path) was left as-is, out of scope for this fix. |
| `src/lib/utils.js` | `cn()` utility (clsx + `extendTailwindMerge` — `rv-*` radius/shadow/colour families merge last-wins, Sprint 7.0) |
| `src/lib/navActive.js` | **Sprint 7.1** — pure `(pathname) => boolean` active-route predicates shared by `NavDesktop` + `NavBottomTabs`: `isExact`, `underAny`, `isCreateActive`, `isStudyActive`, `isManageActive`, `isGroupsActive` (behaviour locked to the Sprint 6.0/6.2 output) + `isReviewTabActive` (bottom-bar "Review" tab). No Supabase, no React. |
| `src/lib/revisop-tokens.js` | RevisOp reskin shared JS (Phase 6 S6.1): `REVISOP_LITERATA_ENABLED` gate (off), `REVISOP_BUCKETS` (forward-ledger scale), `bucketForDays()` / `ledgerFromForecast()` for wiring the ledger primitives to `get_study_queue`/`get_due_forecast`. No Supabase. |
| `src/lib/mcq.js` | **Sprint 7.5** — shared MCQ authoring helpers used by both `FlashcardCreate.jsx` and `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`: `compactMcqOptions`, `deriveMcqBackText`, `toPointsToRemember`, `validateMcqOptions`. **Sprint 7.9:** `toPointsToRemember`'s output now feeds the `explanation` column for graded types (not `points_to_remember` — see §1.1 flashcards `explanation` row); the helper's name/shape is unchanged, only what callers assign its result to. |
| `src/lib/fitb.js` | **New, Sprint 7.11** — shared fill-in-the-blank helpers used by `FlashcardCreate.jsx`/`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` (authoring) and `StudyMode.jsx` (grading): `normalizeFitbAnswer` (the one function called identically on both the student's typed answer and every authored acceptable answer — must never diverge between the two call sites), `isFitbMatch`, `deriveFitbBackText`, `validateFitbBlank`/`validateFitbOptions`/`compactFitbOptions`, `splitFitbSentence` (renders the blank as an inline `<input>`). See D-13 (§3.1) and the fitb Representation note (§1.1). |
| `src/lib/questionTypes.js` | **Sprint 7.6** — `formatQuestionType(qt)` (question_type slug → readable label; moved out of a `Dashboard.jsx`-local const so `ReviewFlashcards.jsx`'s new filter uses identical display strings, not invented ones) and `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` (the types with a real authoring path; add to this array, not hardcoded JSX, as later sprints ship more). **Sprint 7.7** extends `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` to 6 types and adds `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` (`['mcq','true_false','correct_incorrect']` — the condition StudyMode.jsx uses to pick the shared AnswerOption-list rendering branch) + `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS` (auto-populated `["True","False"]`/`["Correct","Incorrect"]` option pairs for authoring). **Sprint 7.9:** drops `test_your_understanding`/`integrated_case` from `formatQuestionType`/`BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` (both now-uninsertable) and adds `THEORY_SUBTYPE_LABELS` (`pure_theory`/`descriptive_case_study`) for the newly-activated `subtype` column. **Same sprint, extended scope (D-14):** drops `true_false` from `formatQuestionType`/`BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES`/`GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`/`VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS` too — `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` is now `['mcq','correct_incorrect']`, `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS` now has only the `correct_incorrect` entry. **Sprint 7.11:** adds `fitb` to `BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` only — deliberately NOT added to `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` (its confidence-gated three-way verdict, D-13, isn't the clean binary that array assumes; `fitb` renders through its own StudyMode branch, same reasoning as `match_the_following`). No Supabase. |

#### Contexts
| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `src/contexts/AuthContext.jsx` | Auth state, user profile, role helpers, `updateUserTimezone()` |
| `src/contexts/CourseContext.jsx` | Active course selection for multi-course professors; wraps entire app via `CourseContextProvider` |
| `src/contexts/StudySessionContext.jsx` | **Sprint 7.1** — one boolean `inStudySession`; `StudyMode` sets it on mount / clears on unmount so `NavBottomTabs` can hide during the full-screen card loop (regardless of entry route). `useStudySession()` is safe outside the provider (no-op). |
| `src/contexts/StudyTimerContext.jsx` | **Sprint 7.3-C** — app-wide home for the manual "offline study" timer, mounted in `App.jsx` alongside `StudySessionProvider`. Owns the 3-tier stale-session policy (<4h auto-resume / 4-16h honest-session prompt / >16h silent discard), now classified ONCE on app mount via lazy `useState` initializers (not an effect) instead of being gated behind whichever page happened to host the old `StudyTimerWidget`. Own localStorage key `revisop_manual_timer_started_at` — no longer shared with `StudyMode.jsx`'s `revisop_session_started_at`/`revisop_session_source` pair (the fix for the cross-feature localStorage collision). `window.addEventListener('storage', …)` syncs start/stop across tabs. Exposes `{ isRunning, startedAt, elapsedMs, recoveryPrompt, start(), stop(), stopAndLog(durationSeconds), discard() }`; `useStudyTimer()` throws outside the provider. |
| `src/contexts/NavDataContext.jsx` | **Sprint 7.0**, extended **7.2-F** — `<NavDataProvider>` (mounted once, above the router) owns `useRole()` / `useNotifications(5)` / `useFriendRequestCount()` / `useDueForecast()` as ONE instance each, so nav consumers read a shared context instead of re-fetching. Exposes a drop-in `useRole()` shim + the full `useNavData()` bundle (role, notifications, friend count, `dueToday`/`dueNext7`/`dueNext30`). |

#### Hooks (all in `src/hooks/`)
| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `useRole.js` | Role check helpers (isAdmin, isProfessor, etc.) |
| `useNotifications.js` | Realtime notification subscription + unread count badge |
| `useFriendRequestCount.js` | Realtime pending friend request count |
| `useDueForecast.js` | **Sprint 7.2-F** — wraps `get_due_forecast` (the same lightweight 3-int RPC `Progress.jsx` uses); consumed as a `NavDataContext` singleton by the Review-tab due badge (7.2-F) and the professor's own due count (7.2-A) |
| `useActivityFeed.js` | Recent content feed for dashboard ActivityFeed widget |
| `useBadges.js` | Badge data fetching + unnotified badge check |
| `usePushNotifications.js` | Push notification permission request + subscription management |
| `useSpeech.js` | TTS (text-to-speech) for flashcard audio reading. **Critical study-engine logic:** `splitIntoChunks()` splits card text on sentence boundaries (`/(?<=[.!?\n])\s+/`) and speaks each chunk sequentially. This is a deliberate workaround for the **Chrome/Edge ~15-second `speechSynthesis` cutoff bug** that silently truncates long utterances. Do NOT refactor to a single `speak(fullText)` call — long cards will cut off mid-sentence. Falls back to the whole string as one chunk when no punctuation is found. |
| `use-toast.js` | shadcn toast hook |

**Deleted hooks (no longer in codebase):**
- `useOCR.js` — Tesseract.js OCR hook, deleted as dead code. Package `tesseract.js` may still be in `package.json` — pending removal.

---

### 1.9 Data Flow Diagram

```
User action (browser)
  │
  ├─ Direct table query (.from('table').select()/insert()/update()/delete())
  │    └─ RLS filter applies → user sees only their own + permitted rows
  │
  ├─ RPC call (supabase.rpc('function_name', args))
  │    └─ SECURITY DEFINER → function runs as DB owner → can bypass RLS
  │    └─ Used for: cross-user aggregates, unauthenticated public pages, complex joins
  │
  └─ Realtime subscription (supabase.channel().on('INSERT'...))
       └─ notifications table → bell badge updates in real time

Push notification flow:
  User action (new card/note/friend) → Edge Function → push_subscriptions → Web Push API → device

Auth flow:
  signUp() → email confirmation → auth.users INSERT → handle_new_user() trigger → profiles INSERT
  (no client session during email confirmation → trigger required)

Date handling (CRITICAL):
  All date comparisons use new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA') — user's local timezone
  session_date stored as local date (not UTC) — critical for India users after 6:30 PM UTC
```

---

### 1.10 Key Relations Map

```
auth.users (Supabase managed)
  └─► profiles (1:1 via handle_new_user trigger)
        ├─► notes (user_id)
        ├─► flashcards (user_id, creator_id)
        ├─► reviews (user_id)
        ├─► study_sessions (user_id)
        ├─► friendships (user_id ↔ friend_id — bidirectional)
        ├─► follows (follower_id → followee_id — unilateral)
        ├─► study_groups (created_by)
        ├─► study_group_members (user_id)
        ├─► user_badges (user_id)
        ├─► user_stats (user_id)
        ├─► notifications (user_id)
        └─► admin_audit_log (admin_id, target_user_id)

flashcards
  ├─► reviews (flashcard_id) — SRS state per student
  ├─► flashcard_decks (5-column join, NOT deck_id)
  └─► content_creators (content_creator_id) — revenue attribution

disciplines → subjects → topics (taxonomy hierarchy)
  └─ Referenced by notes and flashcards for content organization

study_groups
  ├─► study_group_members (group_id)
  └─► content_group_shares (group_id) → notes or flashcard_decks

content_group_shares
  └─ Deleting group cascades to shares but NOT to original notes/decks
```

---

### 1.11 DB Cleanup Backlog & Landmines

Objects that **exist in the live database** but are wrong, duplicated, dead, or dangerous. Verified Jun 29, 2026 via `information_schema` / `pg_catalog` introspection. **Read this before writing any pivot migration** — these are the items most likely to cause silent failures.

#### 🔴 Landmines — resolve in the DB before the pivot

| # | Issue | Evidence | Required action |
|---|-------|----------|-----------------|
| 1 | ✅ **RESOLVED (Jun 30, 2026)** — duplicate profile-creation triggers on `auth.users` | Both `on_auth_user_created → handle_new_user()` AND `trg_create_profile_on_signup → fn_create_profile_on_signup()` fired AFTER INSERT. Root cause: the same orphaned-profile bug was fixed twice (Mar 19 + Mar 20, 2026); the second fix added a duplicate without dropping the first. `handle_new_user` fired first (alphabetical) and won; the second was a silent no-op (both have `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`). | **DONE:** dropped trigger `on_auth_user_created` + function `handle_new_user`; kept `fn_create_profile_on_signup` (referenced by app code at AuthContext.jsx). Verified one trigger remains + end-to-end signup creates a correct profile. |
| 2 | ✅ **RESOLVED (03/07/2026)** — `is_public` dropped from `notes` + `flashcards`; RLS now keys solely on `visibility` | **Correction:** the earlier "friends tier has never been enforced in RLS" claim was **WRONG** — it only looked at the public-read policy. `09_DIAGNOSTIC` found the live DB *already* had `"Users can view friends notes"` / `"Users can view friends flashcards"` policies (both `visibility = 'friends' AND EXISTS(accepted friendship)`). Live public-read predicates were `notes: is_public = true`, `flashcards: is_public = true OR visibility = 'public'`; `09` Block 5 confirmed `is_public = true ⟺ visibility = 'public'` with **zero drift** on both tables (swap = data-equivalent). Block 3 (pg_depend) = no view/rule on `is_public`. | Landmine Cleanup Sprint L2, done in stages. **Stage A** (`10_SCHEMA`): `ALTER POLICY` (not DROP/CREATE — preserves name/roles/cmd) swapped only the two public-read predicates onto `visibility = 'public'`; `TO public` preserved (anon reads public content directly). No friends policy created (already existed). Verified via `11_TEST_verify_visibility_rls_matrix.sql` — 24 cells (owner/friend/stranger/anon × public/friends/private × notes/flashcards) all PASS, incl. the critical stranger/anon-vs-friends/private assertions. **Frontend** (commit `ca044d9`, pushed live): 8 files stripped of `is_public` writes/reads (`FlashcardCreate.jsx`, `MyFlashcards.jsx`, `ProfessorTools.jsx`, `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx`, `NoteUpload.jsx`, `NoteEdit.jsx`, `StudyMode.jsx`'s `.or()` filter, `MyNotes.jsx`'s filter + icon). **Hidden-dep audit** (`14_DIAGNOSTIC`): function-body scan caught `skip_topic_cards` + `suspend_topic_cards` still referencing `fc.is_public` (a redundant clause — `fc.visibility='public'` already in the same OR); migrated off in `15_FUNCTIONS` (data-equivalent). Re-ran `14` q1 (only `user_badges.is_public` hits remain — a different column, not dropped) + `09` q2 (zero policies on `is_public`) as the final gate. **Stage B** (`12_SCHEMA`): dropped `is_public` from both tables (indexes auto-dropped); `13_TEST` PASS (schema gone, note + flashcard inserts succeed on `visibility` alone). Same audit-gap lesson as the SRS/`vw_study_items` trap (see below): **a column drop needs a function-body scan too, not just pg_depend + grep.** |
| 3 | ✅ **RESOLVED (Jun 30, 2026)** — overloaded RPCs → PostgREST ambiguity | `delete_notification` and `mark_single_notification_read` each existed as `(uuid)` and `(uuid, uuid)`. Frontend calls only the single-arg `(p_notification_id)` versions. | **DONE:** dropped the unused `(uuid, uuid)` overload of each + `NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'`. Verified one signature each remains. |

**✅ RESOLVED (04/07/2026, L4) — `profiles` blocked user deletion.** `profiles.id → auth.users.id` was `ON DELETE NO ACTION`, so deleting a user from the Supabase Auth dashboard failed ("Database error deleting user"). `19_DIAGNOSTIC` mapped the FK graph: 16 children of `profiles(id)` already CASCADE, 5 already SET NULL, and **8 `NO ACTION` blockers — all attribution columns** (`notes`/`flashcards`.`contributed_by`, `flashcards.creator_id`, `notes`/`flashcard_decks`.`featured_approved_by`/`featured_nominated_by`, `content_flags.resolved_by`), never the owner. `20_SCHEMA` (one transaction): converted those 8 → `SET NULL` (deleting an approver/creator nulls the credit on other users' content, doesn't delete it), then `profiles_id_fkey` → `CASCADE`. `21_TEST` 12/12 PASS incl. a live end-to-end delete: user delete cascaded the profile away, nulled the attribution on another user's card, preserved that card. Deleting a user from the dashboard now works with no manual `profiles`-row step.

|---|--------|---------------|

The live badge triggers use the `fn_badge_check_*` family instead. Drop script: `docs/database/landmines/02_CLEANUP_drop_dead_trigger_functions.sql`. Dropped via `02_CLEANUP` (deploy verified against `01_DIAGNOSTIC` — none were trigger-wired, no other function called them); `06_TEST` block 1 PASS (all 7 dead trigger-functions gone). |

| 6 | ✅ **RESOLVED (02/07/2026)** — `comments` table | RLS-enabled (2 policies referencing `notes.is_public`) but zero frontend `.from('comments')` usage (PrivacyPolicy.jsx mentions "comments" in prose copy only — not a functional reference, unrelated to this drop). Drop script: `docs/database/landmines/03_CLEANUP_drop_comments_table.sql`. This is the L2 unblocker — dropping it removes the one non-notes/flashcards RLS dependency on `notes.is_public`. Dropped via `03_CLEANUP` (empty table, zero inbound FKs / functions / views; CASCADE removed only its own 2 RLS policies); `06_TEST` block 2 PASS. |

Drop script: `docs/database/landmines/04_SCHEMA_drop_legacy_free_text_columns.sql`. Dropped via `04_SCHEMA` (all-zero non-null, no RLS predicate); `06_TEST` block 3 PASS. `upvotes.note_id`'s legacy `upvotes_note_id_user_id_key` unique index auto-dropped with the column (superseded by `upvotes_unique_user_content`). |

#### Type inconsistencies to watch in migrations

- **SRS columns diverge across tables:** `flashcards.ease_factor` (numeric, **vestigial**) vs `reviews.easiness` (double precision, **live**); `flashcards.repetitions` vs `reviews.repetition`; `flashcards.next_review` (timestamptz, vestigial) vs `reviews.next_review_date` (date, live). Always read SRS state from `reviews`. ✅ **RESOLVED (02/07/2026)** — `flashcards.next_review`/`interval`/`ease_factor`/`repetitions` drop is written (`docs/database/landmines/05_SCHEMA_drop_flashcards_srs_columns.sql`) Dropped 02/07/2026 (`05_SCHEMA`) after the frontend-first gate: sole writer was `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s hardcoded seed payload, stripped in commit `921280b` + verified live. ⚠️ **Audit-gap caught here:** `05` first failed with `2BP01` because the dead `vw_study_items` view still `SELECT`ed these columns — a dependency the grep/index/RLS audit missed. Resolved by dropping the dead view (`08_CLEANUP`, see §Advisor) then re-running `05`; `06_TEST` blocks 4–6 PASS. **Lesson: every column/table drop needs a `pg_depend` view/rule dependency check, not just codebase grep + index/RLS.**
- **`friendships` and `content_creators`** use `timestamp WITHOUT time zone` for created_at/updated_at; every other table uses `timestamptz`.

#### 🔵 Supabase Advisor findings (surfaced Jun 30, 2026)

From Supabase Dashboard → Advisors → Security. Distinct from the landmines above (these are platform-flagged), but in the same "who can see what" territory — **relevant before the landing-page pivot exposes content to anonymous visitors.**

| Severity | Finding | Detail | Action |
|----------|---------|--------|--------|
| ✅ **RESOLVED (Jun 30, 2026)** — was 🔴 CRITICAL | `vw_study_items` was a **SECURITY DEFINER view with `SELECT` granted to `anon` + `authenticated`** → **actively exposed**: anyone with the public anon key could call `/rest/v1/vw_study_items` via PostgREST and read all flashcards (incl. private), bypassing RLS. Frontend never queried it. *(Corrects an earlier note here that wrongly said it wasn't leaking.)* | **DONE (mitigated Jun 30):** `REVOKE ALL … FROM anon, authenticated` + `ALTER VIEW … SET (security_invoker = on)`. **DROPPED ENTIRELY (02/07/2026, L1 `08_CLEANUP`):** the view had **zero real consumers** — `get_anonymous_class_stats` queries `flashcards` directly (the "sole consumer" claim was a stale code comment, `-- Uses vw_study_items equivalent`, not an actual query). Dropping it retired the surface for good and unblocked the `05_SCHEMA` SRS-column drop. |
| ✅ **RESOLVED (04/07/2026, L3)** — was 🟡 Sev 3 | Functions flagged **"Function Search Path Mutable"** — no fixed `search_path` means they inherit the caller's at run time (privilege-escalation vector, esp. for SECURITY DEFINER). | **DONE (L3, `16`–`18`):** `16_DIAGNOSTIC` found **50 our-owned unpinned functions** (44 SECURITY DEFINER + 6 not; excludes extension-owned + already-pinned like `get_public_deck_preview` / `skip_topic_cards`), confirmed zero unqualified extension-function deps (Block 3 empty), and that `anon`/`authenticated` cannot `CREATE` in `public` (Block 4 false/false). Chose **behavior-preserving pin, `search_path = public, extensions`** (matches the live role default per Block 1) over strict `''` — no body rewrites, zero runtime change. Applied to all 50 (SECDEF-first). ⚠️ **Two-bug incident during deploy (both fixed same day):** (1) `17_SCHEMA` originally emitted the value **single-quoted** (`SET search_path TO 'public, extensions'`) — for the `search_path` GUC a quoted comma-string is ONE schema named `"public, extensions"`, so `public` dropped out of the path and every unqualified reference in the 50 fns broke (flashcard/note/review **writes** failed via trigger `update_deck_card_count`). `18_TEST`'s execution smoke missed it because it sampled an already-correctly-pinned fn. (2) The first hotfix (`17b`) re-pinned correctly but appended a `BEGIN…ROLLBACK` write-smoke **in the same editor submission** — the editor wraps the whole script in one txn, so the `ROLLBACK` reverted the `ALTER`s too (false-positive PASS). Fixed by `17c` (apply-only, no rollback). Correct syntax is an **UNQUOTED** identifier list. `18_TEST` hardened to assert `public` is a standalone element of each pinned path (catches bug #1). Post-`17c`: all pins correct, writes healed. Rollback = `ALTER FUNCTION … RESET search_path`. **Lessons:** never single-quote a multi-schema `search_path`; never mix persistent DDL with a verification `ROLLBACK` in one Supabase-editor run; a search_path test must exercise a real write, not just an already-pinned sample. |
| ✅ **RESOLVED (04/07/2026, L5)** — was 🟡 WARN ×246 | **"Public/Authenticated Can Execute SECURITY DEFINER Function"** (~123 fns ×2 roles) + **"Public Bucket Allows Listing"** (×2) + **IDOR** on card-scheduling RPCs (found in the L5 write-guard audit). | **DONE (L5, `security/01`–`06`):** IDOR guards on `skip_card`/`suspend_card`/`reset_card`/`skip_topic_cards`/`suspend_topic_cards` (`p_user_id <> auth.uid()` → `RAISE`); `REVOKE EXECUTE` (robust `FROM PUBLIC + GRANT back`) — both roles off 21 internal/trigger fns, anon off ~70 authenticated-only RPCs, **kept** the 10-fn public allowlist + **`is_admin`/`is_super_admin` (RLS depends on them — do not revoke)**; dropped 2 broad bucket list SELECT policies. `06_TEST` 9/9 PASS + live anon landing smoke 200. Classification = frontend `.rpc()` grep + `03_DIAGNOSTIC` RLS-function-ref scan. **Pending:** leaked-password protection is **Pro-plan-gated** — "Prevent use of leaked passwords" (HaveIBeenPwned API) is unavailable on the Free plan; **defer to Pro upgrade** (the remaining `auth_leaked_password_protection` advisor WARN stays until then). Latent `skip_card`/`suspend_card` reviews-column bug ✅ **RESOLVED 04/07/2026** (was `task_95bdea3d`; `docs/database/bugfixes/09`). **Second, unrelated latent bug in the same two functions ✅ RESOLVED 14/09/2026:** the 04/07 fix's `UPDATE reviews ...; IF NOT FOUND THEN INSERT ...` shape (correct column names, but still two separate statements) was a TOCTOU race — two concurrent calls on the same never-reviewed card (StudyMode.jsx's 5 "Skip 24hr" buttons have no in-flight guard) could both see "NOT FOUND" and both attempt the INSERT, the loser hitting `23505 reviews_user_flashcard_unique`. Found live during Sprint 7.12 verification, fixed same day: both functions rewritten as a single atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (user_id, flashcard_id) DO UPDATE` (`docs/database/bugfixes/17_FUNCTIONS_skip_suspend_card_atomic_upsert.sql`), IDOR guard/timezone logic reproduced verbatim. `18_TEST` — 7/7 PASS, including calling each RPC twice in a row on the same never-reviewed card (the deterministic race-simulation) with no error. Note: the ~103 `authenticated_security_definer` + 12 `anon_security_definer` advisor WARNs that remain are **expected** — legit RPCs (authenticated) and the intentional public allowlist + `is_admin`/`is_super_admin` (anon); not vulnerabilities. |

#### Verification provenance

This section and all §1.1 column/type/trigger corrections were reconciled against four read-only introspection queries (`[DIAGNOSTIC] Full Column Dump`, `All Triggers`, `All Functions + Signatures`, `All RLS Policies`) run in the Supabase SQL Editor on 2026-06-29. Re-run them after any schema change to keep this blueprint authoritative. **Still unverified (low-risk):** owner-only RLS policies on `user_badges` / `user_stats` / `user_activity_log`.

### 2.2 Schema Changes Beyond Original Plan

1. **`batch_id` on flashcards** (Dec 2025): Added after discovering that toggling visibility merged unrelated card groups. Each manual card gets a unique batch_id; bulk uploads share one.

2. **`contributed_by` + `creator_id`** (Jan 2026): Originally one attribution field. Split into creator_id (who uploaded) and contributed_by (professor for attribution) because revenue and display attribution need to be independent.

3. **`visibility` replaces `is_public`** (Jan 2026): Original boolean was too limiting — "friends-only" sharing was a strong user request. Three-tier system (private/friends/public) required a text column with CHECK constraint.

4. **`flashcard_decks` as auto-maintained table** (Jan 2026): Originally planned as a simple tag/group. Discovered that joins on 5 columns instead of a FK was required because `deck_id` on flashcards was never populated (legacy issue). Trigger-maintained card_count avoids COUNT(*) on every page load.

5. **`follows` separate from `friendships`** (Sprint 3.4): Originally only bidirectional friendships. Added unilateral follow graph for professor tracking and leaderboard visibility without requiring mutual acceptance.

6. **`study_sessions` with local date** (Sprint 3.1): Originally planned to use UTC timestamps. Discovered that UTC midnight creates "wrong day" issues for India users after 6:30 PM IST. `session_date` stores user's local date as text.

7. **`question_type` + 9 content columns** (Sprint 6): Originally flashcards was pure front/back. Expanded to 10 question types to support MCQ, case studies, concept cards, match-the-following, etc. `concept_card` items excluded from SRS metrics.

8. **`profile_courses`** (Sprint 3): Multi-course support for professors. `profiles.course_level` kept as primary course for backward compatibility; `is_primary = TRUE` synced via `setPrimaryCourse()`.

---

### 2.3 Features Added Beyond Phases

- **Card suspension and skip system** (Feb 2026): Not in original scope. Added after students reported frustration with cards they wanted to defer without deleting.
- **Content flags** (Mar 2026): Not planned. Added after professors reported student-submitted typos/errors in public content with no way to report them.
- **Batch study groups** (Mar 2026): B2B-specific group type that auto-enrolls enrolled students by `batch_course + batch_institution`. Enables founder's offline class to have an automatic study group.
- **Gemini import source** (`source='gemini_import'`): Not planned. Added to support AI-assisted card creation workflow.
- **Leaderboard** (Sprint 3.5): Not planned. Added after students informally compared streaks — structured competitive view increased engagement.
- **Professor multi-course** (`profile_courses` table): Not planned. Added when one professor teaches multiple CA levels.
- **GuideInfoModal**: Contextual intro modals on public share pages (GroupJoin, NotePreview, DeckPreview) — added to reduce drop-off on landing from shared links.

---

### 2.4 Design Decisions

1. **SuperMemo-2 quality 1/3/5 (not 0-5)**: Odd numbers only. 2 and 4 are too subtle a distinction. Three options (Hard/Medium/Easy) match CA exam preparation mental model.

2. **`session_date` as local date string**: Stores user's local date as YYYY-MM-DD text rather than UTC timestamp. Prevents "wrong day" bugs for India users who study late at night.

3. **No incomplete session rows in DB**: Abandoned sessions live in localStorage only. DB stores only completed sessions — prevents garbage in analytics and avoids complex cleanup queries.

4. **`trg_aaa_*` naming convention**: Counter triggers are named to fire alphabetically before `trg_badge_*` — badge triggers read user_stats counters that the aaa triggers maintain.

5. **Two-column wordmark (not logo)**: Brand identity is pure typography — `Revis` in amber (#f59e0b) + `Op` in navy (#1e1b4b). No icon. Scales to any size, renders sharp on all devices, no SVG complexity.

6. **`bg-background` as universal page background**: Login, Signup, ForgotPassword, ResetPassword, NotePreview, DeckPreview, GroupJoin all use the shadcn `bg-background` CSS variable (white). Ensures brand consistency.

7. **SECURITY DEFINER for all public share pages**: Unauthenticated visitors cannot hit RLS-protected tables. All data for public pages flows through SECURITY DEFINER RPCs regardless of how simple the query looks.

8. **Email confirmation ON → trigger for profile creation**: With email confirmation enabled, `signUp()` returns no session. Client-side profile INSERT is blocked by RLS. The `handle_new_user()` trigger fires at DB level regardless of session state.

---

### 2.5 Scope Explicitly Dropped

- **Per-role JWT session timeouts** (12h super_admin / 24h admin / 7d student): Supabase does not support per-role expiry. One global JWT expiry applies to all roles. Design was aspirational, never implemented.
- **Resend (transactional email)**: Was in original stack. Removed when Google Workspace SMTP was configured in Supabase — eliminating one paid dependency.
- **Tesseract.js OCR in production**: Package installed, `useOCR.js` hook deleted as dead code. OCR is not actively used. Package may still be in `package.json` — pending cleanup.
- **`MigrateNoteImages.jsx`**: Temporary admin page for image migration (base64 → Storage). Migration complete Jun 2026 — page pending deletion.
- **`is_public` boolean on notes and flashcards**: ⏳ **SQL prepared (02/07/2026), not yet deployed.** A `visibility` text column was added in Jan 2026, but the RLS migration was never completed until now: `notes` public-read RLS keyed SOLELY on `is_public = true` (ignoring `visibility`), `flashcards` on `is_public = true OR visibility = 'public'`, and the `friends` tier was never enforced in RLS at all. Landmine Cleanup Sprint L2 rewrites both public-read policies onto `visibility` and adds the first `friends`-tier RLS policy, frontend-first before the column drop. See §1.11 landmine #2 for the full staged plan and script names.

---

## Part 3 — Decision Map & Future Work

### 3.1 Decision Log

**D-01: batch_id for grouping flashcards**
- **What:** Each INSERT gets a `batch_id` UUID (single card = unique UUID, bulk = shared UUID). Display groups by batch_id, never by timestamp.
- **Why:** Toggling visibility from private→public was merging cards from different sessions into one visual group because they shared the same timestamp range.
- **Impact:** All deck display code must group by batch_id. Any SQL that groups by created_at for deck display is wrong.

**D-02: Reviews table as SRS single source of truth**
- **What:** SRS state (easiness, interval, repetition, next_review_date) lives exclusively in `reviews`, not on `flashcards`.
- **Why:** Original design wrote SRS state to `flashcards` — but flashcards are professor-owned, causing RLS failures when students tried to update them.
- **Impact:** `StudyMode.jsx` does explicit SELECT → IF EXISTS UPDATE → ELSE INSERT (not UPSERT) for clarity. Column names: `easiness` (not `easiness_factor`), `repetition` (not `repetitions`), `created_at` (not `reviewed_at`).
- **Superseded (SRS Ladder Epic, ✅ deployed 03/09/2026):** scheduling is now driven by `reviews.rung` + `srs_ladder_curves`, computed server-side by `submit_review` (D-2A). `easiness`/`interval`/`repetition` become legacy-cosmetic (still written for continuity, no longer read for scheduling). The client SELECT→UPDATE/INSERT in `handleRating` is replaced by a single `submit_review` RPC call in Phase 3.
- **Write path superseded again (Sprint 7.4, ✅ deployed 13/09/2026, D-11):** `reviews` is still the current-state SSOT, but the write path is now `apply_review` (which `submit_review` thin-wraps for backward compat). Every write to `reviews` is now paired, in the same transaction, with a `review_events` history row — see D-11.

**D-2A: Deterministic expanding SRS ladder (SRS Ladder Epic — Phase 0 approved + Phase 1/2 deployed & verified 03/09/2026)**
- **What:** replace the flat unconditional per-rating interval (`StudyMode.jsx` `handleRating`: Easy +7 / Medium +3 / Hard +1, computed client-side) with a deterministic expanding ladder. **8 rungs (0–7)**, intervals **1 / 3 / 7 / 14 / 30 / 60 / 120 / 240** days (`srs_ladder_curves`, `_default` curve; parameterisable per `question_type`). **Easy** → +1 rung (cap 7); **Medium** → hold; **Hard** → drop to rung 0 + `next_review_date = today + 1` (relearn step). New card's first grade → rung 0 / 1 / 2 for Hard / Medium / Easy. **MASTERED** = an Easy grade while already at rung 7 → `reviews.status = 'mastered'` (new value; leaves the daily pipeline — `get_study_queue` filter is already `status='active'` — stays visible in a Mastered list). Any non-mastering grade on a mastered card un-masters it. Transition rules live in one `srs_ladder_rules` jsonb row read by `submit_review`, `srs_preview` AND `get_srs_ladder_config` so client-preview and server-enforcement cannot drift.
- **Why:** the flat interval had no notion of a card getting easier over time — every Easy was 7 days forever. An expanding ladder is standard SRS. Server-side (`submit_review`) so the UI never hard-codes intervals (guardrail). Deterministic (rung → interval is a lookup, no EF-scaled maths, no randomness) so it is provable by SQL and reproducible.
- **Migration:** `reviews.rung` backfilled once from `repetition`, capped by `easiness`: `LEAST(repetition, 2)` if last grade was Hard (`easiness <= 2.35`), else `LEAST(repetition, 4)`. **`next_review_date` is NOT recomputed** — the ladder engages on each card's next real review. No card is mastered at migration. Nobody enters rungs 5–7 (they are earned post-migration). Reversible via `UPDATE reviews SET rung = NULL`.
- **Impact:** `submit_review` is the write SSOT for all review scheduling (D-02 impact list is superseded). `get_study_queue` gains a `rung` column. `StudyMode.jsx` (Phase 3) calls `submit_review` for both the review-session grade path and the new-card first-grade path; the `+7`/`+3`/`+1` constants and the client SELECT/UPDATE/INSERT are removed. `ReviewSession.jsx` already only reads (delegates to `StudyMode`) — no constants there. A minimal Mastered list is added so mastered cards never silently vanish. Grade-button reskin + prominent interval preview UI = **Sprint 6.4** (this epic delivers the engine + `srs_preview`, not the reskin).
- **Write SSOT renamed to `apply_review` (Sprint 7.4, see D-11)** — the transition maths above is completely unchanged; only the function name and the addition of a `review_events` history row changed. `StudyMode.jsx` now calls `apply_review` directly; `submit_review` still exists as a compat wrapper.

**D-11: review_events append-only history + apply_review + two-measure analytics semantics (Sprint 7.4, decided/deployed 13/09/2026)**
- **What:** a new `review_events` table logs one durable row per grade, ever (see §1.1) — `reviews` keeps being the current-state SSOT, unchanged. The write path is renamed `submit_review` → `apply_review`, which absorbs the old body verbatim and adds a `review_events` INSERT in the same transaction (atomic by construction — proven by a forced-failure test, `docs/database/sprint7.4/04_TEST`), plus two new optional params: `p_is_correct` (verdict, NULL until Sprint 7.5) and `p_source` (`'new_card'`/`'review_session'`/NULL). `submit_review` becomes a thin compat wrapper so a stale cached client bundle keeps working during the deploy window. The two question-type analytics RPCs (`get_question_type_performance`, `get_educator_accuracy_by_qtype`) gain a second measure: `accuracy_pct` is renamed `recall_success_pct` (self-rated Medium/Easy, computation unchanged) and a new `answer_accuracy_pct` (NULL until `graded_count>0`) is added — surfaced in Progress.jsx and Dashboard.jsx as two labeled rows, with the "No graded answers yet" state built now even though it can't be exercised with real data until Sprint 7.5's graded question types exist.
- **Why:** this is the architectural de-risk gate for the whole question-type epic (Sprint 7.5 MCQ-single onward) — every future graded question type needs a durable per-review verdict history to compute real "answer accuracy," which `reviews` (current-state-only, UPDATE-in-place) cannot provide. Building the history table and the two-measure UI split now, before any graded content exists, means Sprint 7.5 lands on an already-proven write path instead of inventing one under feature pressure. Two separate measures (not one blended number) because "self-rated recall" and "objectively graded correctness" are genuinely different things once graded question types exist, and collapsing them would mislabel one as the other.
- **Storage:** measured (not guessed) at deploy — ~142 bytes/row (`pg_column_size`). Current DB 53MB against the Free-plan 500MB limit; even a 10x-of-observed daily rate projects to low-single-digit MB/year. No archival/rollup/partitioning built — the projection doesn't show a near-term problem worth solving.
- **Impact:** `StudyMode.jsx handleRating` calls `apply_review` (not `submit_review`). No question-type rendering change — StudyMode is still pure front/back; `p_is_correct` stays NULL until Sprint 7.5. Any future RPC needing per-review history (not just current state) reads `review_events`, not `reviews`.

**D-03: SECURITY DEFINER triggers for profile creation**
- **What:** Profile creation on signup uses a DB trigger (`handle_new_user`), not a client INSERT.
- **Why:** Email confirmation ON means signUp() returns no session. auth.uid() is null. Any client-side profiles INSERT is silently blocked by RLS.
- **Impact:** Never add client-side profile INSERT code. Always use the trigger path.

**D-04: 5-column join for deck membership**
- **What:** To find flashcards in a deck, join on: user_id, subject_id, topic_id, custom_subject, custom_topic. Do NOT use `WHERE fc.deck_id = fd.id`.
- **Why:** `deck_id` on the `flashcards` table was never populated — it always returns 0 rows. The column exists as a legacy artifact.
- **Impact:** Every SQL query that fetches cards for a deck must use the 5-column join pattern.

**D-05: Local date for session_date and streak**
- **What:** `session_date` stored as user's local date string. Streak calculated in user's local midnight.
- **Why:** India is UTC+5:30. After 6:30 PM UTC, `CURRENT_DATE` at the server is already "tomorrow." A student who studies at 10 PM IST would have their session credited to the next day if UTC was used.
- **Impact:** Frontend always passes `new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA')`. Never use `toISOString()` for date comparisons.

**D-06: concept_card exclusion from SRS metrics**
- **What:** Cards with `question_type = 'concept_card'` are excluded from all review metrics (Items Reviewed, Items Mastered, accuracy, streak) — and, as of Sprint 7.12, from ever entering a study session at all. No grade, no rung, no `reviews` row, ever.
- **Why:** Concept cards are reference material (cheat sheets, summaries) — not meant to be "reviewed" with hard/medium/easy ratings.
- **Impact:** Every RPC that counts reviews or calculates accuracy must include `WHERE question_type != 'concept_card'` inline. (The `vw_study_items` "safety view" that formerly wrapped this filter was dropped 02/07/2026 as a dead, unconsumed anon-leak liability — filter inline, don't reintroduce the view.)
- **Sprint 7.12 (14/09/2026, ✅ SHIPPED):** concept_card finally got a real structured authoring path (heading/summary/keyTerms) and a browse-only "Read Concepts" viewer — see §1.1 flashcards concept_card representation note. Closed a real gap this decision had left open since it was first written: `get_study_queue` enforced the *due-set* exclusion, but `StudyMode.jsx`'s separate "never-reviewed → treat as new" client-side fallback did not check `question_type`, so a never-reviewed concept card (no `reviews` row by definition) could still reach a student and be graded through the plain flashcard flip UI. Confirmed via live pre-flight that this had never actually happened to real content (0 `reviews`/`review_events` rows for `concept_card` existed before the fix) — a live gap, not a live incident. Fixed in the same fallback filter; see the StudyMode.jsx component note above.

**D-07: trigger_update_deck_card_count is the ONLY counter writer**
- **What:** `card_count` on `flashcard_decks` is maintained exclusively by the `trigger_update_deck_card_count` trigger.
- **Why:** A Feb 2026 bug was caused by the frontend also incrementing `card_count` manually after INSERT, resulting in 2x counts. Adding a second trigger (wrong fix attempt) also caused 2x.
- **Impact:** Before adding any trigger on `flashcards`, run: `SELECT trigger_name FROM information_schema.triggers WHERE trigger_schema='public' AND event_object_table='flashcards'`. Use broad scan (not just event_object_table filter alone).

**D-08: Rebrand localStorage keys**
- **What:** 6 localStorage keys renamed from `recall_*` prefix to `revisop_*`.
- **Why:** Brand migration Recall → RevisOp.
- **Old keys:** `recall_access_ref`, `recall_session_started_at`, `recall_session_source`, `recall-push-banner-dismissed`, `recall-tts-voice-uri`, `recall-tts-rate`
- **Impact:** Fallback reads currently exist in code. One sprint after full rollout, remove fallback reads and old key writes.

**D-09: Complete localStorage inventory (naming is NOT consistent)**
- **What:** Full inventory of every active localStorage key in the code. The rebrand (D-08) only renamed the 6 `recall_*` keys — **3 other active keys were never part of that scheme and use different naming conventions.**
- **Why documented:** A pivot/migration must account for ALL keys, not just the rebranded ones. The inconsistent naming is a real wart.

| Key | Naming style | Owner file | Purpose | Migrated in rebrand? |
|-----|--------------|------------|---------|----------------------|
| `revisop_access_ref` | snake `revisop_` | Dashboard.jsx, Signup.jsx | B2B access-request referral link | ✅ from `recall_access_ref` |
| `revisop_session_started_at` | snake `revisop_` | StudyMode.jsx | In-progress study session start time | ✅ from `recall_session_started_at` |
| `revisop_session_source` | snake `revisop_` | StudyMode.jsx | In-progress session source (`study_mode`/`manual`) | ✅ from `recall_session_source` |
| `revisop-tts-voice-uri` | kebab `revisop-` | useSpeech.js | Saved TTS voice preference | ✅ from `recall-tts-voice-uri` |
| `revisop-tts-rate` | kebab `revisop-` | useSpeech.js | Saved TTS speech rate | ✅ from `recall-tts-rate` |
| `revisop-push-banner-dismissed` | kebab `revisop-` | PushPermissionBanner.jsx | Push-permission banner dismissed flag | ✅ from `recall-push-banner-dismissed` |
| `postAuthRedirect` | camelCase, **no brand prefix** | App.jsx, Login.jsx, StudentGuide.jsx, NotePreview.jsx, DeckPreview.jsx, GroupJoin.jsx | Deep-link target preserved across login/email-confirmation | ❌ never branded |
| `myNotes_viewMode` | camelCase `myNotes_` | MyNotes.jsx | Grid/list view preference on My Notes | ❌ never branded |
| `flashcard_create_draft` | snake, **no brand prefix** | FlashcardCreate.jsx | Auto-save/recovery draft for the card-create form | ❌ never branded |

- **Inconsistency:** Three competing conventions coexist — `revisop_` (snake), `revisop-` (kebab), and unbranded camelCase/snake (`postAuthRedirect`, `myNotes_viewMode`, `flashcard_create_draft`). The last three were never folded into the rebrand and have no `recall_*`/`revisop_*` lineage.
- **Impact:** Any future namespace cleanup or key-migration must enumerate all 9 keys, not the 6 from D-08. The 3 unbranded keys are easy to miss in a `grep recall_`/`grep revisop_` sweep.

**D-10: Professor-only creation for verdict-bearing question types (Phase 7, decided 13/09/2026)**
- **What:** question types that produce a deterministic correctness verdict — `mcq`, `true_false`, `correct_incorrect`, `case_study_mcq`, `integrated_case`, `match_the_following`, `fitb` — are creatable only by `professor`/`admin`/`super_admin` roles. **(Superseded 14/09/2026 — see D-12: `integrated_case` removed, merged into `case_study_mcq`; and D-14: `true_false` removed, merged into `correct_incorrect`. The live gate list is `mcq`, `correct_incorrect`, `case_study_mcq`, `match_the_following`, `fitb`. Superseded again 17/09/2026 — see D-20: `mcq_multi` added, ✅ SQL deployed & verified live — the gate list is now `mcq`, `mcq_multi`, `correct_incorrect`, `case_study_mcq`, `match_the_following`, `fitb`.)** Free-recall types (`flashcard`, `theory`, `concept_card` — **`test_your_understanding` briefly existed as of Sprint 7.7, removed Sprint 7.9, see its own correction note below**) stay open to all users, unchanged from today. (Corrected 13/09/2026: the value is `fitb` in the live `chk_flashcards_question_type` CHECK constraint, not `fill_in_the_blanks` as originally drafted here — see §1.1 flashcards `question_type` row. `test_your_understanding` was also originally listed as a free-recall type in this line but wasn't in the live CHECK constraint at all as of Sprint 7.5 — **Sprint 7.7 added it** via `docs/database/sprint7.7/01_SCHEMA_add_test_your_understanding_type.sql`, so it can now actually be inserted, still ungated per this decision.)
- **Sprint 7.7 update:** built the authoring UI (FlashcardCreate.jsx + BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx) for 2 of the 7 already-gated types (`true_false`, `correct_incorrect`) and for the 2 newly-real free-recall types (`theory`, `test_your_understanding`). Zero RLS changes needed — both `true_false` and `correct_incorrect` were already in the RESTRICTIVE policy's IN-list since Sprint 7.5, this sprint only gave them a real UI. Regression-confirmed live in `docs/database/sprint7.7/02_TEST_verify_new_question_types.sql`.
- **Sprint 7.9 correction (14/09/2026):** `test_your_understanding` was never a real distinct type — it duplicated what `theory` + a real `subtype` value already does better (the same problem the CA Revision Portal's own D14 solved by using `theory` with a required `subtype` of `pure_theory`/`descriptive_case_study`). Dropped from the live `chk_flashcards_question_type` CHECK constraint; the 2 pre-existing rows (both Claude-authored QA/testing artifacts from Sprint 7.7's live verification, confirmed by the operator, not real content) were deleted rather than migrated. The free-recall list in the "What" line above is now just `flashcard`, `theory`, `concept_card` — `test_your_understanding` no longer exists as a value. `theory` authoring now requires the newly-activated `subtype` column (`pure_theory` \| `descriptive_case_study`) — see §1.1 flashcards `subtype` row. Same sprint also split grading-rationale text off `points_to_remember` into a new `explanation` column for graded types (`mcq`/`true_false`/`correct_incorrect`/`match_the_following`) — see §1.1 flashcards `explanation` row. `docs/database/sprint7.9/01_SCHEMA_sprint7.9_hygiene.sql`.
- **Why:** with 100+ new students onboarding against ~3 educators/admins, a review queue for student-authored graded content doesn't scale at that ratio. More importantly, under the Phase 7 hybrid grading model (a wrong verdict auto-submits 'hard' and resets the card to rung 0), a bad `correct_answer` doesn't just mislabel one review — it actively corrects a genuinely-competent student backward. An earlier hybrid proposal (open creation to all, gate only `visibility='public'` behind an `is_verified` review, reusing the Phase 5 landing-page nomination pattern) was considered and rejected: that pattern was built and proven at landing-page-featuring scale, not at "150+ students each producing MCQ decks" scale, and building a general-purpose "submit for verification" workflow at that scale is real, uncosted Phase 7 work, not a reuse of existing machinery. Restricting authorship is also the asymmetrically-safer default — easy to open later once real usage data exists, hard to retroactively restrict content already public.
- **Impact:** `FlashcardCreate.jsx`'s question_type selector is role-gated for these types. Enforced server-side via a RESTRICTIVE RLS policy pair (`flashcards_gate_verdict_types_insert`/`_update`) + `is_professor_or_admin()`, mirroring the existing `is_admin()` pattern — a client-side-only hide would have been insufficient and would have regressed this project's own L5 IDOR-hardening standard. Built as a role check, not a structural schema assumption, so it is cheap to lift if opened in a later phase. The bulk-upload template inherits the same gate for these `question_type` values. Does not affect Sprint 7.4 (no content-authoring surface in that sprint).
- **Status: ✅ SHIPPED — SQL deployed & verified live, frontend live-verified end to end (13/09/2026).** `01_SCHEMA_d10_role_gate.sql` deployed to production; `02_TEST_verify_d10_role_gate.sql` run against real profiles — all 5 rows PASS, including the two regression assertions added after the `fitb` naming catch (student `mcq`/`fitb`/`true_false` inserts all rejected with the RLS policy violation, student plain-`flashcard` insert unaffected, professor `mcq` insert succeeds). Both `FlashcardCreate.jsx` and `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` write via a direct `.from('flashcards').insert()` (confirmed by reading both files — neither goes through a SECURITY DEFINER RPC), so the RLS policy alone is the correct and sufficient enforcement point for this sprint's two write paths. **UI-level confirmation, not just SQL:** a real student's bulk-upload attempt containing an `mcq` row was rejected live with the friendly `42501` error message; a real professor's identical attempt succeeded. See `now.md` for the full live-verification log (authoring, bulk upload, both StudyMode grading paths, both Progress.jsx and Dashboard.jsx answer-accuracy widgets confirmed showing real data).
- **Sprint 7.10 update (14/09/2026):** built the authoring UI + StudyMode rendering for `case_study_mcq`, the 4th of the 5 live-gated types to get a real authoring path (after `mcq`, `correct_incorrect`, `match_the_following` — only `fitb` remains D-10-gated with no authoring UI, its own sprint per D-13). Zero RLS changes needed — `case_study_mcq` was already in the RESTRICTIVE policy's IN-list since Sprint 7.5. **✅ Re-confirmed live 14/09/2026** via `docs/database/sprint7.10/00_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight.sql`, run by the operator (this session has only the anon key): live `chk_flashcards_question_type` is the 8-value enum docs already claimed, `case_study_mcq` included; both RESTRICTIVE policies' `with_check` list `case_study_mcq` in their IN-lists (not narrowed by D-12's `integrated_case` removal, which touched a different value in the same list); `scenario` confirmed genuinely inert (0 non-null rows) before this sprint activated it.

**D-12: `integrated_case` removed — merged into `case_study_mcq` (Phase 7, decided 14/09/2026)**
- **What:** `integrated_case` is not a distinct `question_type`. All case-study content — including a case that draws on multiple chapters within one subject (e.g. an Auditing case referencing material from chs 3 and 5) — uses `case_study_mcq`. `integrated_case` must never be added to the live `chk_flashcards_question_type` CHECK constraint.
- **Why:** grounded in a direct read of the CA Revision Portal's own schema (a separate, real sibling project — `docs/SCHEMA.md` in that repo), which documents `integrated_case` as scenario+MCQ, identical fields to `case_study_mcq`, and states outright in its own renderer notes: "Functionally identical to `case_study_mcq`." A census of that project's 15 real authored chapter files found `case_study_mcq` used 8 times and `integrated_case` used zero times — even the project that designed the type never used it. The one real distinguishing signal raised in discussion — ICAI CA-Final's "Integrated Business Solutions" paper genuinely spans multiple *subjects* (accounts, GST, tax), not just chapters — is real, but it's a tagging/coverage gap, not a rendering/grading difference: `flashcards.subject_id`/`topic_id` are single-value FKs regardless of `question_type`, so no type name — however chosen — lets one question declare it touches 3 subjects. Naming a type after that need without solving the tagging problem would only add a redundant label, not a real capability.
- **Impact:** `case_study_mcq` is the only case-study type going forward (see D-10, superseded line above). If/when genuinely cross-subject case content is authored, the real feature to build then is optional multi-subject tag metadata on a case (e.g. a `related_subjects` array), orthogonal to `question_type` — not scoped now, no real content needs it yet.
- **Authoring — single topic, unchanged flow (clarified 14/09/2026):** a `case_study_mcq` that draws on multiple chapters within one subject (e.g. an Auditing case requiring both Audit Planning and Audit Evidence concepts) is filed under **one anchor `topic_id`** — the chapter the exercise actually belongs to — via the same Course→Subject→Topic authoring flow as every other type. No schema change. This is a filing decision, not a coverage record: "spans multiple chapters" almost always means the scenario assumes prior-chapter knowledge (a normal, desirable property of a good case study), not that the question needs to be discoverable under more than one chapter. Real-world evidence: all 8 real `case_study_mcq` instances in CA Revision Portal's 15 authored chapter files already live under a single `topicId` each — multi-topic filing has never actually been needed in practice. If a professor wants to flag a cross-chapter prerequisite, it belongs in the `scenario` text itself, not structured metadata. True multi-topic tagging is deferred to the cross-subject case above (junction-table-level change, touches the D-01/D-04 5-column deck-grouping join — not undertaken speculatively).
- **Status: ✅ SHIPPED — SQL deployed & verified live (Sprint 7.9, 14/09/2026).** Pre-flight confirmed zero pre-existing `integrated_case` rows (no authoring UI or StudyMode rendering was ever built for it, so it was never actually reachable despite being a live CHECK-constraint value). `docs/database/sprint7.9/01_SCHEMA_sprint7.9_hygiene.sql` dropped it from `chk_flashcards_question_type` and from both D-10 RESTRICTIVE policies' IN-lists in one migration; `02_TEST_verify_sprint7.9.sql` confirmed it now hits a CHECK violation on insert (all 17 assertions PASS, including a D-10 regression check that the surviving gate list — `mcq`/`true_false`/`correct_incorrect`/`case_study_mcq`/`match_the_following`/`fitb` — still correctly rejects a student and accepts a professor after the `ALTER POLICY`).

**D-13: `fitb` verdict is confidence-gated, not binary (Phase 7, decided 14/09/2026)**
- **What:** unlike `mcq`/`true_false`/`correct_incorrect`/`match_the_following` (mechanical, unambiguous verdicts), a `fitb` answer is free-typed text. The author provides an array of acceptable answers (not a single string), normalized (trim/lowercase/strip punctuation/collapse whitespace) for comparison against the student's input. **Match found** → confident-correct path, identical to MCQ's correct path (reveal + `GradeButtonRow` self-grade, `is_correct = true`). **No match** → never auto-marked wrong; falls back to the same UX as free-recall types (flashcard/theory) — reveal canonical answer + accepted variants, full self-grade, `is_correct = NULL` (ungraded, not "wrong").
- **Why:** under the hybrid grading model, a wrong verdict auto-submits 'hard' and resets the card to rung 0 — actively harmful, not neutral, if wrongly applied. For free text, "didn't match the author's list" is never proof of wrongness, only proof the author didn't anticipate that phrasing (synonym, extra words, case/punctuation variance). This is the same asymmetrically-safer-default reasoning as D-10's authorship gate: undercount confident-correct detections rather than ever wrongly penalize a correct student.
- **Impact:** `fitb` stays in D-10's professor-only creation gate — writing a good acceptable-answers list is exactly the judgment call that gate exists for. `is_correct` for `fitb` is `true` or `NULL`, never `false` — this is a genuinely different grading path from the other verdict-bearing types, not a copy of MCQ's binary logic. Authoring UI needs an acceptable-answers array field, not a single string — likely reusing the existing `options` jsonb column (documented today as "answer options for MCQ-type questions") with a generalized meaning, rather than a new column; final call at sprint-build time. `answer_accuracy_pct` will undercount `fitb` specifically (unmatched-but-correct answers are invisible to it, by design) — acceptable, not a blocking concern.
- **Status: ✅ SHIPPED — Sprint 7.11 (14/09/2026), zero SQL needed** (fitb was already live in `chk_flashcards_question_type` and both D-10 RESTRICTIVE policies' IN-lists since Sprint 7.5). Built exactly as scoped above: `options` = acceptable-answer array (final call confirmed — reused, not a new column), `correct_answer` NULL, one shared `normalizeFitbAnswer()` (`src/lib/fitb.js`) called identically on both the student's typed answer and every authored variant. Live-verified both branches: a matching typed answer → `is_correct=true` (confirmed via `get_question_type_performance`'s `graded_count`/`answer_accuracy_pct`); a non-matching typed answer → full self-grade with the card's rung advancing normally off the picked rating (confirmed via the `reviews` table, not forced to rung 0) and no `is_correct=false` ever written for this type (confirmed by the graded-vs-ungraded event math after both reviews). See Sprint 7.11 in Sprint History above and `docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md`'s fitb Representation section for full detail.

**D-14: `true_false` removed — merged into `correct_incorrect` (Phase 7, decided 14/09/2026)**
- **What:** `true_false` is not a distinct `question_type`. All binary-verdict statement content — "true or false" style statements included — uses `correct_incorrect`. `true_false` must never be re-added to the live `chk_flashcards_question_type` CHECK constraint.
- **Why:** grounded in a direct read of the CA Revision Portal's own schema (`docs/SCHEMA.md` in that repo), which documents `correct_incorrect`'s renderer behaviour as "**Identical to `true_false`, with Correct/Incorrect buttons instead**" — no other distinction (question fields, explanation field, confidence-rating flow) is ever described for either type. A real-usage census of that project's live, HTML-loaded chapter files found `correct_incorrect` used **6 times** (5 Auditing & Ethics chapters + 1 Business Laws chapter) and `true_false` used **0 times** — its only occurrence in the entire codebase sits in `data/chapter-data.js`, an orphaned legacy file no HTML page actually loads (confirmed: no `<script>` reference anywhere). `docs/DECISIONS.md` in that project has no entry ever weighing one against the other — they were built as parallel, mechanically-identical twins in the same pass, exactly the same shape as D-12's `case_study_mcq`/`integrated_case` finding (down to "even the project that designed the type never used it in real content"). On RevisOp's own side, both types currently have zero real content — the 2+2 rows either held (Sprint 7.9 pre-flight, §3.1 D-10 correction note) were confirmed QA/testing artifacts and already deleted, so there is no real-content asymmetry to weigh here, only the sibling project's evidence.
- **Impact:** `correct_incorrect` is the only two-way-verdict statement type going forward. `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS['true_false']` and its `'True / False'` label/selector entry are removed from `src/lib/questionTypes.js`; `isTwoWayVerdictType()` in `FlashcardCreate.jsx`/`BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` collapses to a straight `qt === 'correct_incorrect'` check (naming/shape of that helper is a call for sprint-build time — it may not be worth keeping as a "two-way" abstraction once only one member remains). `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES` drops to `['mcq', 'correct_incorrect']`. Same CHECK-constraint/RLS-policy pattern as Sprint 7.9's `integrated_case` removal — dropping the value from `chk_flashcards_question_type` is the actual enforcement point; the D-10 RESTRICTIVE policy's IN-list should drop `true_false` too, even though the CHECK constraint alone already makes it uninsertable (same cosmetic cleanup as D-12).
- **Status: ✅ SHIPPED — SQL deployed & verified live, frontend live-verified end to end (14/09/2026, part of Sprint 7.9's extended scope).** Pre-flight (`03_DIAGNOSTIC_preflight_true_false_removal.sql`) confirmed 0 rows for both `true_false` and `correct_incorrect`, no `srs_ladder_curves` rows for either (only `_default` exists), and no RPC body hardcoding the string — a pure narrowing migration, no DELETE/UPDATE needed. `04_SCHEMA_true_false_removal.sql` deployed; `05_TEST_verify_true_false_removal.sql` — **13/13 PASS**, including a D-10 regression re-check (student `correct_incorrect` insert still rejected, professor's still succeeds, since the `ALTER POLICY` rewrote the whole `WITH CHECK` expression). Frontend: `isTwoWayVerdictType()` retired in both `FlashcardCreate.jsx` and `BulkUploadFlashcards.jsx` (only one member left, so the abstraction was collapsed back to a direct `question_type === 'correct_incorrect'` check rather than kept for a hypothetical future two-way type) — `VERDICT_OPTION_LABELS['true_false']`, `GRADED_QUESTION_TYPES`/`BROWSABLE_QUESTION_TYPES` membership, the "True / False" selector option, and all doc-comment/CSV-template mentions removed. Live-verified: Question Type selector shows exactly 5 options (no True/False), Browse Study Sets' filter shows no True/False, a fresh `correct_incorrect` card authored and studied end-to-end (reveal, WHY block, grading) with console clean.

**D-15: Batch-group membership requires explicit approval — never guessed from course+institution (Phase 8, Sprint 8.0, decided/deployed 15/09/2026)**
- **What:** A batch (B2B) study-group membership is only ever created or changed through an explicit action about one specific student and one specific batch: (a) a student clicks that batch's own invite link → lands in a new `status='requested'` row, invisible everywhere until an admin approves it; (b) an admin picks one exact student and one exact batch group in the UI → activates immediately (that pick *is* the approval); (c) an admin explicitly approves/rejects a pending request; (d) an admin explicitly removes a member (pre-existing `remove_group_member`/`leave_group`, unchanged).
